#### APP-62

**The Stack page is the stack's own** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Stack page shall hold the stack itself, one part at a time - Health (resources, services, scheduled jobs, checks, the last backup), Costs (what was paid, the subscriptions, the agents today and day by day), Tools, Connections (Gmail, outside agents, packs and trackers) and Settings - with the guide one tap from its title row; what belongs elsewhere shall be there and stay within two taps of the menu - the House beside the AI phone on Home's Overview, each memory store's figures and the weekly wiki tidy on Memory, a memory to approve in Needs you - and an old address of a part that moved shall open where it is now.

#### APP-63

**One place to follow a run** · should · functional · **implemented**

A run shall be followed on its task's page - Where it runs (its workflow, run, state and step), its steps each with its own log, the flow from what it read to the answer under Sources, and each agent's lane under Timeline - and on Workflows (n8n's runs); no other screen shall list runs of its own, except the agents' history on the Stack page's Costs, which opens each run's log; an old address of the task map, Telemetry or a run list shall open where the run is now.

The menu shall be Home, Tasks, Workflows (on the desk), Memory, Code, Chat and Stack; the phone's bar shall hold the same in the same order without Workflows, which a phone reaches from Tasks; Home shall have its own address apart from the page the app opens on, the choice of that page shall offer the menu's names and read a choice made under an old name, Home's entry shall count what needs the owner, and what the phone shared shall have its list, one tap from Home's Overview while there is any.

#### APP-65

**The connection state says what is true** · should · functional · **implemented**

The app shall say "Not connected" only when the device gets no answer from the app itself; when the app answers but the runner's state is old it shall say "The stack is starting" (with the runner's expected time to come up from the last boot record) while the machine started after the runner's last state and within the boot check's wait, "The runner did not start" with the machine's time since then after that wait, and "The runner stopped" with how long ago when the runner wrote during this boot and then stopped; the app shall tell the device it is there every 15 seconds also while the runner writes nothing.

A tool of the stack whose web UI can live under a sub-path shall be served under the app's own address behind the app's Tailscale-login gate, framed only by the app's own pages, its own login kept, and shown in a page of the app (a title row and the tool filling the window), not in a tab of its own - n8n at /n8n/ on Workflows and Phoenix at /phoenix/ on Traces, which a step's "Open in Phoenix" opens at that call's trace; a tool that cannot (its pages ask for /assets at the root) shall keep opening in its own tab, behind its own gate.

The app shall follow one written design (docs/UX.md) - what the owner does daily, where each part and tool lives, the colour tokens defined for both themes, one type scale every font size is on, one spacing rhythm, one component for each job (title row, section, card, list, button, pill, tag, segmented control, banner, KPI tile, empty state, explanation, a tool's page and its frame) and the rules every screen follows.

Every schedule being a workflow's (WFL-09), a crew job's Settings and a mission's form shall show its schedule read-only in plain words, with when it runs next and a link that opens its workflow inside the app; a job on a kept timer shall say it runs without n8n; the Stack page shall list the timers that run without n8n as such and point to Workflows for every other schedule; a run started by a workflow shall say so; and no form shall offer a schedule or a time trigger that nothing would apply.

The chat shall let the owner pick the model and the reasoning effort per message (remembered per device) and attach images, sent as images, and documents (PDF, Word, Excel, CSV, text), sent as extracted text.

The scout, mission and builder profiles shall carry only the toolsets they use (no skills, no clarify on unattended profiles), to keep the fixed cost of every call low.

## AUT Autonomy

### AUT-01

**Each kind of action holds a licence** · must · functional · **proposed**

Every kind of action that can wait for the owner shall belong to an action class with a licence at one of three levels (ask, do and tell with an undo window, do quietly), decided in one place that every door calls, with the class's exam, bars, highest level and the evidence for its current level on the class's page.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: Earned autonomy ... Design this out.`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -
- Rationale: The Memory Keeper showed the pattern (DEC-40); the owner's taps queue up in nine places.

### AUT-02

**A licence is earned by exam, shadow and record, and lost at once** · must · functional · **partial**

A class shall rise one level only when its answer-key exam passes on its last three runs, its shadow decisions agreed with at least 90 % of the owner's last 20 decisions in that class and never chose to act where the owner refused, its acts of 14 days had no undo or failed check, and the owner allows the step once; it shall drop one level at once, saying why, on a failed exam, such a disagreement, an undo or a failed act, and go back under exam when the agent, prompt or code it runs on changes.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_shadow_records_once_and_then_the_owner_s_outcome`
- Trace: `ops/decisions.py`
- Gap: The shadow records, from 2 Nov 2026, what the stack would decide for each yes-or-no decision and the owner's outcome where its source keeps it, and shows the agreement per class on #/decide. No class is promoted or demoted by it yet: that needs the exams (TRL-16) and two weeks of answers.
- Findings: [FND-112](#fnd-112)

### AUT-03

**Some actions are never licensed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Money above the ceiling the owner set, a new payee, writing to a person in the owner's name, deleting data that cannot be restored, security settings, credentials, accounts, public exposure and anything on the AI phone outside its listed routines shall stay at ask whatever a class's scores; no code path shall raise them.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_floor_stays_at_ask_whatever_its_configured_level`
- Trace: `config/autonomy.yaml`, `ops/decisions.py`

### AUT-04

**Every unasked act is logged with its evidence and can be undone where possible** · must · functional · **proposed**

Each act a licence allowed shall be recorded with the class, the level and the evidence that allowed it, shown in the run record and the day's digest, and offer an undo for its window where the act can be undone.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### AUT-05

**One decision queue** · must · functional · **partial**

Everything that waits for the owner and that the owner can answer (a task's start or questions, a stopped change, a deploy, a purchase, phone steps, a memory to confirm or forget, an unsettled memory dispute, an agent change, a shipped idea's confirmation, a mission's access, a setup step, a skill, an idea, a licence promotion) shall be one ranked list in the app, each with what, why, the stack's recommendation, what happens without an answer and when, and a one-tap answer or a link to where the owner acts; routine items of a class may be answered together; Home and the queue shall draw that one list, Home showing a kind of more than three items, or of two or more routine items, as one row with its count; a state the owner cannot answer shall stay on the health board.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: One daily decision queue on your phone, by voice too`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_every_source_s_waits_are_one_ranked_list_with_what_why_and_a_recommendation`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_server_adds_the_waits_only_it_sees_and_the_app_draws_the_list`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_what_the_owner_must_answer_reaches_the_queue_and_a_state_does_not`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_every_item_in_the_queue_has_a_working_way_to_act`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_a_link_to_an_agent_s_question_keeps_its_context`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_it_works_waits_in_the_queue_until_the_owner_said_so`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_house_step_in_the_queue_is_the_one_the_house_page_names`, `tests/test_home_work.py::test_home_and_the_queue_draw_one_list_and_a_kind_with_many_items_is_a_count_row`, `tests/test_home_work.py::test_what_the_owner_cannot_answer_stays_on_the_health_board_and_under_stack_upkeep`, `tests/test_changes.py::test_a_stopped_change_waits_on_the_board_until_it_is_answered_and_can_be_left_stopped`
- Trace: `ops/decisions.py`, `ops/checker.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/changes.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `config/autonomy.yaml`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-tasks.js`
- Gap: #/decide and Home draw one list (GEN-02, 7 Nov 2026): builds, purchases, AI phone cleanup steps and approval requests, Gmail's Trash batch, a mission's access, memories to confirm and the Memory Keeper's suggestions, tasks to start or answer, stopped changes, what agents ask, open ideas and disputes, agent changes, the Checker's It works and House setup, ranked, with the stack's recommendation. Skills and licence promotions have no source yet; a Keeper suggestion has Forget and a link, and no Keep: the Keeper proposes it again on its next pass.

### AUT-06

**Decisions reach the owner once a day, and by voice** · should · functional · **proposed**

The queue shall notify once a day at the owner's time (sooner only for a decision expiring within two hours), and Hermes shall read it out and take yes, no or later by voice for every decision except money, which is confirmed by tap where the amount is shown.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### AUT-07

**The queue is measured** · should · quality · **implemented**

The app shall show decisions a day, the median time to answer and how many expired, with the target of at most five a day answered within twelve hours.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_queue_s_numbers_a_day_the_median_and_the_expired`
- Trace: `ops/decisions.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-tasks.js`

### AUT-08

**Low-risk code may deploy itself, and reverts itself** · should · functional · **proposed**

A build that changes under 300 lines, touches no security, compose, secrets, NAS, network or agent configuration path, lowers no requirement's status and passes the checks and both reviews may deploy at the coding-low-risk class's level, and shall be reverted automatically when the health check after the deploy fails.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

## BLD Builder

### BLD-01

**Assignments get their own branch** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a build is assigned, the runner shall validate it and work on a branch named builder/<id>-<slug> in the repository's workbench clone.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_assign_validates_and_names_the_branch`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`

### BLD-02

**Approve needs review, checks and CI** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The runner shall deploy an approved build only when it is in review, its sandbox checks passed and its CI is green; approving while CI still runs shall deploy it the moment CI passes, and not if CI fails.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-07](#cst-07)
- Sources: [DEC-03](#dec-03)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_the_checks_pass_only_on_exit_0_and_their_last_line`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_the_checks_keep_their_exit_code_past_the_tail`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_requires_review_checks_and_green_ci`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_while_ci_runs_deploys_when_it_passes`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_all_takes_only_what_is_ready`
- Findings: [FND-07](#fnd-07)

### BLD-03

**Approve merges, deploys or rolls back** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner approves a build, the runner shall merge it with --no-ff into the default branch, push, deploy, and roll back on a failed deploy.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_approved_build_merges_deploys_or_rolls_back`

### BLD-04

**Change requests carry feedback** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall send a build back only with written feedback, and the builder shall continue on the same branch.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_revise_needs_feedback`

### BLD-05

**Builds check against today's main** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before the checks, the runner shall merge today's main into the build branch and set aside uncommitted leftovers, so that the review shows only the build's own change.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-28: an old branch showed 45 files`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_an_old_build_branch_gets_todays_main`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_uncommitted_builder_leftovers_are_set_aside`

### BLD-06

**A looping builder is stopped** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the builder makes 6 tool calls in a row with empty arguments, then the runner shall stop the build and say why.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-28: empty tool arguments loop`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_empty_tool_call_streak`

### BLD-07

**Sensitive files are flagged** · must · functional · **implemented**

The review shall flag changes to sensitive files (security settings, deploy and build code, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, SOUL text).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `ops/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_review_flags_sensitive_files`

### BLD-08

**Two engines** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each build shall run on one of two engines, Hermes on the ChatGPT subscription or Claude Code on the Claude subscription, Claude Code by default when it is connected; an unknown engine shall be refused, and a build may move to the other engine except while that would lose unfinished work.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_engine_is_validated`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_switch_engine_allowed_and_refused`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_claude_stream_becomes_steps_and_result`
- Findings: [FND-06](#fnd-06)

### BLD-09

**A usage limit pauses a build** · must · functional · **implemented**

When an engine reaches its usage limit, the build shall wait until the reported reset and resume with its unfinished work.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_claude_usage_limit_gives_the_reset_time`

### BLD-10

**The other engine reviews** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before a build reaches the owner, the other engine shall review its assignment, diff and checks and give a verdict (fine to deploy, improve first, do not deploy) with findings; if that reviewer is at its limit, the builder's own engine shall review, marked less independent.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-04](#dec-04)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_the_other_engine_reviews`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_review_verdicts_are_normalised`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_rate_limited_reviewer_hands_over_to_the_other_engine`

### BLD-11

**Code tools for both engines** · should · functional · **implemented**

Both engines shall get Serena's language-server tools and the code graph, read-only.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/builder/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_both_engines_get_serena`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_both_engines_get_the_memory_tool_without_the_owner_s_facts`

### BLD-12

**Builds survive a runner restart** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the runner restarts during a build, the build shall resume; an interrupted deploy shall be marked failed, not repeated.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/DOGFOOD.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_builds_cut_off_by_a_runner_restart_are_resumed`

### BLD-13

**The Forgejo token goes only to the runner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

When the owner pastes a Forgejo token, the runner shall check it against Forgejo and store it encrypted, and the token shall never reach Hermes or any model.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_forgejo_connect_checks_then_stores_encrypted`, `tests/test_server.py::test_forgejo_token_goes_to_the_runner_only`

### BLD-14

**Honest token counts** · should · quality · **implemented**

Builds on both engines shall count all input tokens including cached ones, and show the cached share.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tokens.py::test_claude_builds_count_cache_reads_like_chatgpt_builds`

### BLD-15

**Repositories and their graphs** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Code tab shall list every repository with its graph, code map and digest, reduce the graph to the 200 most-connected nodes for drawing, prefer the code map's digest for the overview, and show a node's source (inside the repository only) with what it calls and what uses it.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repos.py::test_repo_info_from_graph_and_code_map`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_graph_is_reduced_for_drawing`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_overview_prefers_the_code_map_digest`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_node_details_have_source_links_and_code_map`, `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_the_interactive_code_map_is_inside_the_repo_page`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_a_code_graph_the_update_cannot_read_is_built_anew_and_a_failure_is_logged_once_per_commit`
- Trace: `ops/repos.py`, `ops/graphify-sync.sh`
- Findings: [FND-50](#fnd-50)

### BLD-23

**Every repository's code map, shown clean** · should · functional · **implemented**

The repo page shall show the interactive code map of every repository that has one, switching the viewer to it when it shows another, without the viewer's minimap; and a code map shall raise none of the viewer's warnings of the stack's own making (the code graph's cache counted as changed files, the viewer's corrections to the graph).

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repos.py::test_every_repo_with_a_code_map_shows_it_clean_and_without_the_minimap`

### BLD-16

**Removing a repository** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Removing a repository shall delete only the stack's copy (clone, graph, code map, workbench), never the Forgejo repository, and ai-stack itself shall not be removable.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repos.py::test_remove_takes_the_repo_out_but_never_ai_stack`

### BLD-17

**Code maps explain every file** · should · functional · **implemented**

Where Understand Anything left placeholder explanations, the enrichment shall explain each file, function and layer from the code itself, in symbol excerpts for big files, and resume where it stopped after a usage limit.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_calls_run_side_by_side_when_the_deployment_says_so_and_json_mode_is_its_choice`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_two_json_objects_in_one_answer_are_both_read_and_an_empty_file_needs_no_model`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_a_resumed_pass_keeps_counting_its_cost`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_fallback_graph_gets_real_explanations_and_useful_layers`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_a_usage_limit_stops_and_a_second_run_resumes`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_other_fallback_wordings_count_as_placeholders_and_real_text_does_not`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_a_big_file_is_explained_in_parts_that_each_show_their_symbols`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_layer_answers_keyed_by_name_are_used`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_big_files_are_cut_to_symbol_excerpts`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_understand_runs_the_enrichment_and_ignores_graphify_output`
- Trace: `ops/ua_enrich.py`

### BLD-18

**Studied code is never run** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Analysing a third-party repository shall only read it, in a sandbox without internet access or keys. Running its tests shall take the owner's tap Let the builder change it, and then happens in the sealed sandbox only (CHG-12).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/onboard.py`, `ops/claude_tasks.py`

### BLD-19

**Graphs stay current** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a repository's HEAD changes, its code graph shall be rebuilt within 2 minutes, without model calls.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/graphify/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/graphify-sync.sh`, `n8n/workflows/crew-code-graph.json`

### BLD-20

**Supervised sessions land like builds** · should · process · **implemented**

When an owner-supervised coding session (Claude Code outside the builder) lands a change on main, ops/land.sh shall run the checks on the exact commit first and stop on any failure, take the land and deploy locks, restart the runner when the deploy asks for it, and record the deploy with its source and the owner's approval.

- Parents: [CST-07](#cst-07), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/DOGFOOD.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_land_compose.py::test_a_landing_validates_its_compose_files_on_the_host_before_main_moves`, `tests/test_deploy_main.py::test_a_whole_deploy_says_ok_and_runs_the_smoke_test`, `tests/test_deploy_main.py::test_a_failed_smoke_test_health_check_or_build_is_a_failed_deploy`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_an_image_is_built_after_the_image_it_is_built_from`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_a_deploy_is_recorded_with_its_source`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_the_landing_script_checks_before_it_merges`
- Trace: `ops/land.sh`, `ops/deploy.py`
- Rationale: Most changes so far were landed by such sessions (flock ~/.aistack-land.lock, ops/deploy.py) without the builder's review, CI first or a record in Mission Control (FND-04).
- Findings: [FND-04](#fnd-04)

### BLD-21

**A clash only in generated files is not a conflict** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a merge into or from a build branch clashes only in files drawn from other files (requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md), the runner shall draw them again from the merged sources and complete the merge; any other clash shall still stop it.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-29: build #9 and four session commits both changed requirements; only REQUIREMENTS.md clashed`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_clash_only_in_the_generated_requirements_is_drawn_again`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Rationale: Builds and sessions run side by side, and most of them update requirements, so the rendered file clashes nearly every time two land on the same day.

### BLD-22

**Builds run side by side in their own clones** · should · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall run up to BUILD_SLOTS builds at once (2 on the 16 GB PC), each in its own clone of the repo (/srv/ai/workbench/<repo>.<slot>) and only while it holds the lease on its files, with at most one ChatGPT build at a time, and a build cut off by a usage limit shall resume in the clone it started in.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_builds_run_together_only_when_their_files_do_not_overlap`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Rationale: A ChatGPT build's tokens are counted by its key's time window, so two at once would count each other's calls.

### BLD-24

**A repository without a check script runs its tests** · must · functional · **implemented**

The builder's checks on a repository without its own check script shall run its tests as pytest finds them (a tests folder, or test_*.py files up to three folders deep), else its Makefile's test target or its npm test, and say plainly when it has none.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `trial semver, 1 Nov 2026: build #13 checks said "no automatic checks in this repo" though it had 114 tests`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_repo_without_a_check_script_runs_its_tests`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Findings: [FND-94](#fnd-94)

### BLD-25

**The builder is measured on the owner's own projects** · should · quality · **proposed**

The builder's page shall show the changes merged a week into the owner's own repositories and how many were reverted, and one real change a week on such a repository shall be part of the probes.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14), [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: The builder on your own projects`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### BLD-26

**Onboarding always ends in the whole package** · must · functional · **implemented**

Onboarding a repository, from its page, from Add a repository, through Hermes or with make understand, shall run one job that ends in the whole package whatever the repository's size (a copy of the code with its whole history, its code graph, its facts, its code map, every file explained, its digest in the search index, a written analysis on its page, its wiki). It shall wait by itself for usage limits, the PC's memory and the gateway without using up a try, and go on at its first unfinished step after a crash, a deploy or a restart of the PC. The repo page and repos_overview shall show the step it is at. A step that failed three times shall stop the job with the reason on the page, in repos_overview and in a message, and Try again shall go on from that step. The owner shall get one message when the job ends, either way.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_onboard.py::test_the_search_step_does_not_wait_for_embeddings`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_big_repository_gets_the_whole_package_and_one_message`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_no_size_limit_is_left_anywhere_in_the_job`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_step_that_keeps_failing_stops_with_its_reason_and_says_so`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_try_again_goes_on_from_the_step_that_failed`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_usage_limit_waits_uses_no_try_and_goes_on_by_itself`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_too_little_memory_waits_and_a_stopped_enrichment_is_no_failure`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_crash_resumes_at_the_first_unfinished_step`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_the_watchdog_restarts_a_dead_job_and_gives_up_with_a_reason`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_tap_while_it_runs_starts_nothing_more`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_complete_package_is_made_anew_on_request_and_a_failed_redo_keeps_the_old_map`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_different_repository_of_the_same_name_is_refused_with_the_reason`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_what_can_be_onboarded`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_half_done_clone_never_looks_like_a_repository`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_files_that_stay_unexplained_are_said_not_hidden`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_the_analysis_is_asked_once_survives_a_restart_and_is_saved`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_the_study_mode_can_not_run_anything`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_the_app_shows_the_steps_and_promises_nothing_false`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_hermes_reads_why_it_stopped_in_repos_overview`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_removing_a_repository_takes_its_job_and_records`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_long_wait_tells_the_owner_why`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_repository_without_code_still_gets_the_whole_package`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_new_address_is_copied_by_the_job_the_runner_starts_by_name`

### BLD-27

**A repository's facts, measured and sealed** · should · functional · **implemented**

Onboarding shall, after the code graph and without a model, write the facts of a repository to /srv/ai/code-docs/<name>.facts.json from its whole git history (hotspots, coupling, ownership, code age from CodeLore; health sections from repowise), and new clones shall hold the whole history and a shallow one shall fetch it, with hooks off. Each tool shall run pinned and sealed (no network, no secrets, no capabilities, a read-only root) on a throwaway copy that shares no file with the real clone. If one tool fails the other's results shall stay and the failure shall reach the file's errors and the onboarding's status. Coupling on lockfiles, changelogs, generated files and files no longer present shall be left out and counted. The facts shall be rebuilt when a repository's HEAD moved, feed the wiki's first version, and be served by /api/repo/<name>/facts.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `docs/REPO-KNOWLEDGE.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`, `images/repointel/AGENTS.md`

- Refined by: [BLD-37](#bld-37)

### BLD-28

**Only the head the owner reviewed deploys** · must · constraint · **implemented**

When a build goes back to work (changes requested, checks re-run, engine switched, sent back by the merge queue), the build runner shall withdraw an approval given while CI ran; and the build runner shall merge a build only when its branch on Forgejo is at exactly the commit that was put up for review, else it shall put the build back to review with the reason and push a message.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_an_approval_given_while_ci_runs_ends_when_the_build_works_again`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_only_the_reviewed_head_is_merged`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_every_build_action_the_app_sends_reaches_the_builds_handler`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`, `ops/missions.py`
- Rationale: Found on 7 Nov 2026 (review RUN-R2, SEC-R1) - an approval given while CI ran stayed on the build through a revision, so the CI poll would deploy a head nobody had seen; and the merge took whatever the branch held.

### BLD-29

**The code map runs as fixed steps, never as an agent's choice** · must · functional · **implemented**

The code map step shall run the plugin's deterministic scripts (scan, batches, structure) and a program that makes the nodes and edges, in a container without network and without a model, and shall fail its try when the map has no nodes or, for a repository with code, no edges; the explain step shall recognise every placeholder form the map or a plugin fallback writes.

- Parents: [BLD-26](#bld-26)
- Sources: [DEC-48](#dec-48)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_onboard.py::test_a_hollow_map_is_a_failed_try_and_the_map_runs_without_a_model`, `tests/test_onboard.py::test_every_placeholder_form_is_known`
- Trace: `ops/ua_map_in.py`, `ops/onboard.py`, `ops/ua_enrich.py`

### BLD-30

**Every onboarding step has a quality gate and a record** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each onboarding step shall record when it last ran, how long it took, which model it used with its calls and tokens where a model ran, and its result in numbers with a verdict; a step whose result is hollow shall be weak, shown as weak on the repository's page and in repos_overview, and shall never count as done; the page shall show the checklist of all steps always, and each step shall be runnable again on its own.

A repository shall be the owner's own work or something to evaluate, asked at Add a repository and otherwise taken from its origin; the owner's own work shall get an engineer's guide (entry points and flows with file:line, module map, key types, how to extend it, build and test, fragile areas, glossary) written from the repository's own structure, and something to evaluate the review; an analysis under 2,000 characters, with fewer than four sections, or whose file:line claims mostly do not resolve shall be weak.

**The graph view shows the repository's own code, the source pane says what it shows** · should · functional · **implemented**

The repository's graph view shall rank nodes defined in the repository's own non-test files by the links that hold code together (calls, inherits, implements, contains, imports), every big group showing its best-connected few; the source pane shall say which lines of how many it shows and offer the whole file; the code map viewer link shall work off the tailnet.

### BLD-33

**Every onboarding step says its commit and how far behind it is, and the package can be brought up to date** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each onboarding step shall show when it ran, how long it took, which model it used, its calls and tokens, and the commit it was made at with how many commits the repository has moved on since; a run from before these records shall be read from what it left (the map's commit and date, the explanations' count, the analysis' task). Update shall redo the steps that follow the code (copy, graph, facts, code map, explanations, search) from the latest commit, the code map explaining only the files that changed; Run again on a step shall really do that step and the ones after it, never skip it because an earlier result exists. The repository's kind shall not be a tag.

### BLD-34

**Every coding run gets the repository card first, and the owner keeps a hand on it** · must · functional · **implemented**

Onboarding's last step shall make the repository card without a model from what the steps before learned (the commands a change learned or the repository's files show, the wiki's current conventions and fragile areas, the guide's parts and where to start, the code map's layers), each line with its source and the files it cites, at most 6,000 characters; every coding and review run shall get it first as a must-have of its opening, the brief's commands not twice; an exam's older code shall get no line that cites only files it lacks; the owner shall pin, strike and add lines on the repo page's Card tab, kept when the card is made again; Update shall make it again.

**Everything else onboarding makes stays and is reachable, never pushed** · must · functional · **implemented**

A build's sandbox shall hold everything onboarding made for its repository as files to grep (the card, the written guide, the wiki's pages, one line per file from the code map, the facts), named once on the card; the file note at a file's first read shall carry the code map's line on it; a search that printed more than 50 lines shall hear how its hits fall over the top folders with what the code map says each is, once per command; the repo page shall show the note an agent gets for a file and mark the wiki's pages whose cited code changed. Onboarding shall write into the repository's wiki, without a model, a page per part of the code (what it is, how it stands, every file with its explanation) and a page per section of the written guide, each citing its files and commit, marked as its own and made again every time; the Scribe's pages stay as they are, and neither the brief nor a file note repeats these pages.

## CHG Change requests

### CHG-01

**A change request through one front door** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner gives a change request for an onboarded repository (in the chat, on the repository's page, or as a task of kind coding with its repository), the stack shall create one task of kind change with that repository and run it through the Conductor, and every such task shall end in a branch, a pull request on Forgejo, a request page with its evidence and one message, or in a stop with its reason on the page.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14), [SN-19](#sn-19)

### CHG-02

**The change specification** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before any step changes code, the intake shall write a change specification with a title, a goal, numbered acceptance criteria in EARS form that a test can be written for, the scope (files and modules in, and what is out), a size class (S, M or L), the risks, at most three open questions and a budget, and it shall ask the owner a question only when the answer blocks the plan, writing its assumption into the specification otherwise.

- Trace: `ops/changes.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

**The owner's Go on medium and large changes** · must · functional · **implemented**

A request of size class M or L shall wait for the owner's Go on its specification, which he may edit first, and a request of size class S shall start at once under autonomy auto with its specification shown on its page.

### CHG-04

**A plan by size class** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Orchestrator shall start from a plan template by size class (S coder, judge, reviewers, presenter, scribe; M adds the tester before the coder, red before green, fix rounds, the breaker and the prober; L adds the analyst, the architect's task split with file leases and up to three coders in parallel), and may change it at a checkpoint within the task's budget.

**The judge is a program with a baseline** · must · functional · **implemented**

The judge shall run the repository's suite and checks on main before the change (the baseline) and on the branch after it, report the difference, rerun once a test that failed once and record a test that then passes as flaky on the repository wiki, and never read the coder's own account of its tests.

### CHG-06

**Red before green** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each acceptance test the tester writes shall be run by the judge on main before the change, and a test that passes there shall not count as an acceptance test of its criterion; the request page shall show each criterion with its test's result before and after the change.

### CHG-07

**The conventions lens** · should · functional · **implemented**

A third reviewer on the other engine shall judge the change against the repository wiki's Conventions page and the repository's own rules (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, CONTRIBUTING) and say whether docs and requirements were kept current where the repository keeps them, and its block shall stop the merge unless the owner overrides it with a reason.

### CHG-08

**One wiki per repository, one writer** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each onboarded repository shall have a wiki (vault/repos/<name>/wiki) with the pages Overview, Architecture, Conventions, How to build and test, Fragile areas, Decisions, Lessons, Glossary and Open questions, owned by the Scribe as its one writer, seeded by the onboarding job from the code map, the digest and the analysis, indexed by the search, and read in the brief of every code role and of no scout, the role that reads the web.

**Every fact on a repository wiki cites its evidence and its commit** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A fact on a repository wiki shall name its evidence (a commit, a build, a request, a review comment or a run) and the commit it held at, a fact without evidence shall not be written, the Librarian's lint shall mark a page to re-verify when a file it cites changed since that commit, and a superseded fact shall stay under Superseded (MEM-12).

### CHG-11

**The owner's review becomes a lesson** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner requests changes on or rejects a change, the Scribe shall write his text as a lesson candidate on the repository wiki with the request as its evidence, the owner may mark a lesson as a rule, which moves it to Conventions as his decision, and a lesson that concerns a role rather than a repository shall go through the Coach as an agent change that is replayed before it applies.

### CHG-12

**Running a repository's tests is the owner's consent** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Onboarding a repository shall run nothing of it (BLD-18), and the owner's tap "Let the builder change it" shall be the consent to run that repository's build and tests in the sealed sandbox of its toolchain, without secrets, with the package proxy as its only network and under memory and time limits.

**The prober tries the change for real** · should · functional · **partial**

Where a repository can be run in its sandbox, the prober shall start it on the branch, exercise the changed behaviour through its interface or its pages (Playwright for web pages) and keep a log and a screenshot as evidence on the request page, and a change the prober could not exercise shall say so there.

### CHG-15

**Protected paths when the stack changes itself** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A change request on ai-stack shall not change ops/check.sh, the tests of the change pipeline, eval/, the private answer keys, secrets or the deploy, unless the owner's specification names the path, and a diff that touches one shall fail the judge with the path named.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)

### CHG-16

**The report says what was done, with evidence** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Presenter shall write the request's report with each acceptance criterion and its state (met with its test, or not met with why), what the change did not do, the judge's figures, the reviewers' verdicts, the prober's evidence and the cost, and the pull request's description and the message to the owner shall be made from it.

- Trace: `ops/changes.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`

### CHG-17

**The replay exam** · must · functional · **partial**

The stack shall have trials of type replay, each made from a real commit of an onboarded repository and its parent (the task written from the commit's text, the agents starting from a snapshot of the parent with no history, the tests that fail at the parent and pass at the commit as the hidden suite, those that pass at both for regressions), up to ten per repository with their keys outside every agent's reach, with the arms one-coder, swarm, swarm with the wiki, swarm with the wiki and skills, and swarm with the brief, scored on the hidden suite, regressions, tokens and minutes, on the trials' scoreboard.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `docs/CHANGE-REQUESTS.md`, `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_changes.py::test_an_exam_s_run_neither_reads_nor_writes_the_role_s_journal`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_a_case_is_a_real_change_with_tests_and_its_key_stays_in_no_container`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_what_counts_is_what_failed_before_and_passes_after_by_running_it`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_the_score_is_the_hidden_suite_and_regressions_and_a_run_that_breaks_things_loses_points`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_outcomes_are_read_by_name_from_pytest_and_go`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_the_agents_copy_has_no_history_and_no_trace_of_the_answer`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_each_arm_goes_through_its_own_door`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_the_delivered_branch_is_found_whichever_way_the_arm_delivered`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_a_trial_ends_at_its_report_and_never_waits_for_the_owner_or_writes_the_wiki`
- Trace: `ops/replay.py`, `ops/trials.py`, `ops/changes.py`
- Gap: Run on one repository so far; the conventions verdict is on the run's report, not a scoreboard column, and the task text is the commit's own message, not rewritten by the Analyst.

### CHG-18

**A skill stays only where the exam improves** · should · constraint · **implemented**

A skill adopted for a code role shall be pinned by version, reviewed before it is installed, and kept only where the replay exam's score on that role's cases improved against the version without it.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-14](#sn-14)

- Trace: `ops/skills.py`, `config/skills.yaml`, `ops/changes.py`

### CHG-19

**The brief for a coding step** · should · functional · **implemented**

A coding step of an arm that asks for it shall get a brief of at most 4,000 characters plus its cut mark, marked as data and saying the code wins, that names the files of the specification or, when it names none, the files the request's words match in the code map's digest, and for those files how often they change, what changes with them, what uses them when a code graph exists and the tests that cover them when known; the profile's commands; the wiki's lines citing those files; past failures; and what it could not find out. The exam arm swarm+brief shall have the fixed wiki opening off and the brief on, with history facts computed at the case's parent commit and with files outside the trial's own tree left out, and its report shall state what still comes from newer code. Every other arm and every ordinary change shall stay on the wiki opening until the exam shows the brief is better.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `docs/REPO-KNOWLEDGE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repofacts.py::test_the_brief_tells_a_step_what_its_files_need`, `tests/test_repofacts.py::test_the_brief_finds_the_files_by_the_request_s_words_when_none_are_named`, `tests/test_repofacts.py::test_the_brief_keeps_to_its_budget_and_shows_the_cut`, `tests/test_repofacts.py::test_the_brief_says_what_it_does_not_know`, `tests/test_repofacts.py::test_a_trial_s_brief_is_the_wiki_of_repository_s_within_the_trial_s_own_tree`, `tests/test_repofacts.py::test_the_facts_at_a_commit_reach_nothing_later_and_stay_out_of_the_repo_page`, `tests/test_changes.py::test_the_brief_replaces_the_wiki_opening_only_where_the_arm_says_so`, `tests/test_replay.py::test_the_brief_arm_has_the_wiki_opening_off_the_brief_on_and_the_facts_of_the_parent`
- Trace: `ops/repofacts.py`, `ops/changes.py`, `ops/replay.py`, `ops/codegraph.py`
- Rationale: Of 77 coding builds on 7 November 2026 none called the code graph, though every one could. A tool an agent could call is not knowledge it has.

A change's task page shall show its way in one place, the request, the diff with its base and head, the suite, each check's verdict with every problem as its job reported it, and the pull request or why there is none.
