## CNX Connections

### CNX-01

**Nothing without a grant** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every mail tool shall refuse when the owner has not granted its scope, and tell the agent to ask him.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_nothing_without_a_grant`
- Trace: `images/mail/mail.py`

### CNX-02

**Grants by scope and time** · must · functional · **implemented**

The owner shall grant read, organize and trash separately for 1 hour, 8 hours or until revoked, revoke takes effect at once, and grants shall be refused from the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-connections.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_connection_grants_and_the_ai_phone`

### CNX-03

**Trash only, first batch asks, undo** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector shall move mail only to Trash, never expunge, never archive or trash protected mail (bank, payment, security, password reset, verification codes), wait for the owner's OK on the first Trash batch of each grant, and keep an undo record per batch.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_trash_asks_first_keeps_bank_mail_and_can_be_undone`

### CNX-04

**No sending** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector shall have no tool that sends, forwards, or changes filters or settings.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_mail_tools_cannot_send`

### CNX-05

**The connector is sealed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector's firewall shall allow only IMAPS to Gmail, and the app password shall never be logged or returned.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mail/entrypoint.sh`, `images/mail/mail.py`

### CNX-06

**A first-Trash approval survives a restart** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the mail connector restarts while a first-Trash approval waits, then the card shall not be left unanswerable; it shall be dropped and asked again, or kept answerable.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `same pattern as the AI phone's orphaned cards, fixed 2026-10-29`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_a_card_left_by_a_restart_is_dropped_with_its_late_answer_and_a_waited_card_stays`
- Trace: `images/mail/mail.py`
- Findings: [FND-14](#fnd-14)

### CNX-07

**A copy of the owner's mail on the NAS** · must · functional · **implemented**

A job on the NAS shall copy every Gmail message to a file with its checksum and index it, one way, opening All Mail read-only, every 15 minutes, within 2,000 MB of downloads a day. A mail that leaves Gmail shall stay on the NAS, marked as gone. The job shall write no message text anywhere else, call no model, and use an app password of its own that the owner types.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `nas/mailvault/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_every_mail_is_copied_exactly_and_can_be_checked`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_the_second_pass_fetches_only_what_is_new`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_a_mail_that_leaves_gmail_stays_on_the_nas_marked_as_gone`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_a_broken_answer_marks_nothing_as_gone`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_the_daily_allowance_is_kept_and_the_rest_follows_tomorrow`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_the_allowance_stays_under_google_s_limit`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_a_bad_copy_is_never_kept`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_the_census_says_who_writes_and_whom_you_answer_from_headers_alone`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_a_refused_password_is_said_not_asked_again_at_once`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_without_a_password_it_waits_and_says_so`, `tests/test_mailvault.py::test_it_protects_the_same_mail_as_the_connector_and_runs_sealed`
- Trace: `nas/mailvault/vault.py`, `nas/mailvault/compose.yaml`, `ops/deploy-mailvault.sh`, `ops/mailvault-password.sh`

### CNX-08

**The Postmaster files only the stack's own mail, and shows who writes** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Postmaster shall each hour file the stack's own mail, known by its exact sender, under the label AI Stack and out of the inbox, leave older mail where it is until the owner says so, read no message text, and remove nothing. The Your mail page shall show the copy's progress and one line per sender from the headers alone. A copy that stopped or stayed silent over a day shall be said once a day, and the end of the first copy once.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-connections.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_mail_tool_unfolds_a_long_header`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_a_row_continued_over_two_lines_is_read_whole`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_more_mail_than_the_tool_lists_is_read_day_by_day`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_only_the_stack_s_exact_sender_counts_as_its_mail`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_listing_is_read_with_a_protected_mark_too`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_new_stack_mail_is_filed_and_older_mail_waits_for_the_owner`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_past_is_only_listed_until_the_owner_says_so`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_without_a_grant_nothing_moves_and_it_says_so`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_copy_s_files_come_over_whole_and_a_bad_one_changes_nothing`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_owner_hears_of_a_stopped_copy_and_of_the_end_of_the_first_one`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_connector_files_the_stack_s_own_mail_though_its_subject_looks_protected`, `tests/test_postmaster.py::test_the_app_shows_the_copy_and_the_report`
- Trace: `ops/postmaster.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `images/mail/mail.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/keys.sh`

### CNX-09

**A search or label is one plain line** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector shall refuse a search or a label that holds a line break or another control character, with the reason, and send nothing to Gmail; it shall ask Gmail for UTF-8 so a search or label with umlauts works.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`, `docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_a_search_or_label_with_a_line_break_is_refused_and_nothing_is_sent`
- Trace: `images/mail/mail.py`
- Rationale: imaplib sends a line break inside an argument as it is, so model text steered by a mail could add IMAP commands of its own past the Trash grant and the protected-mail filter (review CNX-R1); umlauts raised an error (CNX-R2).

## CST Constraints

### CST-01

**One PC, one NAS** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The stack shall run on one Windows 10 Pro PC (i7-7700K, 16 GB RAM) inside WSL2 (Ubuntu-24.04, capped at 10 GB) with rootless Docker, plus a Synology NAS NAS (4 GB RAM) for git, CI, backups and Home Assistant.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `README.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `README.md`, `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`
- Trace: `nas/forgejo/compose.yaml`
- Rationale: This is the hardware there is; 32 GB RAM is recommended but not bought.
- Refined by: [PLT-01](#plt-01), [SWM-74](#swm-74), [SWM-75](#swm-75)

### CST-02

**Subscription models only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The gateway shall serve only subscription models (the ChatGPT subscription through LiteLLM), and Claude Code shall run only on the owner's Claude subscription; no paid per-token model shall be configured.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), [DEC-16](#dec-16), `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_every_key_holds_only_subscription_models`, `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_models_are_served_by_the_gateway`
- Trace: `config/litellm/config.yaml`, `ops/keys.sh`, `config/litellm/chatgpt-models.yaml`
- Findings: [FND-05](#fnd-05)
- Refined by: [BLD-08](#bld-08), [GW-01](#gw-01), [GW-02](#gw-02), [MIS-05](#mis-05), [SWM-02](#swm-02), [SWM-29](#swm-29), [TRL-11](#trl-11), [TSK-14](#tsk-14), [TSK-18](#tsk-18)

### CST-03

**No admin rights needed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every operation of the stack shall work without Windows administrator rights and without sudo in WSL; root in WSL is used only by the documented bootstrap and disk-heal steps.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `ops/windows/AGENTS.md`, `owner: find no-admin routes`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/windows/AGENTS.md`, `ops/bootstrap-wsl-1-root.sh`
- Refined by: [PLT-07](#plt-07)

### CST-04

**Nothing public** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No service shall be reachable from the internet; PC services shall bind 127.0.0.1 and be reached only through tailscale serve, NAS services only on the home LAN, and no router port forward shall exist.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `tests/test_compose_policy.py::test_every_compose_service_stays_on_loopback_without_socket_namespaces_or_privilege`, `.semgrep.yml`, `compose/AGENTS.md`
- Trace: `compose/apps.yaml`, `compose/core.yaml`, `nas/homeassistant/compose.yaml`
- Refined by: [HSE-09](#hse-09), [PLT-02](#plt-02), [SWM-27](#swm-27), [SWM-77](#swm-77), [TSK-10](#tsk-10), [TSK-19](#tsk-19)

### CST-05

**NAS hard rules** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No agent and no automated job shall delete NAS data or Docker volumes, change RAID/SHR or storage pools, touch unrelated shares, or weaken NAS security; NAS changes shall be applied only host-side by documented make targets or by the owner.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `nas/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `nas/AGENTS.md`, `ops/deploy-ha.sh`, `ops/deploy-testrepo.sh`
- Refined by: [HSE-09](#hse-09), [PLT-22](#plt-22), [SWM-69](#swm-69)

### CST-06

**Secrets encrypted, never read by agents** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Secrets shall exist only sops+age-encrypted in git and decrypted into a process environment per command; no agent shall read secrets/, a .env file or an *.enc.yaml file, and no secret shall be printed, logged or committed in plain text.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `secrets/AGENTS.md`, `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_deploy_never_prints_a_key`, `tests/test_server.py::test_forgejo_token_goes_to_the_runner_only`
- Trace: `.semgrep.yml`, `Makefile`
- Refined by: [BLD-13](#bld-13), [CNX-05](#cnx-05), [HSE-01](#hse-01), [SEC-10](#sec-10), [SEC-16](#sec-16), [SWM-68](#swm-68)

### CST-07

**No deploy without the owner** · must · constraint · **partial**

No change shall be deployed to the running stack without the owner's explicit approval.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `AGENTS.md`, [DEC-03](#dec-03)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_requires_review_checks_and_green_ci`, `tests/test_server.py::test_hermes_can_review_and_request_changes_but_never_approve`
- Gap: Builds need the owner's tap; an interactive Claude Code session lands under the owner's approval in its chat, through ops/land.sh (the checks on the exact commit, both locks, a recorded deploy; FND-04 fixed), but without the builder's AI review.
- Findings: [FND-04](#fnd-04)
- Refined by: [AST-14](#ast-14), [BLD-02](#bld-02), [BLD-20](#bld-20), [SWM-42](#swm-42), [SWM-69](#swm-69)

### CST-08

**Pinned versions, monthly upgrades** · must · constraint · **partial**

Every image and package shall be pinned to an exact version or digest, and upgrades shall happen only through UPGRADE.md, one component at a time.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `UPGRADE.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `versions.env`, `versions.lock`, `UPGRADE.md`
- Gap: Every image is pinned and the health board compares the running images with their tags and the lock file (PLT-20), but it only notices drift and does not prevent it. On 2026-10-30 five containers ran other builds than their tags (FND-67); they were recreated on their tagged builds on 2026-10-31.
- Findings: [FND-67](#fnd-67)
- Refined by: [PLT-19](#plt-19), [PLT-20](#plt-20)

### CST-09

**English** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Code, comments, UI text, docs, reports, wikis, Telegram messages and commit messages shall be in English.

- Parents: [SN-12](#sn-12)
- Sources: [DEC-02](#dec-02), `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_desk.py::test_every_briefing_is_english_even_for_a_topic_once_marked_german`
- Trace: `AGENTS.md`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `images/desk/desk.py`
- Findings: [FND-21](#fnd-21)

### CST-10

**No bot-detection evasion, no ToS bypass** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No part of the stack shall evade bot detection or CAPTCHAs, or automate a provider's web interface against its terms (no ChatGPT-web bridge).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-12](#dec-12), `images/browser/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/browser/AGENTS.md`, `images/pricewatch/AGENTS.md`
- Refined by: [RES-06](#res-06)

### CST-11

**No credentials or payment data typed by agents** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No agent shall type a password, a payment card, a bank or account number, or a one-time code, and no agent shall execute a financial trade or transfer; a purchase shall happen only as the owner-approved final tap of SHP requirements.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`, `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_secret_fields_and_card_numbers_are_never_typed`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_final_check_passes_only_the_approved_order`
- Refined by: [SHP-07](#shp-07), [SHP-09](#shp-09), [SWM-69](#swm-69)

### CST-12

**Not the main phone** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall drive only the old test phone (ai-phone), never the owner's main phone.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-17](#dec-17), `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/phone/entrypoint.sh`, `compose/apps.yaml`
- Refined by: [PHN-08](#phn-08)

### CST-13

**The benchmark stays frozen** · must · constraint · **implemented**

eval/bench-v1.yaml shall never change; a new benchmark is a new file with its own frozen hash.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `eval/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/check.sh`, `eval/FROZEN.sha256`

### CST-14

**Memory budget** · should · constraint · **violated**

The services in WSL together shall stay within the 10 GB WSL cap, each with a mem_limit, and the Windows host shall keep at least 1 GB free while the stack, one build and one Claude session run.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`, `docs/DOGFOOD.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_full_swap_is_a_note_and_an_alert_when_ram_is_short_too`
- Trace: `compose/AGENTS.md`, `ops/windows/host-probe.ps1`, `ops/crew.py`
- Gap: The Windows host drops to about 0.45 GB free with Claude sessions and builds running, and wsl.exe then times out; nothing budgets memory across services (FND-18). Since 2026-10-30 the health board notes full swap and alerts when RAM is short too (This PC), so the limit is seen before reads fail.
- Findings: [FND-18](#fnd-18)
- Refined by: [PLT-01](#plt-01), [PLT-21](#plt-21), [SWM-74](#swm-74), [SWM-86](#swm-86), [TSK-08](#tsk-08)

### CST-15

**The Max plan's API credits, capped, for critical calls only** · must · constraint · **proposed**

The gateway may serve one strong Claude model on the API key of the Console organisation linked to the Max plan's monthly API credits, that organisation having no card and no auto-reload; only the calls of docs/NEXT-PHASE.md Part 6 may use it, the stack shall move them back to the ChatGPT subscription at 80 % of the month's credits, and no other per-token model shall be configured.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: some agents with low token throughput but high criticality use stronger models`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -
- Rationale: CST-02 allows subscription models only; the credits come with the Max subscription and stop when spent, so they add no cost, but they change CST-02's wording, which is the owner's decision (D8).

## CRW Crew

### CRW-01

**Crew jobs are agents** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each crew job shall appear as an agent with its schedule, last and next run, last result and health: its schedule, next run and on/off switch from its n8n workflow (WFL-09; the backup's and the restore drill's from their timers), its runs from its systemd unit or, after a restart of WSL, from the step service's record.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_systemd_state_becomes_crew_cards`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_text_times_and_intervals`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_crew_actions_are_validated`, `tests/test_n8n_move.py::test_a_jobs_schedule_and_next_run_come_from_its_workflows_cron`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/n8n_jobs.py`

### CRW-02

**Crew schedules are editable in the app** · must · functional · **deprecated**

The owner shall be able to set a crew job's schedule (a preset, an interval or an OnCalendar time), switch it off or on, and run it now from its Settings, applied as a systemd drop-in.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_text_times_and_intervals`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_crew_actions_are_validated`
- Reason: DEC-51: a crew job's schedule and switch are its n8n workflow's since 10 Nov 2026; the owner changes them on n8n's canvas inside the app (Workflows, WFL-11). Its Settings shows the schedule read-only in words with a link to its workflow (APP-68); the crew_schedule action left the app, the server and the runner on 10 Nov 2026. Run now stays.
- Superseded by: `W`, `F`, `L`, `-`, `1`, `1`

### CRW-03

**The weekly self-review** · must · functional · **implemented**

Once a week the self-review shall collect the stack's signals (failed runs other than usage-limit pauses, failed jobs, error events, failed or rolled-back builds, red CI, open review items, newer versions) and turn them into ideas in the swarm's Ledger with one model call, and make no model call when there are no signals.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_scan_turns_signals_into_ideas_and_refines_a_known_one`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_signals_no_model_call`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_when_a_self_review_is_due`
- Trace: `ops/improve.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

### CRW-04

**The owner decides every idea** · must · functional · **implemented**

Build it shall turn an idea into an ordinary build assignment (review and tap-to-deploy still apply) and move the idea to active with its build linked, and Dismiss shall decline it with an optional reason that the proposing agent gets as a lesson and later reviews see.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-19](#dec-19)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_accept_creates_a_build_and_dismiss_becomes_a_lesson`

### CRW-05

**What an open idea covers is refined, not proposed again** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a new idea is for health issues or a detector kind that an open idea already covers, the Ledger shall add the sighting as evidence to that idea instead of creating another, and a detector fix shall never cover every failure of an agent.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29: 23 proposals, a third duplicates`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_a_detector_fix_never_covers_every_failure`
- Trace: `ops/improve.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/doctor.py`

### CRW-06

**A cap on open proposals per agent** · could · functional · **deprecated**

Each proposing agent shall keep at most its cap of open proposals (doctor 6, maintainability 2, tests 1, self-review 5).

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`
- Reason: The owner rejected caps on 2026-10-29 (DEC-10); the code still enforces them until the swarm lifecycle replaces them (FND-12).
- Superseded by: `S`, `W`, `M`, `-`, `0`, `8`
- Findings: [FND-12](#fnd-12)

### CRW-07

**The maintainability review** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every Monday the maintainability review shall read the code read-only with Claude Code and propose at most 2 changes, only where code changed often lately, caused a recent bug or where docs now mislead. It shall read origin's newest main from the builder's own clone, made from Forgejo when the machine has none yet (as every crew answer and replay in the sandbox does).

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`, `exercise through n8n, 10 Nov 2026: the review failed on a missing workbench clone`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_the_review_and_the_board_answers_get_the_builders_clone_first_also_on_a_fresh_machine`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/evals.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `n8n/workflows/crew-maintainability-review.json`

### CRW-08

**The test agent** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every Tuesday the test agent shall run the suite three times, report flaky, failing and slow tests and untested functions, and propose tests only for recently changed code, without a model.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_test_agent_finds_flaky_failing_and_slow`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_untested_functions`
- Findings: [FND-28](#fnd-28)

### CRW-09

**The health doctor diagnoses, sceptically** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every 6 hours, and within 30 minutes of a new alert (at most once per 30 minutes), the health doctor shall gather redacted evidence for each open health issue and have Claude Code classify it as expected, noise, external or real with cause, fix and confidence.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_doctor.py::test_open_issues_come_from_the_board_one_per_issue`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_event_lines_keep_errors_when_they_do_not_fit`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_a_new_alert_calls_the_doctor_at_most_every_30_minutes`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_the_doctor_is_a_crew_agent_with_its_units`
- Trace: `ops/doctor.py`

### CRW-10

**The doctor proposes, never acts** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The health doctor shall turn real faults into fix proposals and noise or expected issues into detector proposals, change no code or setting, acknowledge nothing by itself, and wait for the next run when the Claude limit is reached.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_doctor.py::test_diagnoses_become_findings_and_proposals`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_doctor_waits_for_a_usage_limit_without_failing`

### CRW-11

**Phone cleanup steps** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every Sunday the phone cleanup shall turn the AI phone's storage facts into one step per item; each step shall run on the phone only after the owner's Approve in the app (never from the AI phone), exactly once, with the phone's checks, and report the space it freed; a dismissed step shall not be proposed again.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_cleanup_facts_become_one_step_per_item`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approve_writes_exactly_one_step_and_results_come_back`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_a_dismissed_step_stays_dismissed`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_the_phone_runs_an_approved_step_once_with_its_checks`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approve_all_hands_over_every_open_step_shown_and_the_phone_runs_one_at_a_time`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approve_all_is_refused_from_the_ai_phone_and_the_phone_runs_steps_in_turn`
- Trace: `ops/phone_agents.py`, `images/phone/phone.py`

### CRW-12

**The phone check fails only on real faults** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every Monday the phone check shall fail only when the AI phone is not connected, its battery is low while unplugged, or its storage is nearly full; an old security patch shall be a note.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`, [DEC-08](#dec-08)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_phone_check_fails_only_on_real_faults`
- Trace: `ops/phone_agents.py`
- Findings: [FND-01](#fnd-01)

### CRW-13

**The inbox digest only reads** · should · functional · **implemented**

While the owner grants Gmail read, the inbox digest shall hand a newsletter on a watched topic to that topic (MIS-28), file other newsletters worth keeping for his topics into the vault inbox each morning, read back to its last finished run and say when the window held more than it listed, never look at bank, security or code mails, never organise or delete mail, and do nothing (saying so) without the grant.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_inbox_digest_does_nothing_without_the_grant`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_inbox_digest_files_the_picks_and_skips_protected`
- Trace: `ops/inbox.py`
- Findings: [FND-121](#fnd-121)

## GW Gateway

### GW-01

**Every model call through the gateway** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every agent shall reach models only through the gateway (http://litellm:4000), never a provider directly; the only exception is Claude Code, which uses the owner's Claude subscription.

- Parents: [CST-02](#cst-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `config/hermes/AGENTS.md`, `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_models_are_served_by_the_gateway`
- Trace: `config/litellm/config.yaml`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `images/litellm/patch_cache_key.py`

### GW-02

**No fallback model** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The gateway shall configure no fallback to another model; when the subscription pauses, callers wait and retry.

- Parents: [CST-02](#cst-02), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_every_key_holds_only_subscription_models`, `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_budget_mode_is_retired`

### GW-03

**Streamed tool calls keep their arguments** · must · functional · **implemented**

When ChatGPT streams a tool call whose arguments arrive only in the closing event, the gateway shall pass them on as one argument delta, and the image build shall fail if the patch no longer applies exactly.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_arguments_only_in_done_become_one_delta`, `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_calls_that_streamed_deltas_are_left_alone`, `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_patch_is_idempotent_and_refuses_changed_code`, `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_dockerfile_base_matches_the_pin`
- Trace: `images/litellm/patch_stream_args.py`

### GW-04

**One prompt cache per conversation** · must · quality · **implemented**

When a request carries no session id, the gateway shall give it a stable one derived from the key, the system prompt and the first user message, so that the calls of one run share the provider's prompt cache.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tokens.py::test_one_conversation_keeps_one_session_id`, `tests/test_tokens.py::test_gateway_loads_it_and_the_cache_key_stays_off_by_default`
- Trace: `images/litellm/cache_affinity.py`

### GW-05

**Tool servers by grant** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The gateway shall expose an MCP server to a key only when ops/keys.sh grants it, and MCP servers shall be reachable only on internal networks.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/keys.sh`, `config/litellm/config.yaml`

### GW-06

**Tool timeouts cover the waits behind them** · must · quality · **implemented**

Each MCP server's gateway timeout shall exceed the longest wait behind it (the AI phone's 15-minute approval, research within 600 s), and Hermes' MCP timeout shall exceed the gateway's.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`, `images/research/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/litellm/config.yaml`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`

### GW-07

**Every call is recorded** · must · functional · **implemented**

The gateway shall record every model call and every MCP tool call (key, model, tokens incl. cached, duration, status, error) and send traces to Phoenix.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/litellm/config.yaml`

### GW-08

**The tool-call probe after gateway changes** · should · process · **implemented**

When the gateway's image or configuration changes, the deploy shall run the streamed tool-call probe.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/probe_tool_calls.py`, `ops/deploy.py`

### GW-09

**A critical-call model with a fallback and a budget meter** · should · functional · **proposed**

The gateway shall serve claude-critical (Claude Opus 5.5) with the ChatGPT subscription as its fallback on an error, a refusal or a spent budget; the Stack page shall show the month's spend on it against the credits; a test shall fail when code other than the named critical calls asks for it.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

## GEN Generalisation

### GEN-01

**A check says pass, fail or unknown** · should · constraint · **implemented**

A check that could not run shall report unknown and show on the health board, never pass or silence.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_board_that_cannot_be_read_proves_nothing_and_reopens_nothing`

### GEN-02

**One queue for what waits on the owner** · should · constraint · **implemented**

Every wait the owner can act on shall reach one queue, and Home and the queue shall read the same list.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_changes.py::test_a_stopped_change_waits_on_the_board_until_it_is_answered_and_can_be_left_stopped`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_what_the_owner_must_answer_reaches_the_queue_and_a_state_does_not`

### GEN-03

**A journey that decides** · should · constraint · **implemented**

A nightly journey shall take a test item through the queue, decide it and check the result, and shall fail when deciding stops working.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_journeys.py::test_the_decide_journey_takes_a_test_item_through_the_queue_and_leaves_nothing_behind`, `tests/test_journeys.py::test_the_decide_journey_fails_when_deciding_stops_working`

### GEN-04

**One forge module** · should · constraint · **partial**

All calls to the forge shall go through one module behind one interface, so that a deployment can use another forge by adding an implementation; a pull request may come from a fork the deployment names.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`, `docs/PLATFORM.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_forge.py::test_bitbucket_dc_opens_a_draft_comments_reads_state_and_ci_and_declines`, `tests/test_forge.py::test_forgejo_is_home_as_before_and_no_token_means_no_call`, `tests/test_forge.py::test_the_work_example_uses_bitbucket_and_never_merges_and_a_build_is_not_merged_there`
- Acceptance: Test: no file but the forge module calls a forge's API, and the change loop opens a draft pull request on Forgejo and on a Bitbucket Data Center stand-in through the same interface.
- Trace: `ops/forge.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `deployments/example-work/deployment.env`
- Gap: Pull requests, comments, their closing and the builds' CI go through ops/forge.py (Forgejo and Bitbucket Data Center), and MERGE=never keeps a change its draft pull request; the CI reads of ops/missions.py and ops/digest.py and the making of repositories (ops/repos.py, a home feature) still call Forgejo themselves, and Bitbucket is tested against a stand-in server only.

### GEN-05

**A model policy file** · should · constraint · **implemented**

Which providers and models a deployment may use shall come from one policy file, which the card check, the checks and every Claude CLI call read; the gateway's token shall reach the gateway from the secret the policy names.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`, `docs/PLATFORM.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_modelpolicy.py::test_the_gateway_token_reaches_the_gateway_container_under_one_name`, `tests/test_modelpolicy.py::test_home_is_subscriptions_only_and_its_claude_calls_are_as_before`, `tests/test_modelpolicy.py::test_a_gateway_deployment_serves_only_the_gateways_models_and_refuses_any_other`, `tests/test_modelpolicy.py::test_the_claude_cli_under_a_gateway_gets_the_gateway_and_never_the_oauth_token`, `tests/test_modelpolicy.py::test_a_policy_that_mixes_or_leaves_out_is_refused_with_what_to_change`, `tests/test_modelpolicy.py::test_no_claude_call_passes_the_oauth_token_itself_and_the_checks_run_the_policy`
- Acceptance: Test: in a gateway_only deployment LiteLLM's configuration holds no subscription model, every role resolves to a gateway alias, and a request for another model is refused.

### GEN-06

**Constraints split by scope** · should · constraint · **planned**

Constraints that hold for every deployment shall be kept apart from the ones this deployment chose.

- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`, `docs/PLATFORM.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`
- Acceptance: Test: requirements/constraints.yaml holds only what holds everywhere, and each deployment's values come from its constraints.yaml.

### GEN-07

The owner's name and memory bank shall come from the deployment's configuration, and every answer the owner gives shall record who gave it.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`, `docs/PLATFORM.md`

- Acceptance: Test: the owner's name and memory bank are read from the deployment, and the code names no person.

**A clean install from one file** · should · constraint · **partial**

A second stack shall come up on a clean machine from the deployment's configuration file alone, proven by a rehearsal, and the guard test shall cover every value and folder.

### GEN-09

**Domains own their pages and routes** · should · constraint · **proposed**

A domain shall declare its pages and routes in its own module, and the Conductor shall name no domain.

- Sources: `docs/GENERALISATION.md`

**One schedule table** · should · constraint · **proposed**

The app shall show in one table everything that starts by itself, whichever mechanism starts it.

**Where work runs** · should · constraint · **partial**

A step kind shall be able to name where it runs (WSL or Windows); a Windows step shall run only as a job kind declared and approved on Windows, as its own standard account, with typed values and a patch whose paths are checked.

### GEN-12

**More than one person** · could · constraint · **proposed**

The stack shall support more than one person, each with a role and a sign-in, once a second real user exists.

### GEN-13

**Features per deployment** · should · constraint · **partial**

A deployment shall switch each feature on or off; a feature that is off shall start no service or timer, its checks shall report not configured, its pages shall be hidden and its scripts shall make no network call.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/PLATFORM.md`, [DEC-45](#dec-45)

- Acceptance: Test: with a feature off its services, timers and pages are gone, its checks say not configured, and its scripts exit before any network call; home with every feature on behaves as before.
- Trace: `ops/deployment.py`, `deployments/home/deployment.env`, `deployments/example-work/deployment.env`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `ops/deploy.py`, `ops/new-machine.sh`, `ops/ci-compose-check.sh`
- Gap: The feature list, the compose profiles, the timers and the scripts of a feature follow it; the app's pages and the health board's checks do not yet (a check of a feature that is off still runs).

### GEN-14

**Customer data stays in, by the network** · must · constraint · **partial**

In a deployment with an egress list, no container and no host-side process of the stack shall reach a host off that list, and work of the data class customer shall send no content out (web tools, messages, backups, publish); a deployment's gateway shall list only the tool servers of its features, none that sends data out for customer data, and customer code shall never be copied to Forgejo.

- Acceptance: Test: in a deployment with an egress list a connection to a host off the list is refused from a container and from the host side, and a customer change sends only status out.
- Trace: `ops/dataguard.py`, `ops/egress-host.sh`, `ops/egress-selftest.sh`, `ops/notify.py`, `deployments/home/data.yaml`, `deployments/example-work/data.yaml`, `deployments/example-work/egress.txt`
- Gap: The data class, the check against outbound features, the status-only messages and the outbound rule exist; the rule is proven in a sandbox namespace, not yet applied on a work machine (ops/egress-host.sh is run as root there), and the Windows worker's network is the company IT's to limit.

### GEN-25

**Trackers, read by polling and written through gates** · should · functional · **partial**

The stack shall poll trackers (Jira Data Center through atlassian-python-api, Forgejo issues) with a cursor and hand each change of an item on once, with the comments from others not handed on before, for the run named after the item, and write comments, one summary comment, attachments and transitions only as action classes, idempotently, with shadow mode per kind of write, each leaving it when the owner taps, after a stated number of approved shadow items. The one stated exception among the stack's outside writes is a draft pull request (GEN-26): it goes to the forge directly, with no shadow step, because it is a draft that the stack never merges (the owner's decision, DEC-54).

**Jobs of hours wait their turn at a place** · must · functional · **implemented**

The local worker (ops/localworker.py) shall run one job at a time and write in its status the job it runs and how many requests wait behind it, refreshed every 10 seconds while a job runs; the stack shall give a request up as not picked up only after it waited 5 minutes while its worker was not running or idle, never for the time it waited behind a running job; and a job's one run limit shall be its kind's own minutes, from its file (1 minute to a day).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/PLATFORM.md`, `docs/AI-STACK.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_domain_places.py::test_two_long_jobs_queued_at_one_place_both_finish_one_after_the_other_and_neither_is_given_up`, `tests/test_domain_places.py::test_the_pickup_clock_stands_while_the_worker_runs_another_job_and_counts_while_it_is_idle_or_gone`, `tests/test_domain_places.py::test_a_job_kind_sets_its_own_run_limit_in_its_file_and_the_status_carries_it`, `tests/test_winworker.py::test_a_job_past_its_time_limit_is_stopped`
- Acceptance: Test: two long toy jobs (scaled to 4 seconds against a pick-up timeout of 0.5 seconds) queued at one place both finish, one after the other, and the status named the running job with one queued; on a fast clock a request 10 minutes behind a running job is queued, and 5 minutes idle or gone gives it up; a kind of 600 minutes is valid and its minutes are in the status. Shown on 10 Nov 2026: two 6-minute jobs at a throwaway wsl-host worker in a temp folder, with the real 5-minute timeout, both done.
- Trace: `ops/localworker.py`, `ops/domain/places.py`, `ops/winjobs.py`, `docs/PLATFORM.md`
- Rationale: A licensed tool runs for hours at the wsl-host place. Before, the second of two long jobs was given up after 5 minutes while it was only queued (CR of 10 Nov 2026, N3). Capacity pools are left out on purpose.

### GEN-41

**Jira read with a cursor that holds** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Jira adapter shall turn its cursor (Jira's time with the server's offset) into the token user's time zone (read from /rest/api/2/myself) before asking JQL, ask with >= of the cursor's minute and drop the (key, updated) pairs of that minute it handed on before, page until Jira has given every match, so a minute with more items than a page never holds the cursor, and build its JQL without an empty clause; a query with its own ORDER BY is refused at load with the reason, and that tracker is not asked.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_domain_trackers.py::test_jira_reads_by_jql_with_its_cursor_and_edits_its_one_summary`, `tests/test_domain_trackers.py::test_jira_reads_its_cursor_in_the_token_users_time_zone_not_the_servers`, `tests/test_domain_trackers.py::test_jira_hands_on_60_items_of_one_minute_once_and_its_cursor_moves_on`, `tests/test_domain_trackers.py::test_jira_builds_its_query_without_an_empty_clause_and_refuses_one_with_its_own_order`
- Acceptance: Test, with a fake client: a server offset other than the user's zone gives the user's minute; 60 items in one minute are handed on once over two pages and the cursor moves on to a later item; an empty query gives a query without an empty clause, and a query with ORDER BY is refused with its reason and not asked.
- Trace: `ops/domain/trackers.py`

### GEN-42

**A pack's workflow uses only the nodes the deployment's n8n loads** · must · functional · **implemented**

A pack's workflow shall be refused at load, with the node and the reason, when it uses a node type outside the list the deployment's n8n loads (N8N_NODES_INCLUDE in its deployment.env, handed to n8n as NODES_INCLUDE, read through the data guard's dataguard.n8n_nodes(), the one reading of it, WFL-17), or, without that list, n8n's command or local file trigger node; a pack's webhook shall listen under item/<pack>/ or <pack>/ only, so a pack takes no path of the stack's or of another pack.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`, [DEC-51](#dec-51)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_pack_workflow_with_a_node_off_the_deployments_allowlist_is_refused_with_the_reason`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_the_toy_packs_item_workflow_is_a_whole_n8n_workflow_on_logic_nodes_only`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_pack_workflow_that_is_no_n8n_workflow_or_runs_a_shell_command_is_refused_with_the_reason`
- Acceptance: Test: with the data guard's list of logic nodes, a workflow with an HTTP Request node is refused naming the node and N8N_NODES_INCLUDE; without the list it loads; a list that is no JSON list loads no workflow and says why; a webhook on run/crew-checker is refused; the toy pack's item workflow uses logic nodes only and holds no placeholder.
- Trace: `ops/domain/packs.py`, `ops/dataguard.py`, `tests/fixtures/packs/toy/workflows/ticket.json`

### GEN-43

**A pack's workflows run in n8n, and a canvas edit is a proposal** · must · functional · **implemented**

The running commit of each pack shall have its workflows in n8n, named <pack>/<name>, each under an id made from that name and never one the file names, published or not as its file says: imported when the owner approves the commit and on each deploy, replaced in place by an approved new commit, and unpublished when the pack is refused or leaves packs.txt. An edit made on n8n's canvas to a pack's workflow shall not change what runs: it goes to the pack's own repository as a branch ai/canvas-<date> and, where the pack's forge knows the repository, a draft pull request, never into the stack's repository, and the approved version is imported again; it runs only once a commit with it is approved.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`, [DEC-51](#dec-51)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_an_approved_packs_workflows_reach_n8n_under_ids_from_their_names_and_leave_when_the_pack_goes`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_new_commit_replaces_its_workflows_in_place_and_a_refusal_unpublishes_them`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_canvas_edit_is_a_proposal_and_the_approved_version_keeps_running`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_proposal_goes_to_the_packs_own_repository_as_a_branch_and_a_draft_pull_request`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_an_export_sends_a_packs_workflow_to_its_repository_never_into_the_stacks_git`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_deploy_syncs_the_running_packs_before_its_one_restart`
- Acceptance: Test: approving the toy pack imports its three workflows under ids from their names with one restart, a second sync changes nothing, removing it unpublishes them; a new commit replaces them in place and refusing it brings the old version back; a canvas edit is sent once and the approved version runs; the edit lands on the pack's branch with a draft pull request; an export writes only the stack's workflows into git; a deploy syncs the packs before its one restart.
- Trace: `ops/domain/packs.py`, `ops/n8n_sync.py`, `tests/fixtures/packs/toy/workflows/ticket.json`

### GEN-44

**A pack's item workflow runs end to end in a customer-class deployment, and the core names no domain** · must · functional · **implemented**

In a customer-class deployment the stack shall carry a pack's item workflow from its tracker item to its end: the item starts one run (also when its event comes twice), an agent step triages it, every tracker write stays in shadow, a wait on the item times out to a reminder and is woken by a reply from someone else but not by the stack's own comment, a job of hours at the wsl-host place finishes with a second job queued behind it, a change goes out as a draft pull request whose review comment wakes the run, and the run writes its summary and ends; restarts of the runner side and of n8n while the run waits and while a job runs shall lose nothing, n8n shall get only summaries, and the run shall export as one zip. No file of ops/, images/, compose/, config/ or n8n/ shall name the domain a pack brings.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`, `docs/AI-STACK.md`, [DEC-54](#dec-54)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_work_deployment.py::test_the_toy_pack_runs_end_to_end_in_a_customer_class_deployment_against_stand_ins`, `tests/test_work_deployment.py::test_the_core_names_nothing_of_the_stand_in_domain`
- Acceptance: Test, against stand-ins (a stand-in tracker, a stand-in forge, the local worker in a temp folder, the step service and the run register on temp paths, example-work's data class and node list) with a stand-in n8n that runs the toy's item workflow node by node: every step of the statement, the runner side reloaded from disk mid-wait and mid-job, n8n restarted mid-wait and mid-job, each command submitted once, nothing of the triage's whole text in n8n's executions while the register holds it, and the zip with every step in order; the stand-in domain's names over ops/, images/, compose/, config/ and n8n/ find nothing. Shown on 10 Nov 2026 on a throwaway n8n 2.44.7 with example-work's node list and the step service's real runner code in a throwaway container: the same flow ended in success with n8n restarted mid-wait and mid-job, one execution for the item, and none of the triage's whole text in n8n's execution data (docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md §12).
- Trace: `ops/flows.py`, `ops/runreg.py`, `ops/domain/trackers.py`, `ops/domain/forgework.py`, `ops/domain/places.py`, `ops/domain/packs.py`, `ops/domain/record.py`, `tests/fixtures/packs/toy/workflows/ticket.json`
- Rationale: The CR of 10 Nov 2026 for the first work deployment counts as done when this holds for the toy pack against stand-ins.

### GEN-45

**Home's inbox repository is a front door** · should · functional · **partial**

The home deployment shall watch the owner's private repository <FORGEJO_OWNER>/inbox on its Forgejo as its tracker (deployments/home/trackers.yaml, the owner named once in deployment.env as ${FORGEJO_OWNER}; restart on_comment; asked every 5 minutes), so the work deployment's item flow runs at home every day: an issue starts the stack's own item workflow home/inbox (n8n/workflows/item-home-inbox.json, webhook item/home/inbox, inactive until the lead switches it on), whose main agent reads the issue and answers it or does it, the whole answer is commented on the issue, the run waits up to 7 days for the owner's reply, answers that in a second comment, writes the summary and closes the issue; with no reply it writes the summary and closes. Its writes start in shadow like every tracker's. No pack may take the names home, item or run, so no pack can listen on the stack's own item webhooks or n8n's webhook roots.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`, `docs/PLATFORM.md`, [DEC-54](#dec-54)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_home_inbox.py::test_homes_tracker_is_its_inbox_repository_and_its_entry_is_valid`, `tests/test_home_inbox.py::test_the_inbox_workflow_listens_where_the_run_register_starts_it_and_is_built_from_the_real_kinds`, `tests/test_home_inbox.py::test_an_issue_in_the_inbox_is_answered_waits_for_the_owners_reply_and_is_closed`, `tests/test_home_inbox.py::test_with_nothing_live_and_no_reply_in_seven_days_the_inbox_run_holds_its_writes_and_ends`, `tests/test_domain_trackers.py::test_a_trackers_entry_may_name_a_value_of_its_deployment_and_one_it_does_not_set_is_refused`, `tests/test_domain_packs.py::test_a_pack_that_clashes_or_names_no_commit_of_its_source_is_refused_with_the_reason`
- Acceptance: Test: home's trackers.yaml loads with no refused entry, its repository from FORGEJO_OWNER; the inbox workflow listens on item/<the tracker's workflow> and is made of the real kinds; against a stand-in Forgejo where every write has the owner's account and a stand-in n8n running the workflow, an issue starts one run (also when its change comes twice), the main agent's answer is commented whole, the stack's own comment does not wake the run, the owner's unmarked reply does, the second answer, the summary and the close follow, the run ends, and a later comment starts a new run; with nothing live and no reply in 7 days every write waits in shadow and the run ends; a value a trackers.yaml names that its deployment does not set is refused; a pack named home or item is refused.
- Trace: `deployments/home/trackers.yaml`, `n8n/workflows/item-home-inbox.json`, `ops/domain/trackers.py`, `ops/domain/packs.py`, `ops/flows.py`, `ops/runreg.py`, `images/mission-control/guide/22-workflows.md`
- Gap: Built and tested against a stand-in Forgejo and a stand-in n8n; live once the lead switches on the poller's and the inbox's workflows and the owner lets comment, summary and transition go live.

## HSE House

### HSE-01

**Connect by Home Assistant's own sign-in** · must · functional · **implemented**

Connect shall send the owner to Home Assistant's own sign-in and back, accept only well-formed codes, and store a long-lived token encrypted; the stack shall never see his password.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_connect_rejects_odd_codes`, `tests/test_server.py::test_house_connect_sends_owner_to_home_assistant_and_back`
- Trace: `ops/house.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`

### HSE-02

**Status of every device by room** · must · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall read every light, plug, switch, shutter and Echo by room every 30 seconds, and say plainly when it is not connected or its token is refused.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_snapshot_over_websocket`, `tests/test_house.py::test_snapshot_bad_token`, `tests/test_house.py::test_not_connected`

### HSE-03

**Switching by name** · must · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall switch, dim and move what it is asked to by name, and refuse unknown or ambiguous names with the candidates.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_switch_light_dim_and_cover`, `tests/test_house.py::test_unknown_or_ambiguous_names`

### HSE-04

**Protected things only by the owner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A plug or switch whose name says it powers the fridge, freezer, router, NAS, PC, server or similar, and anything the network and NAS integrations offer (router Tools, Synology DSM) whatever its name, shall be readable by agents but switchable only by the owner on the House page, also not through an Alexa command.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_protected_things_only_by_the_owner`, `tests/test_house.py::test_snapshot_over_websocket`, `tests/test_server.py::test_house_owner_taps_ai_phone_and_hermes`

### HSE-05

**Alexa commands, without shopping** · must · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall pass a command to an Echo as if spoken, and refuse commands that buy or order or name a protected thing.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_alexa_command_and_refusals`
- Findings: [FND-87](#fnd-87)

### HSE-06

**Speaking on the Echos** · should · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall speak a message on one Echo, or on all of them with the announcement chime.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_say_everywhere_with_chime`

### HSE-07

**Talking to Hermes through an Echo** · should · functional · **implemented**

When an Echo hears a request starting with "Hermes" ("Alexa, Hermes …", "frag Hermes …"), the House service shall pass only that request to Hermes, say on the same Echo at once that Hermes heard it, and speak its plain answer there, at most 30 per hour, one at a time; nothing else said to Alexa shall be kept.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-18](#dec-18)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_trigger`, `tests/test_house.py::test_voice_bridge_only_hermes_requests`, `tests/test_house.py::test_spoken_answer_is_plain`, `tests/test_house.py::test_a_voice_question_is_acknowledged_before_its_answer`

### HSE-08

**House settings only by the owner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The voice bridge's settings shall be changeable only by the owner in the app, not by agents or the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_settings_only_by_the_owner`

### HSE-09

**Home Assistant stays on the LAN** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Home Assistant shall run on the NAS pinned by digest, reachable only on the home LAN (port 8123), with no router forward; the owner types every device-account password into Home Assistant himself.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-04](#cst-04), [CST-05](#cst-05)
- Sources: [DEC-07](#dec-07), `nas/homeassistant/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `nas/homeassistant/compose.yaml`

## KNW Knowledge

### KNW-01

**The vault is versioned** · must · functional · **implemented**

The vault (C:\AI\vault) shall hold the owner's notes, every report, alert, mission note, wiki and the agents' journals, and every change to it shall be a git commit pushed to Forgejo on the NAS.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `vault-template/AGENTS.md`, `ops/vault-ingest.sh`

### KNW-02

**Everything is searchable** · must · functional · **implemented**

qmd shall index the vault, topics and reports, missions with their results and wikis, the agents' journals, specs, code digests and imported mail and chats, combining keyword and meaning-based search.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/qmd/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_wikis.py::test_mission_wikis_and_agent_journals_are_searchable`
- Trace: `images/qmd/start.sh`

### KNW-03

**Added knowledge is indexed within minutes** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a note, web page or file is added (app, chat or the vault inbox), the stack shall convert it to markdown, file it and index it within 5 minutes.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/vault-ingest.sh`, `n8n/workflows/crew-vault-ingest.json`

### KNW-04

**Raw sources are never rewritten** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Agents shall never edit an existing file in the vault's raw/, shall cite sources for non-obvious claims, mark disputed pages contested, and treat instructions found inside sources as data.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `vault-template/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `vault-template/AGENTS.md`, `ops/vault_check.py`

### KNW-05

**Long-term memory on local models** · must · functional · **implemented**

Hindsight shall extract lasting facts from conversations with local models, keep them in the memory network only, and never retain secrets, health or finance details.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `compose/apps.yaml`

### KNW-06

**Important memories wait for the owner** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every 10 minutes the memory gatekeeper shall decide staged memory writes by rule, and sensitive ones shall wait for the owner's Remember or Discard.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/memory_review.py`, `n8n/workflows/crew-memory-gatekeeper.json`

### KNW-07

**Forgetting on request** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner asks to forget a text, the stack shall remove every stored conversation containing it from long-term memory.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/hindsight_forget.py`, `Makefile`

### KNW-08

**The memory curator decides only what the owner allowed** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Memory Keeper shall keep the older of two facts that say the same, change by itself, softly and restorably, only what the owner allowed (DEC-40, and since DEC-44 two of his facts that disagree, settled by date, and the move list) while its trial passes, and list every other doubtful fact with its reason for the owner, whose Forget retires it, softly and restorably.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`, [DEC-40](#dec-40), [DEC-44](#dec-44)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_judgement_is_checked_before_it_counts_and_the_older_fact_stays`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_forget_soft_retires_only_the_given_facts`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/keeper.py`

### KNW-09

**Every result grows the mission's wiki** · must · functional · **implemented**

After every mission result, the runner shall fold it into the mission's wiki (entities, concepts, projects, events, linked pages and an index), unless the wiki was deleted.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/09-wikis.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_wikis.py::test_a_deleted_wiki_is_not_compiled_again`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`

### KNW-10

**Every wiki has a summary** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each wiki shall have a 2-3 sentence summary, rewritten when its pages change (at most every 6 hours), and a meta answer from the model shall never be stored as a summary.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/09-wikis.md`

- Trace: `ops/wikis.py`

### KNW-11

**Deleting a wiki is restorable** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner deletes a mission's wiki in the app, the runner shall remove it in one restorable vault commit and stop it growing back; the vault wiki and paths outside wikis shall not be deletable, and the delete shall be refused from the AI phone and from agents.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)

- Trace: `ops/wikis.py`

### KNW-12

**Browsing a wiki** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Wiki tab shall open on the list of every wiki, not on one picked for the owner; a wiki shall show its name, search, and its pages as one list per folder with each page's summary, with its graph and a tab with its summary, its agent and delete one tap away; an open page shall fill the screen with its links and backlinks (aliases resolved) and a way back to its wiki; and the app shall copy a page's original markdown only for pages that exist and are approved.

**Search quality is measured before changes** · should · process · **implemented**

Before a change to qmd's collections or models, the retrieval evaluation (recall@8, nDCG@8, MRR) shall be run before and after and the results compared.

## MEM Memory

### MEM-01

**Every memory is in one inventory** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall keep one inventory of every memory store (vault notes, vault wiki, agent wikis, journals, mission notes, long-term memory, the Ledger, skills, run records and the search index), refreshed every hour without delaying the runner's state, with one snapshot a day for 120 days.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_every_memory_store_is_declared_with_its_kind_writers_readers_limit_and_curator`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_a_store_that_cannot_be_measured_says_why_and_the_runner_never_waits`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `ops/missions.py`
- Refined by: [APP-55](#app-55)

### MEM-02

**Each memory has one kind and one home** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each memory store shall be declared with its kind (episodic, semantic, procedural, shared work or search), what it holds, who writes it, who reads it, its limit and its curator.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_every_memory_store_is_declared_with_its_kind_writers_readers_limit_and_curator`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`

### MEM-03

**One writer per wiki** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each wiki shall be a Ledger artifact owned by its one writer (the curator for the vault wiki, the desk for a briefing's topic wiki, the mission card for a mission's), and another agent's contribution shall reach it as a help on that artifact (SWM-109) that the writer's next compile folds in, naming the helper, and marks as folded. The Librarian's tidy (MEM-08) is the one other change to a wiki, and it adds no facts (DEC-32).

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_one_writer_per_wiki_folds_in_the_help_other_agents_gave_it`
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/wikis.py`

### MEM-04

**Each agent reads the wikis its card names** · should · functional · **deprecated**

Each agent's card shall name the wikis it reads (by default its own, the vault wiki and wikis on related subjects), a run's start shall search only those and its own wiki first of all, and a wiki built from the owner's personal mail (a mission that holds a Gmail grant) shall reach only its own mission. A topic's newsletters (MIS-28) are bulk mail handed through its report and do not make its wiki private (FND-121).

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_a_run_reads_the_wikis_its_card_names_its_own_first_and_never_a_private_one`
- Trace: `ops/journal.py`, `ops/registry.py`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/missions.py`
- Superseded by: `M`, `E`, `M`, `-`, `2`, `4`
- Findings: [FND-45](#fnd-45), [FND-121](#fnd-121)

### MEM-05

**One opening per run** · should · functional · **deprecated**

Every run's opening shall be assembled in one place in a fixed order within one budget (card instructions, the owner's messages, its own state, its knowledge, the owner's facts, the team), with the swarm's pack fetched once by the runner and the agent told not to call swarm_context again, and a mission's working state shall be a section of its journal that each run replaces, not a second file.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_a_run_s_opening_has_one_order_one_budget_and_the_swarm_pack_once`, `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_a_mission_s_working_state_lives_in_its_journal`, `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_an_old_note_moves_into_its_journal_even_when_its_mission_never_runs_again`
- Trace: `ops/journal.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/missions.py`
- Superseded by: `M`, `E`, `M`, `-`, `2`, `6`
- Findings: [FND-42](#fnd-42)

### MEM-06

**Personal facts in long-term memory, world knowledge in the wikis** · should · functional · **implemented**

Long-term memory (Hindsight) shall hold the owner's own facts (preferences, decisions, people), the wikis shall hold sourced knowledge of the world, and an agent's journal entry shall not be copied into long-term memory.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)

- Trace: `ops/journal.py`
- Findings: [FND-42](#fnd-42)

### MEM-07

**Memory is observable** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Stack page shall show every memory store with its kind, what it holds, who writes and reads it, its size, its growth over 7 days, how full it is against its limit, who curates it and when that last ran, and its problems.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-13](#sn-13)

- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### MEM-08

**Every memory has a limit and a curator** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every memory store shall have a limit and a curator, or a stated rule that keeps it small. The Librarian (the memory curator's weekly pass) shall lint every agent wiki, merge duplicate pages keeping every link of both (a merge that would lose or add one is refused), mark contradictions only with sources already on the pages, write the hub pages of the subjects two or more wikis share in the vault wiki, and run qmd cleanup on the search index, changing on its own only what the vault's history restores (one commit per pass, of exactly the files it wrote) and leaving forgetting to the owner (KNW-08). A store over its limit for 8 days shall raise an alert on its curator's health item.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_inventory_measures_each_store_names_its_problems_and_reads_growth_from_daily_snapshots`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_librarian_merges_marks_and_lints_every_agent_wiki_and_changes_only_what_the_vault_restores`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_merge_that_would_lose_a_link_is_refused_and_a_page_changed_meanwhile_is_left`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_what_a_pass_wrote_before_a_step_failed_is_still_in_its_commit_and_nothing_else_is`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_pair_judged_two_subjects_is_not_asked_again_until_one_of_its_pages_changes`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_search_index_cleanup_is_measured_before_and_after`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_memory_over_its_limit_for_eight_days_alerts_its_curator`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `ops/librarian.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/vault_check.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-43](#fnd-43), [FND-44](#fnd-44)

### MEM-09

**The wikis' map** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Wiki tab shall open with a map of every wiki of the vault and of a mission that is not archived (one bubble per wiki, sized by its pages, its name below, above or beside it where it covers no other name or bubble) and a line between two wikis for the subjects both have (a page with the same title or alias), each subject named in full on a tap; a tap on a bubble shall open its wiki (FND-83); an archived mission's wiki shall stay readable in a fold below the list.

### MEM-10

**Who reads and writes which wiki** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Wiki tab shall show for each wiki which agents read it and wrote it in the last 7 days, counted in runs, from the run record (a run's start brief, its vault searches, its file reads and writes, and every page a result compiles into a wiki).

### MEM-11

**Prompts are versioned files on the agent cards** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every agent prompt of ops/ and the Hermes configure scripts shall live in prompts/<owner>.md, one file per agent named by its card's prompts field (or by a service, or shared.md for templates more than one card uses). Each prompt shall carry a version (the sha of its text) that every send records in the run's record, with the file's version in mission and crew manifests and in the card hash. A prompt shall change only after a replay passed, which runs its last cases with the last approved text and the new one on the agent's own model and needs valid answers as often and a judge that prefers the old answer no more often. The build gate (ops/check.sh) shall refuse a prompt text with neither its first-record baseline nor a passed replay.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-33](#dec-33)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_prompts.py::test_no_agent_prompt_is_left_in_ops_or_a_configure_script`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_every_prompt_loads_from_its_file_with_its_version_and_keeps_it_through_format`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_a_send_records_the_ref_and_a_live_call_keeps_its_case`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_the_build_gate_refuses_a_changed_prompt_until_its_replay_passed`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_a_replay_the_judge_prefers_the_old_text_on_fails_and_the_gate_stays_shut`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_an_agent_s_standing_brief_is_replayed_on_the_requests_its_cases_bring`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_a_filled_prompt_that_drives_tools_is_replayed_as_a_dry_run`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_the_deploy_hands_each_configure_script_its_soul_text_from_the_prompts_file`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_card_names_its_own_prompts_file_and_every_prompts_file_has_an_owner`
- Trace: `ops/prompts.py`, `prompts/AGENTS.md`, `ops/registry.py`, `ops/deploy.py`, `ops/check.sh`, `ops/missions.py`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`

### MEM-13

**The desk's and the scout's prompts are files too** · could · functional · **implemented**

The Topic Desk's prompts (images/desk/desk.py), the research scout's brief and the research privacy check (images/research/server.py) shall live in prompts/desk.md, prompts/scout.md and prompts/guard.md under the same replay gate as every other prompt. Their containers shall read prompts/ through a read-only mount, log each prompt's version, and the desk shall hand its calls back as replay cases; a research call whose prompt file cannot be read shall be refused.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-33](#dec-33)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_prompts.py::test_no_agent_prompt_is_left_in_ops_or_a_configure_script`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_every_image_has_a_tag_the_deploy_rebuilds_also_one_run_from_an_ops_script`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_the_desk_and_the_scout_read_their_prompts_from_the_mount_and_the_desk_hands_back_its_cases`

- Findings: [FND-46](#fnd-46)

### MEM-12

**A changed fact is superseded, not overwritten** · could · functional · **implemented**

When a compile changes a fact on an agent wiki's page, the old fact shall be kept under "Superseded" with the date it stopped holding, and a line once under Superseded shall never be dropped; a page whose Contradictions section remains shall stay contested; and the Librarian shall mark an agent wiki's page not confirmed for 90 days as stale (a later compile that confirms it clears the mark). Long-term facts are never overwritten by an agent; the owner retires them (KNW-08).

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_compile_keeps_superseded_lines_and_a_contested_page_and_skips_merged_stubs`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_librarian_merges_marks_and_lints_every_agent_wiki_and_changes_only_what_the_vault_restores`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/librarian.py`

### MEM-14

**A compile sees what it rewrites** · must · functional · **implemented**

A wiki compile shall show the model each page it may rewrite whole up to 12,000 characters (past that its start, with the cut marked), and every list line of the old page that the model was not shown shall stay on the page under its own section.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_line_written_while_the_compile_waited_stays`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_compile_sees_a_long_page_whole_and_keeps_the_lines_it_was_not_shown`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`
- Findings: [FND-103](#fnd-103), [FND-110](#fnd-110)

### MEM-15

**Hand-offs by reference past a budget** · should · functional · **implemented**

A task step shall get each earlier result it builds on whole while they fit a budget together, and past it each result's marked start, its size and where the whole result is (a file in an OpenCode step's work folder, a vault file for a Hermes step; a Claude or build step takes them whole apart from its brief), and a prompt too long for one command-line argument shall be refused with its size.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_result_too_long_for_a_prompt_goes_by_reference_through_every_adapter`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`
- Rationale: FND-99 moved the results out of the builder's brief, but every step still gets them copied whole; a Hermes step takes its prompt as one argument, which Linux refuses past 128 KiB. A size contract test pushes a 150,000-character result through every adapter.
- Findings: [FND-104](#fnd-104)

### MEM-16

**A cut a model reads shows itself** · should · quality · **implemented**

Every cut of text going to a model (cap reason prompt) shall carry the textcap marker with how much is missing, and ops/truncation.py shall refuse a prompt cap without it.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MEMORY.md`, `docs/TRUNCATION-AUDIT.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_truncation.py::test_every_cap_names_its_reason_and_an_unexplained_one_fails_the_check`
- Trace: `ops/truncation.py`, `ops/textcap.py`
- Rationale: 92 of 96 prompt caps sliced without a mark; FND-100 and FND-103 showed that a model takes an unmarked cut for the whole text. All 98 prompt caps are marked since the audit of 2 November.
- Findings: [FND-105](#fnd-105)

### MEM-17

**A contradiction is worked to an end** · should · functional · **implemented**

A contradiction the Librarian marks shall become a Ledger item owned by its wiki's writer, the writer's next compile shall be told of it, a mark open for 14 days shall start a refinement task (TSK-34) that researches it, and a settled claim shall move under Superseded with the item closed.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_a_contradiction_on_a_wiki_becomes_a_dispute_a_task_researches_and_its_settling_closes`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_a_dispute_its_research_could_not_settle_stays_marked_on_both_pages`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_compile_is_told_of_the_disputes_on_the_pages_it_rewrites`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Findings: [FND-106](#fnd-106)

### MEM-18

**Long-term memory holds each fact once** · should · functional · **implemented**

Long-term memory shall keep one fact for one statement of the owner (a repeat confirms it, it is not added again), in English, with no facts on agents' runs, and a fact on a passing state shall carry the date it held; its retain mission shall ask for this at the source and the Memory Keeper (MEM-21) shall fix what still gets in.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_judgement_is_checked_before_it_counts_and_the_older_fact_stays`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_each_change_is_logged_with_what_was_there_and_undone`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `compose/apps.yaml`
- Rationale: Measured 2 Nov: the same voice preference up to five times, facts in Spanish and German, about 35 facts on agent runs, and 55 forget suggestions waiting since 30 October (KNW-08 retires only on the owner's tap; decision D2 in docs/MEMORY.md). The owner decided D2 on 2 Nov (DEC-40).
- Findings: [FND-107](#fnd-107)

### MEM-19

**The inventory has every store** · should · functional · **implemented**

The memory inventory (MEM-01) shall also declare a task's hand-offs (the steps' results), Hermes' own memory (USER.md), the code graphs and the chats, each with its kind, writer, reader, limit and curator.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), `docs/MEMORY.md`

- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
