### MEM-20

**A memory trial** · should · process · **implemented**

The stack shall have a trial with an answer key for the Memory Keeper's judgement (eval/trials/memory.yaml) that plants faults among true facts and scores the changes it would make itself (precision, a true fact touched counts against it) and the faults it finds (recall), and a contradiction of the owner's facts shall count as found only when the older of the two is dated (DEC-44); its last three runs on the current prompt shall gate the Keeper's automatic changes.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/MEMORY.md`, `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`, [DEC-44](#dec-44)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_trial_scores_the_automatic_changes_against_the_answer_key`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_nothing_is_changed_while_the_trial_has_not_passed_three_times_on_the_current_prompt`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `eval/trials/memory.yaml`

### MEM-21

**One Memory Keeper, by rules** · must · functional · **implemented**

One agent, the Memory Keeper, shall look after every memory store every night (its judgement parts on Wednesdays) by these rules: it deletes nothing (a fact is soft-retired, rewritten with its old text kept, or moved under Superseded); it writes every change with what was there, why and how to undo it, and the owner can undo each from its page; it corrects world knowledge only through a research task's sourced findings; it makes no automatic change to the owner's facts beyond retiring duplicates, test noise and agents' runs, translating into English and dating passing states, and dates the older when two of his facts disagree (DEC-44); it makes those changes only while its trial passes (MEM-20) and at most 40 a pass; and it says once a week what it changed.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-40](#dec-40), [DEC-44](#dec-44), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_each_change_is_logged_with_what_was_there_and_undone`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_nothing_is_changed_while_the_trial_has_not_passed_three_times_on_the_current_prompt`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_crew_saves_the_keeper_s_report_and_undoes_a_change_on_the_owner_s_tap`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `config/agents.yaml`, `n8n/workflows/memory-keeper.json`, `prompts/memory.md`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`, `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`

**One catalogue of memory collections** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall keep one catalogue of memory collections (public/memory-catalogue.json), written with the hourly inventory, that lists for each collection its id, sector, parent, the question it answers, size, last change, trust (exact, compiled or extracted), access class, writer and retrieval methods, and how many runs were given it in 7 days.

- Trace: `ops/memstack.py`, `ops/memory.py`

### MEM-23

**The memory's settings in one file** · should · functional · **implemented**

The budgets of each run kind, the presets by role, the ranking weights and the memory agent's limits shall live in config/memory.yaml; each run shall record the budget it used, and a trial arm shall be able to override a budget for its runs.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-44](#dec-44), `docs/MEMORY-STACK.md`

### MEM-24

**Subscriptions come from the card's roles** · must · functional · **implemented**

A run shall be subscribed to the collections config/memory.yaml presets for its card's roles, filled for its repository, its own wiki and itself; every card of config/agents.yaml that runs a model shall resolve to one subscription or more, so a new card is wired by its roles alone.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-44](#dec-44), `docs/MEMORY-STACK.md`

- Trace: `ops/memstack.py`, `config/memory.yaml`, `config/agents.yaml`

### MEM-25

**Every opening ends with the offer** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every opening shall end with the offer: the collections whose title or question match the task and that the run is not subscribed to, at most the configured number, each with its id and the question it answers, and how to subscribe to it.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-44](#dec-44), `docs/MEMORY-STACK.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memstack.py::test_the_opening_offers_matching_collections_it_did_not_give`
- Trace: `ops/memstack.py`

### MEM-26

**The opening is chosen for the task within its budget** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every run's opening shall be assembled by ops/memstack.py from its subscriptions: the items its run kind always needs first, then every other item ranked by its match to the task times its trust times its freshness times the share of its collection that earlier runs of the same kind used, placed in rank order while it fits the run kind's budget; the highest-ranked item that does not fit shall be cut to what is left when 800 characters or more are left, each cut naming its size and where the whole is, and every other item that does not fit shall be listed as a pointer with its ref.

- Sources: [DEC-44](#dec-44), `docs/MEMORY-STACK.md`

### MEM-30

**Each collection has an access class** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each collection shall carry an access class: all; private (a wiki built from the owner's mail, for its own mission); own (an agent's journal, for that agent and the memory agent); owner (long-term memory, for the assistant whole and for other agents as a task-matched recall within the owner budget). Search, open and the opening shall enforce it. The scout and the coders (a shell on others' code) shall get memorypublic, which reads the World without private wikis and filed sources, the Code and the How-to sectors only, and a coding opening shall hold no owner's facts. The class shall be the one the caller's server sets, never derived again from the agent the caller names; a mission's other files (results, notes) shall take its wiki's class, a vault file outside every sector shall be for the all class only, and when the private wikis cannot be read every mission and topic wiki shall count as private.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)

The stack shall have trials for the memory stack (ops/memtrials.py): coverage on a fixture (what must be given, pointed to, or never reach an agent), mission continuity on the live missions, and recall in a trial bank of long-term memory that starts empty each time, never the owner's; the memory agent shall run coverage and continuity every night and recall every Wednesday, and its structural changes (splits, archives, moves, skill proposals) shall run only while each trial's last result passed within 14 days.

### MEM-50

**A repeated fact with a new detail is merged** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a newer fact of the owner says the same as an older one and adds a detail, the Memory Keeper shall rewrite the older to hold both in one sentence, keeping every number and date of both and in English, and retire the newer; a merge into one fact shall happen once a pass, and each step shall be logged with its own undo.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-44](#dec-44), `docs/MEMORY-STACK.md`

- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `prompts/memory.md`, `eval/trials/memory.yaml`
- Rationale: Commissioning on 7 Nov 2026: the owner's voice-answer preference was held six times, each copy adding one detail, and the Keeper's rule "facts that add different details are not duplicates" kept them all.

### MEM-51

**A mission's working state is a section of its journal** · must · functional · **implemented**

A mission's working state (where the work stands, what is open, the next step) shall be the Working state section of its journal, replaced by each run, never a second file; an old NOTES.md shall move there at the mission's next run.

## MIS Missions

### MIS-01

**A mission is a file in the vault** · must · interface · **implemented**

Each mission shall be vault/missions/<slug>/MISSION.md with its type (briefing or task), status (active, paused, archived), schedule, triggers, depth, one-off flag and delivery channels, versioned with the vault.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_a_burst_of_locks_writes_who_held_what_once`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_a_write_that_fails_on_a_lock_gives_up_its_transaction`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_create_task_writes_mission_and_queues_first_run`
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`

### MIS-02

**Schedules** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall accept the schedules daily HH:MM, weekdays HH:MM, weekly <days> HH:MM, every Nh (1 to 24) and manual and refuse any other text; a mission's schedule shall become its task's n8n workflow (WFL-09), from which its next run is read.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_valid_schedule`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_schedule_text`, `tests/test_n8n_move.py::test_a_jobs_schedule_and_next_run_come_from_its_workflows_cron`, `tests/test_n8n_move.py::test_a_missions_schedule_goes_to_its_task_and_then_its_workflow_holds_it`
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`, `ops/missions.requests.py`, `ops/n8n_jobs.py`

### MIS-03

**A missed run catches up once** · must · functional · **deprecated**

When the PC was off at a scheduled time, the runner shall run the mission once when it is back (within 20 hours), never for a time before the mission was created, and not after a resume.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Reason: DEC-51: n8n starts every scheduled run since 10 Nov 2026 and the runner's own schedule check is removed; n8n's schedule trigger does not catch up a time the PC was off (WFL-09's gap).
- Superseded by: `W`, `F`, `L`, `-`, `0`, `9`

### MIS-04

**One run at a time** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall run missions one at a time from a queue, drop a duplicate queued run of the same mission but always queue event-triggered runs, and let a run that parks on a wait free the slot until it times out.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_one_runner_at_a_time`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_enqueue_dedup_but_events_always_queue`, `tests/test_queue.py::test_a_parked_run_frees_the_slot`, `tests/test_queue.py::test_park_times_out`
- Findings: [FND-29](#fnd-29)

### MIS-05

**Usage-limit pause** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a run fails because the subscription's usage limit is reached, the runner shall mark it waiting and retry it every 30 minutes, up to 16 times (8 hours), and never mark it failed for that reason. A usage limit is the gateway's or the engine's error (a 429, usage_limit_reached, the deployments in cooldown), never words in what a run wrote; a task's intake, a change's specification and a Conductor step wait for it the same way.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), `ops/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_a_run_a_restart_cut_off_says_why_and_waits_for_the_stack`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_paused`
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`

### MIS-06

**Creating a mission** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a mission is created by chat or form, the runner shall validate it, write its MISSION.md, give it a unique ASCII slug and queue its first run.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`

### MIS-07

**No duplicate missions from retries** · must · functional · **implemented**

If a mission with a similar title (word overlap at least 0.4) was created in the last 30 minutes, then the runner shall refuse the new one as a duplicate.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-27: Hermes retried a slow create and made a duplicate mission`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_create_refuses_a_duplicate_within_30_minutes`

### MIS-08

**Changing, pausing and archiving** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall let the owner or Hermes run, pause, resume, archive and update a mission (goal, questions, sources, schedule, delivery, depth), validating every field and recording the change in the mission's activity.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_update_validates_and_resume_skips_catch_up`

### MIS-09

**Event triggers fire once** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall check each mission's triggers (SEC filing, price move, news) every 10 minutes, take the first check as a baseline, and run the mission once for each new event.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/05-triggers.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_triggers.py::test_clean_triggers`, `tests/test_triggers.py::test_clean_triggers_rejects`, `tests/test_triggers.py::test_check_events_baseline_then_fire`

### MIS-10

**Triggers proven end to end** · should · quality · **implemented**

Each trigger kind shall have fired at least once end to end, or been replayed from a recorded event, with its alert delivered.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-13](#sn-13)

A briefing run shall have the Topic Desk collect the brief's news, filings and prices over the window its schedule gives (36 hours for a daily briefing, the time between runs plus 12 hours for a weekly one), search Google News in each query's own language, write a structured report with sources, file it under topics/<slug>/reports/YYYY/MM/, skip filing boilerplate, and commit it to the vault.

### MIS-23

**Results shows what you asked for, first** · must · functional · **implemented**

A mission's Results shall begin with the files the owner asked it to make. Each file shall show its name, words, checkboxes and size, whether it is final or a draft, how it was delivered, and Open, Download and Copy. A file that was asked for and not saved shall be shown as asked for, not delivered, with the reason and a way to run again. Drafts its runs left in the Ledger shall follow, then the run reports. The mission header, Home's result card and the chat card shall show the files too. Files shall live outside the vault's git, so that deleting one frees its space.

- Parents: [MIS-15](#mis-15), [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-06](#sn-06)

### MIS-24

**The mission saves the files it was asked for** · must · functional · **implemented**

The files the owner asks for shall be recorded with the mission when it is made, and can be changed in its Settings or by Hermes. The mission's agent shall be told them and shall save each one with file_save, either as text or from a Ledger report unchanged. The tool is on its own server, given only to the mission agent. Saving a file again shall keep the earlier version.

### MIS-25

**Files arrive as real attachments; a missing one makes the run partial** · must · functional · **implemented**

A run's delivery shall attach the files it saved: to the e-mail as attachments under their own names, and on Telegram as documents after the result's message. Each file shall record its own delivery. A run that ends without a file that was asked for shall be marked partial, and its notification and e-mail shall name the missing file.

### MIS-26

**Mission files are cleaned up by rules the owner sees** · must · functional · **implemented**

Deleting a file shall move it, with its versions, to a trash it can be restored from for 30 days. Every day a cleanup shall: - keep only the newest 5 versions of each file; - move to the trash the unpinned files of missions archived and untouched for 180 days; - empty what has been in the trash for 30 days. A Files page shall show every mission's files, the space they take against a limit, the trash and these rules, and offer Clean up now, which first shows what it would do. Past 80 % of the limit the owner shall get a notice.

- Parents: [MIS-23](#mis-23), [PLT-21](#plt-21)
- Sources: `docs/DELIVERABLES.md`

- Trace: `ops/files.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-tasks.js`

### MIS-27

**Hermes promises only what the stack can deliver** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner asks for a file the stack cannot make (Word, PDF, images, archives), Hermes shall say so in the chat and offer Markdown before any mission is made. mission_create shall refuse such a file. The tools' descriptions shall say what can be made and how it is delivered.

- Parents: [MIS-24](#mis-24), [AST-02](#ast-02)

### MIS-28

**A newsletter on a watched topic reaches that topic** · should · functional · **implemented**

While the owner grants Gmail read, a newsletter that names an active briefing's topic (the watchlist and searches of its TOPIC.md) and passes one check against its key questions shall go to that topic's folder and be read by its next report as untrusted data, with the mail named as a source line; through the report it reaches the topic's wiki. A mail without a List-Unsubscribe header, a protected mail and the stack's own briefings shall never go there, no link in a mail shall be opened, and a mail's text shall never decide what the desk fetches. The topic's page shall list each mail with what came of it: waiting for the next report, cited in a report, or read and not used.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: does everything in my copper newsletter reach the wiki and make the AI smarter?`, [FND-121](#fnd-121)

## OBS Observability

### OBS-01

**One record for every agent** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent run (Hermes profiles, Claude Code, the AI phone, Gmail, missions, builds, Claude tasks, crew jobs) shall report its events into one runtime record, open on its first event and stay closed after its end.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05), `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_run_opens_on_first_event_and_stays_closed_after_its_end`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_requests_that_come_together_each_read_their_own_runs`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_chat_sessions_are_tied_to_their_chat`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_hermes_runs_without_a_ref_are_tied_to_the_live_mission`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `ops/runtime.py`, `config/hermes/plugins/aistack-runtime/__init__.py`, `config/hermes/plugins/aistack-runtime/plugin.yaml`, `config/hermes/install-runtime-plugin.sh`, `config/hermes/claude-hooks/claude-runtime-settings.json`
- Findings: [FND-88](#fnd-88)

### OBS-02

**One honest end state** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall end in exactly one of done, failed, stopped, stalled or limit; an unknown end state shall become done and a malformed event shall be skipped.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_unknown_end_states_become_done_and_bad_events_are_skipped`

### OBS-03

**Silent runs are closed as stalled** · must · functional · **implemented**

The watchdog shall close a run that is silent for 20 minutes as stalled (3 hours for a declared wait), keep a parent alive while its children work, and let a late real end replace the watchdog's verdict.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_watchdog_closes_silent_runs_honestly`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_declared_wait_is_not_a_stall`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_children_show_under_their_parent_and_keep_it_alive`

### OBS-04

**Observers never block** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Hermes runtime plugin, the Claude hooks and the phone and mail emitters shall never block, veto or fail the action they observe, and shall spool events while Mission Control is unreachable.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `config/hermes/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_bad_event_or_a_refused_batch_jams_nothing_and_a_spool_link_is_not_followed`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_plugin_records_a_turn_and_spools_without_mission_control`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_claude_hook_turns_hooks_and_transcript_into_events`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_forwarder_sends_spools_and_keeps_what_failed`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_the_mail_tools_survive_a_log_they_cannot_write`

### OBS-05

**What it does and what it touches** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every run's view shall show its steps in order, and what it read, changed, was refused and saw only partly, with the tool that did it, across both engines.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_files_read_and_changed_across_engines`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_touches_refusals_connections_and_retention`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_pipeline_steps_reads_writes_and_model_calls_go_to_the_current_run`
- Refined by: [SWM-78](#swm-78)

### OBS-06

**Statistics and stages** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every run shall show per-tool calls, errors and durations, model latency (p50, p95) and cache share, and its stages in one schema.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_stats_pair_calls_with_results`, `tests/test_observe.py::test_stages_use_one_schema`, `tests/test_telemetry.py::test_timeline_merges_sources_and_warns`

### OBS-07

**Problems by fixed rules** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each finished run shall be checked by deterministic rules (unknown tools, empty arguments to a tool that needs arguments, repeated calls, tool errors, results cut off and not read on before the answer, no clean end, usage-limit stalls) without a second model; the list of tools whose arguments are all optional shall match every tool in the repo, and the deploy's smoke-test refusals shall not count as problems.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`

**The health board** · must · functional · **implemented**

Mission Control shall keep one health item per agent (missions, crew jobs, engines), shown on the task's row and under Tasks → Team, raising alerts for failing streaks (naming the last run's reason), missed schedules, long usage-limit blocks, stalls and lost tools and notes for single failures and anomalies, rolling sessions up into the agent that started them and clearing each kind of issue with the agent's next finished run that does not have it.

**Told once** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall send each new alert once by push and Telegram, again after a day if it is still open, and never send notes. An alert that leaves the board for less than 6 hours shall count as the same alert when it comes back (a deploy hides the run record for a moment), and Hermes shall send no restart notices of its own.

**Acknowledge hides until it happens again** · should · functional · **implemented**

When the owner acknowledges an issue, the board shall hide it until it happens again and record the acknowledgement in the activity feed.

**Results per agent** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each agent's page shall show, under Results, what it delivered first (each result, newest first, opening it; for a mission run as a task, a link to the task's runs) and then what it took (runs, success, time, model calls, tokens, cost, wiki pages; for proposing agents the proposals and their fate).

Every notification the stack sends (push, Telegram) shall go through one path that applies the same once-per-issue rule, the owner's preferences per kind of message and channel, and quiet hours in which only approvals go out and Telegram messages wait to be sent as one when the quiet hours end; each decision, and each report e-mail sent or failed, shall be logged and shown in the app.

The Stack page shall show, for ChatGPT and Claude, the tokens and calls of the last 5 hours (ChatGPT's usage window) and 24 hours, the agents that used most, and whether the subscription is at its usage limit now and until when, so that the owner sees a limit coming before work waits for it.

A scout run that answers a research call shall be recorded under the run whose call asked that question (matched by the question), not under whichever run used the scout at the same time, so that a task's agents, sources, tokens and cost hold only its own work.

## PHN AI phone

### PHN-01

**What asks the owner waits for the answer** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Before anything on the AI phone that needs the owner's OK (a job, PHN-26; money, a setting, deleting), the AI phone service shall wait up to 15 minutes for the answer and treat no answer as no; looking at the screen, going back and going home need no approval.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_an_action_waits_for_the_owner`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`
- Findings: [FND-13](#fnd-13)

### PHN-02

**A time window the owner opens** · must · functional · **implemented**

Where the owner opened a time window (minutes, or until he stops it), the AI phone service shall run ordinary actions without asking until it ends, still logging each one; spending money, settings and deletions shall ask each time, and typing or a web address only when it holds private data (DEC-38, PHN-19).

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_in_free_mode_only_an_address_or_text_with_private_data_asks`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_allow_for_a_few_minutes`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_allowance_any_length_or_until_stopped`
- Findings: [FND-02](#fnd-02), [FND-13](#fnd-13)

### PHN-03

**Money and destructive steps always ask** · must · constraint · **implemented**

An action that would buy, pay, order or subscribe, uninstall an app, delete files or trim caches, or change a phone setting shall need its own approval every time, even inside a time window, and that approval shall open no window.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_money_buttons_always_ask_even_in_a_time_window`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_uninstall_always_asks_even_inside_the_time_window`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_delete_always_asks_and_never_blocked_or_everything`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_start_in_ai_mode_asks_once_for_all_that_is_missing`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_only_a_plain_path_on_shared_storage_is_offered_for_deletion`

### PHN-04

**Blocked apps are never touched** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall check the block list (banking, investment, payment, wallet, password, authenticator, Play Store, Settings) before and after every action and before any screen read, and go to the home screen when a blocked app is in front.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_block_list_covers_money_passwords_and_settings`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_blocked_app_and_stop_refuse_everything`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_url_block_list_and_done_summary`

### PHN-05

**Stop ends it at once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner presses Stop, the AI phone service shall disconnect, refuse every action and approval, and end any time window until he switches it on again.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_blocked_app_and_stop_refuse_everything`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_stop_refuses_approvals`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_stop_ends_free_mode_and_an_approved_task_even_when_disconnected`

### PHN-06

**A secure lock is never entered** · must · constraint · **implemented**

If the AI phone has a PIN, pattern or password lock, then the AI phone service shall stop and never enter it; a swipe lock may be dismissed.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_pin_locked_phone_is_never_unlocked`

### PHN-07

**The AI phone cannot approve itself** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Mission Control shall refuse approvals, time windows, grants, purchases and cleanup approvals that come from the AI phone's own address; the AI phone may only press Stop.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_the_ai_phone_is_known_by_the_address_the_proxy_wrote`, `tests/test_server.py::test_the_ai_phone_cannot_approve_its_own_actions`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approvals_are_refused_from_the_ai_phone`, `tests/test_server.py::test_the_ai_phone_can_ask_the_app_for_nothing_but_stop`

### PHN-08

**The phone service reaches one phone only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service's firewall shall allow traffic only to the AI phone's tailnet address, no internet and no home network.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-12](#cst-12)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_egress_ipv6.py::test_an_ipv6_rule_that_cannot_be_set_stops_a_container_that_has_ipv6`
- Trace: `images/phone/entrypoint.sh`, `images/mail/entrypoint.sh`
- Gap: The test covers the IPv6 rules failing closed (review CNX-R13); the IPv4 rules are checked by reading images/phone/entrypoint.sh, and the mail connector's identical IPv6 rules (CNX-05) by the same test.

### PHN-09

**An offline phone is not scanned** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the AI phone does not answer, then the AI phone service shall not scan its ports and shall retry with a back-off of up to 10 minutes.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-29: 11,000 hanging connections exhausted rootless Docker's network`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_an_offline_phone_is_not_port_scanned`

### PHN-10

**Reconnects after a restart** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the AI phone service restarts, it shall reconnect to a paired phone within a minute without the owner's action.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/phone/phone.py`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_a_paired_phone_reconnects_after_a_restart`

### PHN-11

**No approval card is left unanswerable** · must · functional · **implemented**

If an approval card is left behind by a restart, then the AI phone service shall drop it, log why and ask again where the work was mid-way (Starts in AI mode), and Mission Control shall tell the owner when an answer was not taken.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29: Allow gave no feedback`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_cards_left_by_a_restart_are_dropped_and_start_in_ai_mode_asks_again`, `tests/test_server.py::test_the_ai_phone_cannot_approve_its_own_actions`

### PHN-12

**The approval card answers at once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner taps Allow or Decline, the card shall change at once to what happens next and disappear when the phone is done.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### PHN-13

**The screen sleeps** · must · functional · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall let the screen sleep after 30 seconds when idle and not keep it on while charging, and wake it before each action (while the agents work it stays on, PHN-17).

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-08](#dec-08)
- Verified by: inspection: `images/phone/phone.py`
- Findings: [FND-01](#fnd-01)

### PHN-14

**Starts in AI mode after a restart** · should · functional · **implemented**

The AI phone shall come back to AI mode after a reboot (wireless debugging on through KeepADB, Tailscale as always-on VPN, both exempt from battery saving), applied only after the owner's one Allow.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-13](#dec-13)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_start_in_ai_mode_asks_once_for_all_that_is_missing`

### PHN-15

**Deletes and uninstalls stay inside safe limits** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall never uninstall blocked or system apps and shall delete only at allowed paths, optionally only files older than a given age, showing files, size and dates in the approval.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_uninstall_refuses_blocked_and_system_apps`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_old_personal_data_is_offered_for_deletion_by_age`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_delete_always_asks_and_never_blocked_or_everything`

### PHN-16

**Screenshots go to the owner** · should · constraint · **implemented**

Screenshots shall go to the owner's approval card and log; a model shall get one only when it asks with phone_look(picture=true).

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/phone/phone.py`

### PHN-17

**The owner sees the AI phone work; idle, its screen is off** · should · functional · **implemented**

While the agents act on the AI phone, its screen shall stay on until two minutes after the last action and while an action waits for the owner's OK (up to 15 minutes); then it shall go off at once with the 30 s timeout back. The Phone page shall show the screen's state and let the owner keep it on to watch (up to an hour) or turn it off.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_the_screen_stays_on_while_working_or_waiting_and_goes_off_when_idle`, `tests/test_server.py::test_the_owner_keeps_the_phone_screen_on_for_an_hour_at_most`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-devices.js`, `images/mission-control/server.devices.py`

### PHN-18

**One OK for a whole phone task** · must · functional · **implemented**

An agent shall ask the owner once for a phone task, naming its apps, the exact texts it will type and how long (at most an hour); after one Allow, taps, swipes and keys in those apps and on the home screen, opening them and typing those texts shall run without asking, counted on the task; anything else shall be refused until the agent asks for the wider job (PHN-26), money always asks; the task shall end with phone_done (its summary says how many actions ran) or the owner's End task.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `requirements/decisions.yaml`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_one_ok_covers_a_whole_task_and_money_still_asks`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-devices.js`

### PHN-19

**Free mode asks only for private data** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Inside the owner's time window (free mode), typing and opening an app or a web address shall run without asking unless the text or address holds an e-mail address, a phone number, an IBAN, a key or an IP address.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `requirements/decisions.yaml`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_free_mode_types_freely_but_private_text_asks`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_in_free_mode_only_an_address_or_text_with_private_data_asks`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`

### PHN-20

**A waiting request goes ahead when the permission comes** · should · functional · **implemented**

A request waiting for the owner's OK shall run as soon as the owner opens free mode or approves a task that covers it, without a second tap.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `requirements/decisions.yaml`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_waiting_request_goes_ahead_when_the_owner_opens_free_mode`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`

### PHN-21

**The owner's permission outlives a restart** · should · functional · **implemented**

The owner's free mode and an approved task shall stay in force across a restart of the AI phone service (every deploy) until they run out, unless the owner pressed Stop.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `requirements/decisions.yaml`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_free_mode_and_a_task_outlive_a_restart_but_not_stop`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`

### PHN-22

**The AI phone becomes Hermes' speaker after one Allow** · should · functional · **implemented**

On the owner's request (the House page), the AI phone service shall make Home Assistant the phone's default digital assistant, grant it the microphone and exempt it from battery saving (so it hears its wake word when locked), all on one card that only the owner's Allow applies (never free mode or a task), report whether both are in place, and ask nothing once they are.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_home_assistant_becomes_the_phone_s_voice_assistant_after_one_allow`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### PHN-23

**An app opens fresh when a blocked screen sits on top of it** · should · functional · **implemented**

When opening an app brings up a blocked screen that app had opened (Android's settings), the AI phone service shall open the app fresh at its start screen instead.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_an_app_opens_fresh_when_a_blocked_screen_sits_on_top_of_it`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`

### PHN-24

**An approved cleanup step says so, can be undone, and waits for the phone** · should · functional · **implemented**

An approved cleanup step shall show as approved with an Undo until the AI phone starts it, then as running, then its result. While the phone is not connected the app shall say so, and approved steps shall wait for it up to 4 hours instead of failing; Approve all shall also cover failed steps that were shown.

### PHN-25

**The pairing does not lapse** · must · functional · **implemented**

Starting in AI mode shall include switching off Android's limit that drops a paired computer after 7 days, on the owner's Allow; and when the phone answers but refuses the connection, the app shall name a lapsed pairing and offer to pair again.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-02](#sn-02)

### PHN-26

**The owner approves whole jobs, not single steps** · must · functional · **implemented**

A tap, swipe, key, typing or opening an app or address on the AI phone shall run only inside a job the owner approved or in free mode. Otherwise it shall be refused without a card, telling the agent to ask once for the whole job. A step outside the job shall be refused the same way. Money, phone settings, deleting and private text in free mode keep their own card, and the set-up for starting in AI mode takes one Allow.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: I also do not want to allow every singel action on AI Phone. I want to allow more macro.`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_single_step_gets_no_card_and_tells_the_agent_to_ask_for_the_job`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_job_covers_its_steps_and_a_step_outside_it_is_refused_not_carded`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_money_and_private_text_in_free_mode_keep_their_own_card`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_start_in_ai_mode_asks_once_for_all_that_is_missing`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_setting_goes_ahead_when_the_owner_is_still_in_settings`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`, `prompts/main.md`, `ops/missions.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`

### PHN-27

**The AI phone's page is easy to find** · should · functional · **implemented**

The AI phone's page shall be one tap away from the top of Home, Agents and Stack and from its cleanup steps, shall be found by searching for pairing or wireless debugging, and Home shall list a long disconnection under what needs the owner.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: Where are these apps Phone page? No idea how to find which is itself a big issue. Phone page needs to be easier to find.`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_the_phone_page_is_one_tap_from_home_agents_stack_and_search`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/tools.json`

## PLT Platform

### PLT-01

**Every service has limits and rotated logs** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every compose service shall have a mem_limit and the shared json-file logging block (10 MB, 3 files).

- Parents: [CST-01](#cst-01), [CST-14](#cst-14)
- Sources: `compose/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `ops/ci-compose-check.sh`
- Trace: `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`

### PLT-02

**Least network per service** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Each compose service shall join only the networks it needs; internal networks shall be internal, and only services that call a provider, a registry proxy or the web reader networks shall reach the internet.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: `compose/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `compose/apps.yaml`, `compose/core.yaml`

### PLT-03

**No Docker socket in containers** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No container shall mount the Docker socket, and no service shall run privileged.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `compose/AGENTS.md`, `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `.semgrep.yml`, `ops/semgrep_selftest.py`, `tests/semgrep`

### PLT-04

**Services come back by themselves** · must · quality · **implemented**

When a service crashes, the PC reboots or WSL restarts, the stack shall restart its services and timers without the owner's action.

- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/12-stack-page.md`

A Windows task shall check the WSL disk at logon and every 15 minutes and repair file-system errors without administrator rights.

### PLT-26

**What nobody uses is found and offered for retirement** · should · functional · **partial**

The app shall count which screens the owner opens (route, day, count, nothing else; the AI phone and the stack's own browsers not counted), and from 30 days of data a monthly report shall list agents with no run, screens with no visit and services with no call, each offered to the owner for retirement with what it costs and what goes with it.
