### PLT-27

**The stack keeps a memory budget** · must · constraint · **partial**

WSL shall have a swap of 16 GB on an SSD with room for it, so the memory of idle services moves to disk instead of crowding the work (the stack waits on model calls, not on memory); the services that grow while idle (qmd, Phoenix, Open WebUI) shall be restarted each night while the stack is idle, with their memory before and after logged; each container shall have a memory budget the doctor checks; and services only trials and builds use shall start on demand and stop when idle.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: you need to make due with small ram ... it forces us to optimize`, `chat 2026-11-02: Can we not use ssd memory as ram? ... We could make space in ssd and give generous capa. ... Do both.`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recycle.py::test_busy_names_what_runs_and_ignores_a_stale_task`, `tests/test_recycle.py::test_an_idle_night_restarts_the_three_and_logs_memory_before_and_after`, `tests/test_recycle.py::test_a_busy_hour_skips_the_night_without_restarting`
- Trace: `ops/recycle.py`, `n8n/workflows/crew-nightly-restart.json`, `ops/systemd/recycle.service`, `ops/windows/host-setup.ps1`
- Gap: The doctor's check of each container against its budget, and on-demand services for trials and builds.
- Rationale: On 2 Nov the 3 GB swap was full, held mostly by idle qmd, Hindsight, Open WebUI and Phoenix; Open WebUI was back at 655 MB an hour after a restart.

### PLT-28

**Nothing starts against a stack that is not up** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner and every scheduled job shall first ask whether the stack is up (the boot check done, Docker answering, the services every job leans on running and not paused, the gateway live). Until it is, the runner shall start nothing and keep queued runs queued; a job shall wait or be skipped, not fail; a run the stack went away under shall wait and run again; and the kept timers' jobs the PC slept through (the backup, the restore drill) shall catch up one after the other. No health alert shall go out during a start or while the stack is down, and the hours the PC was off shall not make a job overdue. If the stack stays down for 10 minutes, one message shall name the cause, and one shall say when it is back.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: I restarted the PC and we had a lot of failures ... When I start PC everything should go online and working again. No failures.`, `docs/PROBES.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_gate_opens_only_when_docker_the_services_and_the_gateway_are_up`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_backup_waits_only_for_its_databases`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_gate_stays_shut_until_the_boot_check_is_done_and_opens_by_itself_later`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_wait_returns_when_the_stack_comes_up_gives_up_after_its_time_and_stops_on_request`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_cli_exits_1_when_the_stack_is_down_so_systemd_skips_the_job`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_every_scheduled_job_waits_for_the_stack_except_the_job_that_brings_it_up`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_each_job_s_start_timeout_has_room_for_its_slot_and_the_wait`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_jobs_that_run_every_few_minutes_skip_at_once_and_catch_ups_each_get_their_own_slot`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_run_the_gate_skipped_is_no_run_and_its_wait_is_not_its_run_time`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_hours_the_pc_was_off_do_not_make_a_job_overdue`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_no_health_alert_while_the_stack_starts_or_is_down`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_skipped_job_does_not_show_as_failed`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_job_waiting_its_turn_is_not_charged_with_its_last_run_s_failure`, `tests/test_journeys.py::test_a_tool_check_needs_two_timeouts_to_call_a_tool_broken`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_run_the_stack_went_away_under_waits_and_runs_again_and_alarms_nobody`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_real_failure_is_still_a_failure_while_the_stack_is_up`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_log_that_names_a_paused_container_counts_as_the_stack_s_doing`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_queued_runs_stay_queued_while_the_stack_is_down`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_owner_hears_once_after_ten_minutes_and_once_when_it_is_back`
- Trace: `ops/stack_gate.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/observe.py`, `ops/recycle.py`, `images/mission-control/obs_core.py`, `ops/systemd/AGENTS.md`
- Rationale: FND-119. On 4 Nov Docker stayed down for three hours; four missions, the backup and 70 runs of scheduled jobs failed against it, and the alerts named each symptom but never the cause.
- Findings: [FND-119](#fnd-119)

### PLT-29

**Docker never gives up starting** · must · quality · **implemented**

Docker shall keep trying to start for as long as it takes, without a limit on attempts, and shall read what containerd needs into memory before each try.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: When I start PC everything should go online and working again.`, `docs/PROBES.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_boot.py::test_docker_never_gives_up_and_warms_what_containerd_needs`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_a_deploy_installs_the_drop_in_for_docker_and_a_new_machine_too`
- Trace: `ops/systemd/docker-boot.conf`, `ops/deploy.py`, `ops/new-machine.sh`, `ops/bootstrap-wsl-2-user.sh`
- Rationale: FND-119. Docker allows containerd 15 seconds and that cannot be configured; systemd's default limit of 3 starts a minute made three slow starts final.
- Findings: [FND-119](#fnd-119)

### PLT-30

**Each start of the PC reports how it went** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each boot shall leave one line in /srv/ai/state/boots.jsonl (when, how long until online, Docker's tries, services running, tools missing), shown at the top of the Stack page, and send the owner one message saying the stack is online or what is missing. The gate shall open even when something is missing, so the jobs do not wait for ever.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: When I start PC everything should go online and working again.`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_first_check_of_a_boot_restarts_hermes_opens_the_gate_logs_it_and_says_so_once`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_missing_tools_still_open_the_gate_say_what_is_missing_and_fail_the_unit`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_check_waits_only_for_tools_the_main_key_is_granted`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_set_up_jobs_that_ran_once_are_not_counted_as_down`, `tests/test_boot.py::test_the_message_names_services_that_did_not_come_up_and_a_slow_docker`
- Trace: `ops/stack_ready.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-119](#fnd-119)

### PLT-31

**The stack asks Windows through a desk it cannot command** · should · constraint · **implemented**

A script on Windows, started at logon without admin rights, shall publish the PC's status and fetch questions the stack leaves in a folder; it shall treat a request as data and run nothing from it. A question shall go to Claude Code with reading tools only, inside folders listed in a file on Windows, never key or password files. Only a turn of the owner's own chat shall be able to ask.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-43](#dec-43)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_windows_desk.py::test_a_question_is_left_in_the_folder_and_its_answer_published`, `tests/test_windows_desk.py::test_a_silent_desk_is_said_not_waited_for`, `tests/test_windows_desk.py::test_only_your_own_chat_can_ask`, `tests/test_windows_desk.py::test_the_desk_gives_claude_reading_tools_only_and_runs_nothing_from_a_request`, `tests/test_windows_desk.py::test_a_use_of_windows_is_a_line_on_the_map`
- Trace: `ops/windows/desk.ps1`, `ops/windows/desk-common.ps1`, `ops/windows/desk-settings.json`, `ops/windows/desk-setup.ps1`, `ops/windows/desk-signin.ps1`, `ops/windows_desk.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/server.mcp.py`
- Rationale: Interop stays off and no port opens, so nothing in WSL can start anything on Windows, where the owner's logins are.

### PLT-32

**Remote Control runs from logon on Windows** · should · functional · **implemented**

From logon, one `claude remote-control` shall run on Windows under the owner's user in the first allowed folder, started again if it ends with growing waits, with no permission flags and its connection link never logged.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-06: I always want to start claude with remote control on reboot.`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_windows_desk.py::test_remote_control_keeps_one_server_and_passes_no_permission_flags`
- Trace: `ops/windows/claude-remote.ps1`, `ops/windows/desk-setup.ps1`
- Rationale: The desktop app starts at logon, but its old sessions stay offline after a restart until a new message runs in them.

### PLT-33

**This deployment's hosts, paths and accounts live in one file** · should · constraint · **implemented**

The addresses of the NAS, the PC and the AI phone, the Forgejo address and owner, the Home Assistant and app addresses, the home, stack, code and skills folders, the Docker socket, and the git and Windows account names shall be set once, in deployments/home/deployment.env. Compose shall read that file as an env file and hand a container what it needs through its environment, host-side Python, shell and Make shall read it through ops/deployment.py, and a variable of the same name in the environment shall win over the file. A test shall fail when one of these values is written out in running code outside its named allowlist.

- Parents: [SN-09](#sn-09), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `review 2026-11-06: the NAS address, the code folder and the Forgejo owner counted 27 times across 18 files`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_deployment.py::test_no_converted_value_is_hard_coded_in_running_code`, `tests/test_deployment.py::test_the_guard_finds_a_value_written_out_and_respects_its_allowlist`, `tests/test_deployment.py::test_a_line_may_name_a_line_above_and_the_environment_wins`, `tests/test_deployment.py::test_shell_and_python_read_the_same_values_and_the_environment_wins_in_the_shell_too`, `tests/test_deployment.py::test_every_compose_call_reads_the_file_and_a_changed_file_is_applied`, `tests/test_deployment.py::test_the_page_carries_the_apps_addresses_and_the_tools_name_only_those`, `tests/test_deployment.py::test_units_name_the_home_and_docker_by_specifier`
- Trace: `deployments/home/deployment.env`, `ops/deployment.py`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `Makefile`, `ops/deploy.py`, `ops/ci-compose-check.sh`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/index.html`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/tools.json`, `ops/systemd/missions.service`
- Rationale: A move to another machine or address then means one file, and the public snapshot's rewrite (docs/PUBLISHING.md) works on it as on any other file.

### PLT-34

**The Bitbucket mirror says when it falls behind** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Windows task ai-stack bitbucket sync shall compare Forgejo's main, fetched on each run, with the main Bitbucket last took, push the difference fast-forward only, and end with exit 1 and a log line on every failure (fetch, bundle, copy, a non-fast-forward or the push), so a failed push is tried again on the next run.

- Parents: [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/windows/bitbucket-sync.ps1`
- Trace: `ops/windows/bitbucket-sync.ps1`
- Rationale: From 25 Oct 2026 the push failed for want of a stored Bitbucket credential; the local clone was already fast-forwarded, so every later run saw nothing to do and the task reported success (review OPS-R3).

### PLT-35

**Calls into WSL always get their channel** · must · constraint · **implemented**

WSL's kernel shall keep whole blocks of memory free (a larger free reserve, earlier reclaim, proactive compaction) and hand its file cache back to Windows every 30 minutes, set once as root and kept across restarts, so a call from Windows into WSL never fails because its Hyper-V socket could not get 512 KB of contiguous memory; a new machine gets the settings from the root bootstrap.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `owner 2026-11-09: Can you stop wsl from timing out all the time? There has to be a fix possible?`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_wsl_memory.py::test_the_settings_are_written_to_sysctl_d_and_applied_at_once`, `tests/test_wsl_memory.py::test_it_refuses_to_run_without_root_and_says_how`, `tests/test_wsl_memory.py::test_the_file_cache_goes_back_to_windows_every_30_minutes`, `tests/test_wsl_memory.py::test_a_new_machine_gets_it_from_the_root_bootstrap`
- Acceptance: Test: the script writes the three settings to sysctl.d, applies them and compacts memory at once, refuses to run without root, and the root bootstrap runs it.
- Trace: `ops/wsl-memory.sh`, `ops/bootstrap-wsl-1-root.sh`
- Rationale: 9 Nov 2026: calls into WSL timed out again and again (WSAETIMEDOUT); the kernel log showed order-7 page allocation failures in vmbus_alloc_ring and UtilAcceptVsock accept4 failed 110, with 6 GB swapped and Windows at 0.4 GB free.

### PLT-36

**A full Windows disk never stops the stack unannounced** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every 20 s the runner shall read the free space on Windows C:, which holds WSL's whole disk; below 10 GB it shall prune the unused Docker build cache (at most once an hour, never images in use, containers or volumes) and tell the owner once per episode, and below 5 GB it shall alert the owner urgently, at most once an hour.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `owner 2026-11-10: Cant reach stack from mobile`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_diskguard.py::test_low_disk_prunes_the_build_cache_and_tells_the_owner_once`, `tests/test_diskguard.py::test_nearly_full_alerts_urgently_at_most_once_an_hour`, `tests/test_diskguard.py::test_enough_space_or_no_reading_does_nothing_and_recovery_ends_the_episode`, `tests/test_diskguard.py::test_the_runner_checks_every_reading_of_the_host_probe`
- Acceptance: Test: a low reading prunes the build cache and dangling images and tells the owner once; a nearly full one also alerts urgently, paced to once an hour; enough space or no reading does nothing; recovery above 13 GB ends the episode; the runner checks every host reading. (10 Nov 2026: 20 GB wiped the cache every hour while C: sat near 14 GB, so every image build started from nothing.)
- Trace: `ops/diskguard.py`, `ops/missions.py`
- Rationale: 10 Nov 2026, 03:36: a night of image builds grew the Docker build cache to 52 GB and the WSL disk file to 135 GB; C: reached 0.2 GB free, WSL stopped and would not start (E_FAIL), and the owner's phone got HTTP 501. The Stack page showed C:'s free space, but nothing acted on it.

### PLT-37

**The runner has its own pinned Python environment** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The host runner shall run in a Python environment of its own, outside the system Python, made by uv (pinned by version and checksum, no sudo) from a lock with a hash for every package it pulls in, none released less than 14 days before the lock was written, and made again only when the lock changes; the tests shall use the same pins.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-52](#dec-52)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner_env.py::test_the_runner_environment_is_pinned_checksummed_and_follows_its_lock`
- Acceptance: Test: uv is pinned by version and checksum, the lock holds the pins and every package they pull in (atlassian-python-api for the Jira tracker among them) with their hashes, the lock is written without releases younger than 14 days, the tests use the same pins, and the runner unit runs in that environment. Shown on 10 Nov 2026: an environment made from the lock with hashes required imports atlassian's Jira client.
- Trace: `ops/runner_env.sh`, `ops/runner-requirements.txt`, `ops/runner-requirements.lock`, `tests/requirements.txt`, `ops/systemd/missions.service`

## QA Quality and process

### QA-01

**One check script for everyone** · must · process · **implemented**

CI, the builder and make check shall run the same ops/check.sh, and a check shall exist only there.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `.forgejo/workflows/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/check.sh`, `.forgejo/workflows/ci.yml`

### QA-02

**Every directory explains itself** · must · process · **implemented**

Every directory with files shall have an AGENTS.md (the vault template a README.md) listed in the root map, and CLAUDE.md shall import the root AGENTS.md.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repo_docs.py::test_every_directory_has_a_guide`, `tests/test_repo_docs.py::test_root_map_links_every_guide`, `tests/test_repo_docs.py::test_claude_entry_point_imports_agents_md`

### QA-03

**Scripts are executable and clean** · must · process · **implemented**

Every shell script shall be executable in git and pass shellcheck at error level.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `ops/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repo_docs.py::test_every_shell_script_is_executable`

### QA-04

**Unit tests fake everything external** · must · process · **implemented**

The unit tests shall use no Docker, gateway, Hermes, vault, network or secrets, write only under a temporary directory and never sleep, and the whole suite shall finish within 120 seconds in the CI image.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `tests/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `tests/AGENTS.md`, `tests/conftest.py`
- Findings: [FND-15](#fnd-15), [FND-27](#fnd-27)

### QA-05

**New behaviour has a test** · must · process · **implemented**

Every change that adds or changes behaviour shall add or change a unit test in the file for its module.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `AGENTS.md`

### QA-06

**Docs change with the code** · must · process · **implemented**

A change shall update, in the same commit, the AGENTS.md of the directories whose structure or rules changed, the guide when users see it, docs/ when architecture or security changed, and README.md when the component list changed.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `README.md`, `compose/AGENTS.md`, `tests/AGENTS.md`, `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Findings: [FND-09](#fnd-09), [FND-15](#fnd-15), [FND-16](#fnd-16)

### QA-07

**Model behaviour changes are gated** · must · process · **implemented**

Before a change to prompts, profiles, models or the curator ships, the frozen benchmark's gate subset shall be run and shall not score worse.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `eval/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `eval/bench.py`, `eval/AGENTS.md`

### QA-08

**Requirements are checked on every change** · must · process · **implemented**

The checks shall refuse the requirements when a record misses a required field, has an unknown status, type, priority or verification method, duplicates an id, points at a parent, source, finding, file or test that does not exist, uses a vague word in its statement, or when a stakeholder need is refined by no requirement.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14), `requirements/README.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_requirements.py::test_the_repo_requirements_pass_the_checker`, `tests/test_requirements.py::test_missing_fields_and_unknown_values_are_refused`, `tests/test_requirements.py::test_dangling_references_are_refused`, `tests/test_requirements.py::test_vague_statements_are_refused`, `tests/test_requirements.py::test_vague_words_are_known`, `tests/test_requirements.py::test_status_needs_its_evidence`
- Trace: `ops/req.py`, `ops/check.sh`

### QA-09

**Every test is claimed by a requirement** · must · process · **implemented**

The checks shall refuse a unit test that no requirement claims in its verification, and requirements/unmapped-tests.txt shall only shrink.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_requirements.py::test_an_unclaimed_test_is_refused_and_the_baseline_only_shrinks`
- Trace: `ops/req.py`

### QA-10

**The readable rendering is never stale** · must · process · **implemented**

requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md shall be generated from the YAML files, and the checks shall refuse it when it differs from a fresh rendering.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_requirements.py::test_the_rendering_is_current_and_complete`
- Trace: `ops/req.py`, `requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md`

### QA-11

**Changes name their requirements** · must · process · **implemented**

A commit that adds or changes behaviour shall update its requirement records in the same commit and name their ids in the commit body ("Requirements:").

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14)
- Verified by: inspection: `AGENTS.md`, `requirements/AGENTS.md`

### QA-12

**Contradictions are written down** · must · process · **implemented**

When a contradiction or incoherence between parts of the stack is found, it shall be recorded as a finding with evidence and a recommendation, and linked from every requirement it affects, before or instead of being resolved silently.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_requirements.py::test_status_needs_its_evidence`
- Trace: `requirements/findings.yaml`

### QA-13

**What the docs say is checked** · should · process · **implemented**

The checks shall compare the guide's and the AGENTS files' lists that mirror the configuration (tool servers, services, step harnesses, build engines) with the configuration itself.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_docs.py::test_what_the_docs_list_matches_the_configuration`
- Trace: `compose/AGENTS.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/20-tasks.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`
- Rationale: The coverage tests check that a feature is mentioned, not that what is said about it is true; most findings of 2026-10-29 are this kind of drift.
- Findings: [FND-09](#fnd-09), [FND-15](#fnd-15), [FND-16](#fnd-16)

### QA-14

**The stack checks itself against its requirements** · could · process · **proposed**

Every week an agent shall check the code against these requirements with the spec pipeline (link and judge, spec/), report requirements it finds contradicted, and propose a finding or a fix for each.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `spec/README.md`
- Verified by: -
- Trace: `spec/ingest.py`, `spec/extract.py`, `spec/link.py`, `spec/judge.py`, `spec/pilot/`
- Rationale: The spec pilot already links requirements to code and judges them (contradicted reliable on MQTT); its own requirements are the obvious next corpus.

### QA-15

**Planned requirements say how they will be accepted** · must · process · **implemented**

The checks shall refuse a planned requirement without an acceptance criterion, and a requirement whose phase is not one of its area's phases.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-21](#dec-21), `requirements/README.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_requirements.py::test_planned_needs_acceptance_and_a_known_phase`, `tests/test_requirements.py::test_groups_and_phases_are_rendered`
- Trace: `ops/req.py`
- Rationale: ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 asks that every requirement be verifiable; for a requirement not built yet, the acceptance criterion is that verification, written before the code (acceptance-test-driven development).

### QA-16

**Every text cap names its reason and shows itself** · must · process · **implemented**

Every place in the code that cuts text (a slice of 20 or more or by an UPPERCASE constant in Python or JS, a textcap helper, an ellipsis or line clamp, a shell head, tail or cut, a SQL limit of 2 rows or more) shall name its one reason on its line (prompt, untrusted, api, id, parse, store, preview, list or rotate); a cut with a reason someone reads (prompt, untrusted, store, preview) shall carry a visible marker; and the checks shall fail on one that does not.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: [DEC-27](#dec-27), `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_truncation.py::test_every_cap_names_its_reason_and_an_unexplained_one_fails_the_check`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_the_image_copies_of_textcap_are_the_same_file`
- Trace: `ops/truncation.py`, `ops/check.sh`, `images/mission-control/textcap.py`, `images/swarm/textcap.py`, `images/browser/textcap.py`, `images/desk/textcap.py`, `images/mail/textcap.py`, `images/phone/textcap.py`, `images/research/textcap.py`
- Findings: [FND-38](#fnd-38), [FND-105](#fnd-105), [FND-109](#fnd-109)

### QA-21

**A model's answer cut at its output limit is not used** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every model call shall read its answer's stop reason, and an answer that stopped at the model's output limit (finish_reason length) or a Claude Code run that stopped at its turn limit shall not be parsed, stored or delivered as a whole answer; the chat shall say so under such an answer.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: [DEC-27](#dec-27), `docs/TRUNCATION-AUDIT.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_task_s_name_is_its_first_sentence_whole_and_never_cut`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_a_cut_within_an_outside_limit_lists_and_model_answers_show_what_they_left`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_every_cap_names_its_reason_and_an_unexplained_one_fails_the_check`
- Trace: `ops/textcap.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `ops/claude_tasks.py`, `ops/truncation.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Findings: [FND-109](#fnd-109)

### QA-17

**A failure is not dropped without a trace** · should · process · **implemented**

A broad catch (except Exception, or bare except) that drops what it caught shall not be added; the checks shall count these and fail when there are more than the recorded number (67 on 2026-10-30), and a catch around a write or an owner-facing event shall log what failed.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-30.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_broad_catches_that_drop_the_error_can_only_become_fewer`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `ops/builds.py`
- Findings: [FND-68](#fnd-68)

### QA-18

**Every source file is traced** · should · process · **implemented**

Every source file under ops, images, config, compose, nas, converters, smoke and spec shall be named by a requirement (a trace, a source, a verify path or its text; a named directory covers what is under it, and an image's Dockerfile and configuration go with its code) or be listed in requirements/untraced-files.txt with its reason; the checks shall fail on a file that is neither, and on a listed file that is traced or gone.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-30.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_requirements.py::test_every_source_file_is_traced_or_listed_with_a_reason`
- Trace: `ops/req.py`, `requirements/untraced-files.txt`
- Findings: [FND-69](#fnd-69)

### QA-19

**The API reference is drawn from the code** · should · process · **implemented**

Every HTTP route of Mission Control and of the run record's ingest shall have a handler docstring that says what it does, and docs/API.md shall list every route with its methods and every MCP tool the app offers, drawn from the code by ops/api_doc.py; the tests shall fail on a route without a docstring and on a stale file.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-30.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_docs.py::test_the_api_reference_is_drawn_from_the_code_and_every_route_says_what_it_does`
- Trace: `ops/api_doc.py`, `docs/API.md`, `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Findings: [FND-72](#fnd-72)

### QA-20

**What the documents name exists** · should · process · **implemented**

Every file path, make target, app screen and relative link that a Markdown document in the repository names shall exist, every make recipe shall call its interpreter and a repository file that exists, and docs/README.md shall list every document under docs/ as current guidance or a record; the tests shall fail on one that does not.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-30.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_docs.py::test_every_path_make_target_screen_and_link_a_document_names_exists`, `tests/test_repo_docs.py::test_every_make_recipe_calls_a_file_that_exists`, `tests/test_docs.py::test_the_readme_and_the_index_point_at_what_is_current`
- Trace: `README.md`, `docs/README.md`
- Findings: [FND-71](#fnd-71), [FND-81](#fnd-81)

### QA-22

**No limit stays secret** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every limit that stops, refuses, drops or delays work shall carry in the code what it does and why it exists, and docs/LIMITS.md shall list them all. A limit without that tag, a list slice without a reason or an out-of-date list shall fail the checks. A limit that drops what the owner or an agent asked for shall say so where they see it, or refuse with the limit named.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/LIMITS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_limits.py::test_a_limit_is_seen_whatever_its_name`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_the_limit_that_refused_the_owner_s_repository_would_fail_the_check_today`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_a_limit_needs_what_it_does_and_why`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_the_list_of_limits_stays_current`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_every_limit_in_the_repository_says_what_it_does_and_why`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_nothing_is_left_of_the_size_limit_on_repositories`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_a_short_list_slice_needs_its_reason`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_triggers_and_tickers_over_the_limit_are_refused_not_dropped`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_a_briefing_checks_every_source_it_was_given`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_a_fan_out_runs_every_item_or_refuses_the_plan`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_past_the_day_s_budget_the_owner_is_told_once`, `tests/test_limits.py::test_an_attachment_says_which_sheets_and_rows_it_left_out`
- Trace: `ops/limits.py`, `ops/truncation.py`, `ops/check.sh`, `docs/LIMITS.md`
- Findings: [FND-123](#fnd-123)

### QA-23

**Unknown is not clean** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A check whose report is missing, cannot be read or is over two days old shall show on the health board as not known, never as a pass; a report that cannot be read or is old shall be an alert, and one that has never been written a note.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/CHANGE-REQUESTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_a_check_s_report_that_is_missing_broken_or_old_is_no_pass`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`

### QA-24

**The tests need no machine** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The unit tests shall pass in CI's container, which has no /srv, make or ssh, by keeping every state folder inside the test's own temporary folder, so that a green check in the builder and CI's verdict cannot disagree over what is installed.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `.forgejo/workflows/ci.yml`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_a_test_touches_no_folder_of_this_machine`

### QA-25

**The CI runner keeps its process table clean** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The CI runner shall run under an init process that reaps the children its jobs leave behind, so that finished jobs cannot fill its process limit and turn every later push into a failure that does not come from the code.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `compose/apps.yaml`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_the_ci_runner_has_an_init_that_reaps_what_its_jobs_leave_behind`
- Trace: `tests/conftest.py`, `ops/postmaster.py`

### QA-26

**A check that could not run says so** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A check that could not run shall report unknown, never pass or silence, and the health board shall show a sentence naming what could not be read. A review with a lens not done shall read as incomplete and stay out of Approve all, and ops/check.sh shall fail on a missing tool unless CHECK_NOT_HERE names it, in which case its summary line shall name the check not run. A NAS that gives no answer to ssh shall stay quiet, and any other ssh failure shall be unknown.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `GEN-01 in the generalising audit of 7 Nov 2026`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_board_that_cannot_be_read_proves_nothing_and_reopens_nothing`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_readable_board_still_passes_and_fails`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_check_that_cannot_read_its_logs_or_metric_is_unknown`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_checker_s_unknown_reaches_its_card_on_the_board`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_job_s_unknown_is_one_alert_on_its_card_and_its_next_clean_run_clears_it`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_an_unreadable_backup_log_is_an_alert_on_the_drill_and_one_bad_line_costs_one_line`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_one_bad_line_in_the_app_s_errors_keeps_the_alert_and_says_so`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_docker_failing_is_a_note_then_an_alert_and_never_no_cut_networks`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_default_docker_call_treats_a_failed_exit_as_a_failure`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_memory_that_cannot_be_read_is_an_alert_on_this_pc`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_runtime_database_error_is_reported_and_reaches_the_memory_keeper_s_card`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_code_that_cannot_be_read_is_no_zero_similarity`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_review_with_a_lens_not_done_is_incomplete_and_not_approved`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_all_leaves_out_a_review_with_a_lens_not_done`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_queue_does_not_recommend_a_review_with_a_lens_not_done`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_build_page_shows_an_incomplete_review_and_counts_it_out`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_missing_tool_fails_the_checks_unless_this_place_lacks_it_and_the_summary_says_so`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_summary_line_names_what_a_place_lacks_on_purpose`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_builder_lacks_compose_on_purpose_and_nothing_else_may_be_missing`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_self_review_says_what_it_could_not_read`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_nas_that_gives_no_answer_stays_quiet_and_any_other_ssh_failure_is_unknown`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_a_nas_that_took_the_connection_and_stopped_answering_is_not_asleep`, `tests/test_unknown.py::test_the_postmaster_s_unknown_reaches_its_card_on_the_board`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/checker.py`, `ops/keeper.py`, `ops/trials.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `ops/decisions.py`, `ops/check.sh`, `ops/improve.py`, `ops/postmaster.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-memory.js`, `ops/builds.review.py`

### QA-27

**The landing's tests run in parallel** · should · quality · **partial**

The checks shall run the whole test suite over worker processes (six by default, leaving the running stack two threads), each with a temp folder of its own, so a landing waits minutes, not a quarter of an hour, and no test meets another's files.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `owner 2026-11-10: I do not always want you to wait 15min. This is too slow. ... Parallel tests.`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_parallel_tests.py::test_the_checks_run_the_suite_over_workers_when_xdist_is_there`, `tests/test_parallel_tests.py::test_each_test_process_has_a_temp_folder_of_its_own`, `tests/test_parallel_tests.py::test_the_publish_runs_the_snapshots_tests_over_workers_too`
- Acceptance: Test: the checks run pytest with workers when pytest-xdist is there, and CI installs it; each test process has its own temp folder.
- Trace: `ops/check.sh`, `ops/publish.py`, `tests/conftest.py`, `tests/requirements.txt`
- Gap: the builder image does not carry pytest-xdist yet (a rebuild from scratch after the build cache was pruned); it is installed in the running builder container, and the image takes it at its next rebuild.
- Rationale: 10 Nov 2026 a landing took about 15 minutes, 12 to 14 of them for about 1,600 tests on one of eight cores; a lock file another user had left in /tmp failed one landing.

## RES Research

### RES-01

**Questions pass the privacy guard** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Before a research question leaves the house, the research tool shall check it against patterns, the private-terms list and a model check on its own key, and block it when unsure.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/research/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_research_guard.py::test_patterns_terms_and_length_refuse_before_anything_leaves`, `tests/test_research_guard.py::test_a_missing_terms_list_refuses_and_says_so`, `tests/test_research_guard.py::test_the_guard_model_refuses_when_it_says_private_or_cannot_answer`, `tests/test_research_guard.py::test_a_public_question_reaches_the_scout_and_comes_back_labelled`
- Trace: `images/research/server.py`, `ops/private-terms.sh`

### RES-02

**The scout searches the web** · must · functional · **implemented**

The scout shall search the web with its keyless search backend before reading pages, and never guess URLs.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/hermes/AGENTS.md`, `config/hermes/configure-scout.sh`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_scout_search.py::test_the_scout_s_search_package_is_in_the_environment_hermes_runs_from`
- Findings: [FND-10](#fnd-10)

### RES-03

**Pages and PDFs read in full, isolated** · must · functional · **implemented**

The page reader shall read HTML (also when the site sends it compressed) and full PDFs in the isolated reader container, identify itself with the SEC contact user agent on sec.gov, and page long output instead of truncating it unbounded.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/fetch/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_fetch_reader.py::test_a_gzip_page_rebuilt_from_decoded_bytes_reads_as_text`, `tests/test_fetch_reader.py::test_the_reader_rebuilds_its_response_with_plain_headers`
- Trace: `images/fetch/fetch_pdf.py`, `images/fetch/entrypoint.sh`

### RES-04

**Research answers are cited and labelled** · must · functional · **implemented**

The research tool shall return the scout's cited answer labelled as untrusted web content, within Hermes' 600-second MCP timeout.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/research/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/research/server.py`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`

### RES-05

**Deep research** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a task runs as deep research, the runner shall write a plan, run 3 to 5 research steps and a synthesis, and show each step live.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/06-deep-research.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/mission_extras.py`

### RES-06

**The browser only looks** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The scout's browser shall refuse login, account, cart, checkout and payment pages before and after every step, refuse buy, cart, order and sign-in buttons and typing outside search fields, and go back when a click lands on checkout.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-10](#cst-10)
- Sources: `images/browser/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_guard_refuses_login_cart_checkout_and_payment`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_guard_goes_back_when_a_click_lands_on_checkout`
- Trace: `images/browser/guard.py`

### RES-07

**Research results stay small** · should · quality · **partial**

The scout's page and search results shall be capped and the mission's earlier findings passed into each research question, so that a run does not re-read what it already found.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/fetch/fetch_pdf.py`, `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Gap: Page output is capped at 20 k characters; search results are not capped and findings are not passed in (TOKENS.md "Still open").

## SEC Security

### SEC-01

**Tailscale login is the only sign-in** · must · functional · **implemented**

Mission Control shall refuse every /api request that lacks the Tailscale-User-Login header.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `images/mission-control/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_api_needs_the_tailscale_identity`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Rationale: A local process could forge the header; that is an accepted risk (it could read the files directly).

### SEC-02

**Gates in front of apps with their own login** · must · functional · **implemented**

The gates in front of the Hermes dashboard and the code map shall refuse every request without the Tailscale-User-Login header.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `config/gate/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/gate/hermes-dashboard.Caddyfile`, `config/gate/codemap.Caddyfile`, `smoke/smoke.sh`

### SEC-03

**Only the scout reads the web** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Only the scout profile shall read web pages or web search results directly, and the scout shall have no access to the vault, memory, mail or the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tokens.py::test_agent_profiles_carry_only_what_they_use`
- Trace: `config/hermes/configure-scout.sh`, `ops/keys.sh`

### SEC-04

**No way out for an agent that holds private data** · must · constraint · **implemented**

An agent that holds private data and reads untrusted content shall have no tool that can send data to a third party without the owner's approval of that action, of a task naming it, or (for text and addresses that hold no e-mail address, phone number, IBAN, key or IP address) of his free mode on the AI phone (DEC-38).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: analysis: `tests/test_phone.py::test_in_free_mode_only_an_address_or_text_with_private_data_asks`, `ops/keys.sh`, `images/phone/phone.py`, `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Findings: [FND-02](#fnd-02), [FND-22](#fnd-22)
- Refined by: [SWM-67](#swm-67)

### SEC-05

**Untrusted content stays labelled** · must · functional · **implemented**

Web research results, mail content and page text shall reach an agent wrapped and labelled as untrusted data.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_search_is_marked_untrusted_and_flags_protected`
- Trace: `images/research/server.py`, `images/mail/mail.py`
- Refined by: [SWM-67](#swm-67)

### SEC-06

**Isolated readers** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The page reader, the scout's browser and the price watch shall run behind an egress firewall that blocks private, LAN and Tailscale ranges, and the desk shall refuse private and loopback URLs.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `images/fetch/AGENTS.md`, `images/browser/AGENTS.md`, `images/pricewatch/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_desk.py::test_public_url_refuses_private_targets`, `tests/test_desk.py::test_a_redirect_to_a_private_address_is_refused`
- Trace: `images/fetch/entrypoint.sh`, `images/browser/entrypoint.sh`, `images/pricewatch/entrypoint.sh`, `images/desk/desk.py`

### SEC-07

**One key per agent** · must · constraint · **partial**

Each agent shall call the gateway with its own virtual key, limited to its own models, daily budget and MCP servers.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_only_the_creation_script_writes_the_keys_table`, `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_update_keys_only_reads_the_table`, `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_background_jobs_use_their_own_keys`
- Trace: `ops/keys.sh`, `ops/update-keys.py`
- Gap: Every background job has its own key since 2026-10-29; mission runs of every type (task, shopping, cleanup) still share vk-mission, so two runs at the same time mix in cost attribution. The swarm registry gives every card its own key (SWM-23).
- Findings: [FND-03](#fnd-03)
- Refined by: [SWM-23](#swm-23), [SWM-24](#swm-24)

### SEC-08

**The builder is sealed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The builder container shall see only /work, hold no vault, secrets, SSH keys or Docker socket, reach only the gateway and its package proxy, and be unable to push or deploy.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/builder/AGENTS.md`, [DEC-03](#dec-03)
- Verified by: inspection: `compose/apps.yaml`, `images/builder/AGENTS.md`

### SEC-09

**Package proxies are allow-lists** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Sandboxes shall reach package registries only through the package proxy's allow-list, and the builder's list shall add only api.anthropic.com.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/pkgproxy/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/pkgproxy/allow.txt`, `images/pkgproxy/allow-builder.txt`

### SEC-10

**The Claude token lives only per run** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The runner shall pass the Claude Code token into the builder only for the run that needs it, never as part of the container's environment or files.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/builds.py`, `ops/claude_tasks.py`

### SEC-11

**The runner validates every request** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall execute a request from the app or an MCP tool only after validating its action against an allow-list and its fields (slugs, schedules, sizes, ids), and refuse anything else.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `ops/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_create_and_unknown_actions_are_validated`, `tests/test_server.py::test_requests_go_to_the_inbox_and_unknown_actions_are_refused`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_crew_actions_are_validated`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_requests_are_validated`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_requests_are_validated`, `tests/test_claude_tasks.py::test_requests_are_validated`
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`
- Refined by: [SWM-13](#swm-13)

### SEC-12

**The app never executes** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Mission Control shall not start jobs itself; it shall hand every action to the runner through the inbox, except the documented exceptions (chat with Hermes, the runtime ingest, the owner's answers to phone and mail approvals and grants written to their shared folders, push subscriptions, the app's own error reports), which AGENTS.md, docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md and docs/API.md shall list.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`, `docs/API.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_docs.py::test_the_readme_and_the_index_point_at_what_is_current`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mail/AGENTS.md`, `images/phone/AGENTS.md`, `images/mission-control/server.devices.py`
- Findings: [FND-20](#fnd-20)

### SEC-13

**Paths stay inside their roots** · must · functional · **implemented**

Paths taken from requests shall be resolved through the existing guards and refused when they leave the vault, a wiki root or a repository.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_vault_files_stay_inside_the_vault`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_source_snippets_stay_inside_the_repo`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `ops/repos.py`

### SEC-14

**E-mail only to the owner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every e-mail the stack sends shall go to the owner's own address (MAIL_TO) and to no one else.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/07-delivery.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/mission_extras.py`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`

### SEC-15

**The repo's own security rules run on every change** · must · quality · **implemented**

The checks shall run Semgrep with the repo's rules (no public ports, no Docker socket, no secrets in code, dangerous patterns) and prove with fixtures that the rules still catch what they should.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/check.sh`, `.semgrep.yml`, `ops/semgrep_selftest.py`

### SEC-16

**Evidence is redacted** · must · functional · **implemented**

Logs, journals and records gathered as evidence for a Claude task or the health doctor shall have keys, tokens and passwords blanked before any model sees them.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `ops/doctor.py`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_doctor.py::test_evidence_is_redacted`, `tests/test_claude_tasks.py::test_evidence_for_reads_each_ref_and_redacts`
- Trace: `ops/doctor.py`, `ops/claude_tasks.py`

### SEC-17

**Personal exports never leak** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The export converters shall never print message contents and shall name output files from dates and subjects, not addresses.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `converters/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `converters/bulk_convert.py`

### SEC-18

**Providers that see data are named** · must · quality · **implemented**

The guide and README shall name every provider that receives data (OpenAI through the ChatGPT subscription, Anthropic through Claude Code) and what each one sees.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: inspection: `README.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/01-welcome.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/14-safety.md`
- Findings: [FND-05](#fnd-05)

### SEC-19

**The public snapshot holds nothing personal** · must · functional · **implemented**

make publish shall push the committed stack to the public GitHub repository only as a snapshot without the private history, in which every identifier of the private map outside the repository is replaced and the encrypted secrets are left out with an example naming their values, and only when a scan finds no identifier in any case, e-mail address, LAN or Tailscale address, tailnet name, key or token outside the allowed placeholders, and the snapshot's own tests pass. A binary file shall stop it, except an image without metadata in docs/screenshots.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/PUBLISHING.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_publish.py::test_images_come_only_from_one_folder_and_hide_nothing`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_a_generated_file_stays_what_its_generator_makes_after_the_name_swap`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_every_landing_runs_the_privacy_scan_and_a_leak_stops_it`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_an_address_written_as_a_regular_expression_is_still_found`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_real_runs_stay_out_but_the_evidence_requirements_cite_stays`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_a_fresh_publish_replaces_the_public_history`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_terms_are_replaced_as_whole_words_and_only_a_capitalised_sentence_start_is_capitalised`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_the_scan_names_every_leftover_without_printing_the_term_and_lets_placeholders_pass`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_the_snapshot_leaves_out_the_secrets_and_follows_the_public_text`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_a_snapshot_with_a_leftover_runs_no_tests_and_pushes_nothing`, `tests/test_publish.py::test_the_push_commits_every_file_of_the_snapshot_whatever_its_gitignore_says`
- Trace: `ops/publish.py`, `ops/private-terms.sh`, `Makefile`
- Findings: [FND-75](#fnd-75), [FND-124](#fnd-124)

### SEC-20

**No write from another site** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Mission Control shall refuse every /api request other than GET, HEAD and OPTIONS whose Sec-Fetch-Site header names another site (cross-site or same-site), so a page elsewhere cannot act under the owner's Tailscale login.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_a_write_from_another_site_is_refused_and_the_app_s_own_writes_pass`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Rationale: tailscale serve adds the owner's login to every request his browser sends, a forged one from another page included (review APP-R1, SEC-R4). Programs send no Sec-Fetch-Site and are not affected; /share keeps its own reasoning.

### SEC-21

**The runner's git never runs what the sandbox wrote** · must · constraint · **partial**

The build runner shall run git in a clone under the workbench only with hooks and fsmonitor off, and shall refuse a clone whose .git is not a plain folder or whose own config sets anything but the core, origin, branch and user keys a clone of Forgejo needs, or whose origin is another clone in the workbench.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_clone_the_sandbox_rigged_is_refused_and_its_hooks_never_run`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Gap: Only ops/builds.py (and changes.py and conductor.py through it) is sealed; repowiki.py, replay.py, trials.py, claude_tasks.py and crew.py still run git in the workbench with their own helpers, and the design fix (the runner keeps its own clones and takes the builder's work in as data) waits for the owner (docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md).
- Rationale: The builder writes every clone's .git (its /work), and the runner that runs git there holds every secret and the Docker socket (review SEC-R1).

### SEC-22

**What a deployment forbids never reaches git** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A commit or a push of the stack's repositories that holds what the deployment's private never-in-git list forbids (in a changed line, a file name or a message) shall be refused, also on the runner's own pushes from the builder's clones, without printing the match; the list shall be in no repository, and every deploy shall install the repository's hooks again.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_termguard.py::test_a_commit_holding_a_forbidden_term_is_refused_and_a_clean_one_passes`, `tests/test_termguard.py::test_a_push_of_a_commit_that_skipped_the_hooks_is_refused`, `tests/test_termguard.py::test_the_runners_own_push_is_guarded_and_a_removed_line_is_no_leak`, `tests/test_termguard.py::test_without_a_list_everything_passes`
- Acceptance: Test: a commit with a forbidden term in a staged line or its message is refused, a push of a commit that skipped the commit hooks is refused, the runner's push guard refuses it too, a removed line and a term inside a longer word pass, and without a list everything passes.
- Trace: `ops/termguard.py`, `ops/git-hooks/pre-commit`, `ops/git-hooks/commit-msg`, `ops/git-hooks/pre-push`, `ops/builds.py`, `ops/deploy.py`
- Rationale: The owner, 9 Nov 2026: "From now into the future never can something Example specific reach git." The stack is a platform; one deployment's employer, products and projects stay out of every repository.

## SHP Shopping

### SHP-01

**Only budget and deadline are asked** · should · functional · **implemented**

When the owner wishes for something, Hermes shall create a shopping agent asking only for budget and deadline, and the agent shall read the PC's own facts (board, slots, modules, graphics card, disks) instead of asking.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-shopping.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_shopping_missions_can_be_created`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_the_pc_facts_reach_the_prompt`
- Trace: `ops/shopping.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/shop.js`

### SHP-02

**Offers with shipping, seller and delivery** · should · functional · **implemented**

The agent shall compare current offers from at least two German shops in euros including shipping, with seller, rating and delivery date, reading prices in any page format.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-shopping.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_best_price_is_read_in_any_format`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_first_run_reports_and_remembers_the_price`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_a_shop_page_s_price_is_read_whole_in_german_and_english`

### SHP-03

**Buy now or wait, with a reason** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall end in a proposal card with a verdict (buy now, or wait until a price or date, with the reason) that knows the sales ahead, and a wait verdict shall keep watching.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-shopping.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_a_run_ends_in_a_proposal_card`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_sales_ahead_know_black_friday`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_wait_verdict_keeps_watching`

### SHP-04

**The price watch stays quiet** · should · functional · **implemented**

The sealed price watch shall re-read open proposals' offer pages and report only when the price drops below the target; it shall send no notifications itself.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/pricewatch/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_the_same_price_under_the_target_proposes_once`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_price_watch_stays_quiet_until_the_price_drops`
- Trace: `ops/pricewatch.py`
- Findings: [FND-29](#fnd-29)

### SHP-05

**Limits** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Purchases shall stay within the owner's limits (default 150 EUR per purchase, 300 EUR per month counting what was bought, shops amazon.de), set only by him in the app and validated; nothing shall be bought until his postcode is set.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-shopping.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_monthly_cap_counts_what_was_bought`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_limits_are_validated`

### SHP-06

**Over a limit, only a link** · must · constraint · **implemented**

If the agent's pick is over a cap or in a shop not on the list, then the card shall offer only a Buy it yourself link, and an over-cap pick shall be watched and proposed again when it fits. Every shop link the agent gives shall be kept and shown only as a web address (http or https).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`, `docs/REVIEW-2026-11-07.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_a_caller_that_gave_up_does_not_reset_the_browser`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_over_a_cap_only_a_buy_yourself_link`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_a_top_pick_from_another_shop_is_a_buy_yourself_link`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_an_over_cap_pick_is_watched_and_proposed_again_under_the_cap`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_an_offer_link_is_kept_only_as_a_web_address`

### SHP-07

**Only the owner approves a purchase** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Only the owner's tap in Mission Control on his own device shall approve a purchase; the approval shall name exactly the item, variant, seller, ceiling (total x 1.03 + 1 EUR, never over the cap), address words and shop app, and expire after 45 minutes. The approval shall hold for the proposal as the owner saw it; when the proposal changed after that (a re-run with the same link updates it in place), the tap shall be refused with the reason.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-11](#cst-11)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_an_ai_phone_down_for_half_an_hour_is_an_alert_unless_the_owner_stopped_it`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_only_the_owner_approves_and_the_phone_gets_exactly_that`, `tests/test_server.py::test_purchases_are_decided_only_by_the_owner`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_the_shopping_agent_s_phone_requests_are_readable_by_the_phone`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_one_unreadable_request_never_stops_the_phone`
- Findings: [FND-116](#fnd-116)

### SHP-08

**Not now and Change** · should · functional · **implemented**

Not now shall keep watching and propose again when the price is at most 95 % of the proposal's total or after two weeks, and Change shall look again with the owner's words.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-shopping.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_not_now_keeps_watching_and_change_looks_again`

### SHP-09

**The final screen is checked without a model** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Before the one buy tap, the AI phone service shall read the whole final screen and tap only if the item, variant, seller and address words are present, the total is at most the ceiling and there is no sign-in, code, captcha or card check; the tap shall happen once.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-11](#cst-11)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_the_final_tap_checks_the_button_under_it_on_the_screen_read_just_now`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_enter_is_refused_during_a_purchase`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_final_check_passes_only_the_approved_order`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_the_approved_final_tap_happens_once`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_mismatch_stops_without_buying`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_an_amount_is_read_whole_or_not_at_all`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`

### SHP-10

**Purchase mode is tighter** · must · constraint · **implemented**

While a purchase approval is open, the AI phone service shall refuse typing and taps on change, edit, address, payment and voucher labels.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_during_a_purchase_no_typing_and_no_address_changes`

### SHP-11

**After the order** · should · functional · **implemented**

After a buy, the agent shall report the order number from the confirmation screen and follow the order mails (under the Gmail read grant) until delivery; a stopped purchase shall ask the owner with a screenshot.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_buy_run_reports_the_order`, `tests/test_shopping.py::test_a_stopped_purchase_asks_the_owner`

### SHP-12

**A run cut off by a restart starts again once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the runner restarts during a shopping run, the run shall start again once.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `ops/shopping.py`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_shopping.py::test_a_run_cut_off_by_a_runner_restart_starts_again_once`

### SHP-13

**A wish ends delivered, and scored** · should · functional · **proposed**

A shopping task shall track its delivery, ask the owner once that the item arrived and works, and close with the price paid against the lowest valid offer seen in its window and the days from wish to delivery.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### Registry and identity

#### SWM-01

**Every agent described in one place** · must · functional · **partial**

Every agent shall be described once, as an agent card in config/agents.yaml, and its crew unit and workflow, its gateway key with MCP grants, its Hermes profile settings, its Board contact and wake rules and its card under Tasks → Team shall be generated from that card.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_repo_cards_pass_the_registry_check`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_shared_key_and_extra_grants_are_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a sample agents.yaml generates every derived file; the checks fail when a unit, key grant or profile in the repo differs from what the file generates.
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/registry.py`
- Gap: The cards exist and the checks fail on drift from keys and units; the units, Hermes profiles and the Agents page are not generated from them yet.
- Rationale: Today an agent is wired in up to five places (crew.py, keys.sh, Hermes config, systemd units, the app), which is how the shared vk-mission key (FND-03) happened unnoticed.

#### SWM-22

**A card states the agent's job** · must · interface · **implemented**

An agent card shall name the agent's id, purpose, responsibilities, owned artifact kinds with a definition of done for each, harness, model, key, schedule, roles, rooms, contact policy, wake policy, budgets, tools and autonomy, and the checks shall refuse a card that misses one or names an unknown harness, model, tool, role or room.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MAST failure taxonomy (Cemri et al., arXiv 2503.13657)`, `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_card_missing_a_field_or_naming_unknown_values_is_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a card missing each field in turn, and a card naming an unknown value of each kind, are refused with the field named.
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/registry.py`
- Rationale: Specification failures (unclear role, unclear task, no termination condition) are the largest of MAST's three failure categories; the card makes each agent's job explicit. The fields map onto an A2A AgentCard (name, description, skills) plus the stack's own.

#### SWM-23

**One identity per agent** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each agent shall have its own gateway key and its own swarm token, no key or token shall be listed by two cards, and the Swarm service shall take the actor of every event and the sender of every message from the token of the call, never from its arguments.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-07](#sn-07), [SEC-07](#sec-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI03 identity and privilege abuse`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_identity_comes_only_from_the_stored_credential`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_shared_key_and_extra_grants_are_refused`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_no_agent_config_or_environment_holds_a_swarm_token`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/registry.py`, `ops/swarm_setup.py`
- Rationale: Phase 0 (docs/SWARM-PHASE0.md): the gateway's per-user BYOK credentials carry each agent's own token to an MCP server; a key needs a LiteLLM user for it, so phase 1 gives every agent one.
- Findings: [FND-03](#fnd-03)
- Refined by: [SWM-89](#swm-89)

#### SWM-24

**Least privilege from the card** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Each agent's gateway key shall grant exactly the MCP servers and swarm tools its card lists, and the checks shall refuse a key grant that no card lists.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SEC-07](#sec-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI02 tool misuse`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_shared_key_and_extra_grants_are_refused`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_repo_cards_pass_the_registry_check`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the key grants generated from a sample card equal its tool list; an extra grant in ops/keys.sh fails the checks.
- Trace: `ops/registry.py`

#### SWM-89

**Swarm tokens stay in the gateway** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Each agent's swarm token shall be stored only as the agent's per-user credential in the gateway, never in an agent's environment, configuration or prompt, and the Swarm service shall take a caller's identity only from that stored credential and refuse a call that carries a client-supplied credential header.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SWM-23](#swm-23)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `docs/SWARM-PHASE0.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_identity_comes_only_from_the_stored_credential`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_no_agent_config_or_environment_holds_a_swarm_token`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/swarm_setup.py`, `ops/update-keys.py`
- Rationale: Phase 0 found that a credential header sent by the client replaces the stored one, so an agent that knew another agent's token could act as that agent; agents therefore never see tokens.
- Findings: [FND-32](#fnd-32)

#### SWM-25

**Each run names the card it ran with** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent run shall record the content hash of the card it ran with in its run manifest, so that the run view names what changed in the card since the agent's previous run.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [OBS-08](#obs-08)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_card_hash_changes_with_any_field`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_run_names_its_card_and_the_next_run_shows_a_change`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: two runs with different cards show the changed fields in the second run's drift line.
- Trace: `ops/registry.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`

#### SWM-26

**Pause any agent, a role or the whole swarm** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner pauses an agent, a role or the whole swarm in Mission Control, the Swarm service shall refuse the paused agents' swarm tool calls and the runner shall start none of their runs within 60 seconds, until the owner resumes them.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI10 rogue agents`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_paused_agents_are_refused`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_owner_pauses_and_resumes_agents_from_the_app`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_a_paused_agents_runs_are_skipped`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: after a pause, a swarm tool call from the agent is refused and a due scheduled run is skipped with the reason paused; demonstration on the phone.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/improve.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### SWM-27

**Agent cards in the A2A format** · could · interface · **implemented**

The Registry shall serve each card as an A2A v1.0 AgentCard document, on the Docker network only.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`, [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_each_card_is_an_a2a_agent_card_on_the_internal_network`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`

### Harnesses and models

#### SWM-02

**Different harnesses and models** · must · functional · **planned**

An agent shall run on any of the harnesses Hermes profile, Claude Code, OpenCode, Python job or script without a model, with any model the gateway serves or the Claude subscription, when only its card changes.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: -
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Acceptance: Test: changing a sample card's harness changes only the generated unit and adapter; the adapter conformance suite (SWM-28) passes for each harness.

#### SWM-28

**One adapter contract, tested for every harness** · must · interface · **partial**

Each harness adapter shall give its agent the swarm tools, run the start and finish steps of the run contract and start the agent when it is woken, and one conformance test suite shall run against every adapter.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_finish_is_whole_or_nothing_and_keeps_every_field`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_wake_waits_out_the_usage_limit_and_a_hermes_agent_gets_one_turn`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_woken_crew_agent_answers_its_requests_with_its_model`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_finish_is_checked_first_and_a_run_without_one_is_a_health_note`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/crew.py`
- Gap: The Hermes adapter (one turn through the running Hermes, ending with swarm_finish), the Claude Code crew (answers from its model, start and finish recorded by crew.py) and script agents (declined at once) are covered; OpenCode has no adapter yet.

#### SWM-29

**Subscription and local models only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A card shall name only a model the gateway serves under CST-02, the owner's Claude subscription or a local Ollama model, and the checks shall refuse any other.

- Parents: [CST-02](#cst-02), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_paid_models_are_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a card naming a paid API model is refused.
- Trace: `ops/registry.py`

### Ledger

#### SWM-03

**One artifact model with a lasting identity** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent-produced artifact (idea, finding, report, wiki page, purchase, build, agent change, requirement) shall be one Ledger record with a kind, an identity key unique per kind, versions, an evidence log, links, watchers and references to outside records.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-10](#dec-10), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_history_is_append_only_and_rebuilds_the_state`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_kinds_are_data_not_code`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an artifact of each kind is created, versioned, linked and watched through the same tools.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Builds and purchases keep their own stores, where the owner's approvals and money rules live (SWM-69); the Ledger links them to their ideas by ref (build:<id>).
- Findings: [FND-24](#fnd-24)

#### SWM-30

**History is append-only** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every Ledger change shall be stored as an append-only event with actor, run id, time, sequence number, reason and evidence, the current state of an artifact shall be derived from its events, and no event shall be changed or deleted.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `event sourcing`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_history_is_append_only_and_rebuilds_the_state`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: replaying an artifact's events rebuilds its stored state; the database refuses an update or delete of an event (trigger).
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Audit, replay for the improvement loop (SWM-82) and restart safety (SWM-19) all need the full history, not the latest row.

#### SWM-31

**Retried writes take effect once** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every Ledger write shall carry an idempotency key, and a write repeated with the same key shall return the first result and change nothing.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `Hermes Kanban idempotency keys`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_a_key_that_comes_round_again_moves_the_state_again`, `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_a_key_is_its_author_s_own_and_private_text_stays_from_a_way_out`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_retried_writes_take_effect_once`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the same upsert sent twice leaves one version and one event.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-32

**No lost updates between agents** · must · quality · **implemented**

A Ledger update shall name the version it was based on, and the Swarm service shall refuse an update based on an older version with the current version, so that the agent can merge and retry.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `optimistic concurrency control`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_a_stale_update_is_refused_with_the_current_version`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: two agents update the same artifact from version 3; the second is refused and receives version 4.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-33

**Look up before writing** · must · functional · **implemented**

When an agent creates an artifact whose key already exists, the Ledger shall apply it as an update of the existing artifact; when the TF-IDF cosine similarity of a new artifact's text to an open artifact of the same kind reaches the calibrated threshold (0.20 on the owner's triage of 2026-10-29), the Ledger shall refuse the create unless the agent links it as different with a reason.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_same_key_updates_and_a_similar_new_one_is_refused`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_migration_report_names_what_the_guard_would_have_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a reworded idea with the same key updates the existing one; a reworded idea with a new key above the threshold is refused with the similar artifact named; the calibration set catches all 17 known pairs.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: 7 of 26 ideas on 2026-10-29 were reworded duplicates that title matching missed. Phase 0 measured TF-IDF (AUC 0.992, 7 of 7 nearest neighbours right) as good as embeddings (0.993, 7 of 7) on that triage, at no memory cost; the threshold catches every known duplicate and is recalibrated on the Ledger's own merges.
- Findings: [FND-26](#fnd-26)

#### SWM-09

**Agents see each other's artifacts** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent shall be able to search, read, link and watch every other agent's artifacts, within the information-flow rules of SWM-67.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_private_artifacts_never_reach_an_agent_with_a_way_out`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_the_builders_prompt_lists_the_finding_the_doctor_linked_to_its_idea`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/builds.py`
- Findings: [FND-22](#fnd-22)

#### SWM-34

**Watchers hear of every change** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a watched artifact changes, the Swarm service shall put the change into each watcher's next context pack, and into the owner's Chatroom feed when he watches it.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_watchers_see_changes_once`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an agent watching an artifact finds its new version in its next context pack, once.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-35

**One store per artifact** · must · constraint · **implemented**

When a kind moves into the Ledger, its existing records shall be migrated with their history in one step, and in the same phase the old writer shall be removed, so that no artifact is written in two stores.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-10](#dec-10)
