- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Trace: `images/mission-control/interactions.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`

#### SWM-112

**What you asked and decided is on the map** · should · functional · **implemented**

The list shall hold what the owner did: each chat and spoken turn with its answer, Run now, a typed task, a question to Claude, and decisions at the app's door and on the shared work, each reaching the agent concerned; an arrow from the owner shall open those rows. Nothing the owner said or was told shall be shown to the AI phone. Tests shall stay apart from work and one piece of work shall count once, a chip showing the tests when asked.

- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach

#### SWM-113

**Tool use, hand-overs and messages to you are on the map** · should · functional · **implemented**

The list shall hold what an agent does through another (Main on the AI phone, Inbox on Gmail), hand-overs through a file or an output (a plan's next step, a mail filed for the curator or a topic, an idea to the builder), and every message to the owner with its named sender, all senders naming themselves; a link worked out from timing shall say it is matched.

#### SWM-114

**A run the shutdown cut off is not stuck** · should · functional · **implemented**

A run that fell silent while the stack itself was down shall end as stopped at its last activity, with that reason, and raise no alert; a bar shall end at the run's last activity when it was silent to its end.

Every tool server of the gateway shall say what a call to it means for the map, or the build fails; a record the map reads that can't be read shall be named on screen instead of showing nothing.

| 2 | Plan graph and durable steps | [TSK-05](#tsk-05), [TSK-06](#tsk-06), [TSK-07](#tsk-07), [TSK-08](#tsk-08), [TSK-09](#tsk-09), [TSK-45](#tsk-45) |
| 3 | Event fabric | [TSK-10](#tsk-10), [TSK-11](#tsk-11), [TSK-12](#tsk-12) |

| 5 | Reach | [TSK-18](#tsk-18), [TSK-19](#tsk-19) |

### Task kernel

#### TSK-01

**One task for every kind of work** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall represent every piece of work as a task with its goal, definition of done, budget, deadline, autonomy level and rhythm (once, on a schedule, on an event, or after other tasks finish), and chat, missions, builds and Claude tasks shall each create one.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: the user should just give a task and the AI Stack figures out the rest`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_every_door_s_work_becomes_a_task_with_every_field`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Test: a task created from each door carries every field; the task list holds every run of the last 7 days.
- Trace: `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/missions.py`

#### TSK-02

**Give a task in one sentence** · must · interface · **implemented**

Mission Control shall start a task from one sentence in a single composer, and its deliberate mode shall show the task's fields filled with their presets before the task starts.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: give more deliberate tasks with details preset, but just giving a task is the usual way`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_sentence_gets_a_specification_and_reaches_its_door`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_owner_s_presets_are_kept_and_show_me_the_plan_first_waits`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Test: a sentence becomes a task with every field set, and a preset changed in deliberate mode is kept. Checked on screen at 375 px.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app-tasks.js`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/guide/20-tasks.md`

#### TSK-03

**The stack writes the specification** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before a task's first step, the intake agent shall write its specification (the goal, a checkable definition of done, the plan, and the agents it proposes with the reason for each), ask at most two questions and only when the goal cannot be planned without them, and wait for the owner only where the task's autonomy level asks for it.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, `Blitzy: requirements into a specification and an action plan, checked against the result`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_planning_a_restart_cut_off_is_planned_again_or_failed_when_old`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_at_most_two_questions_and_answers_plan_it_again`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_coding_task_without_a_repository_asks_which_one`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_failed_intake_says_why_and_can_be_planned_again`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_intake_prompt_names_every_agent_and_repository_and_the_app_cannot_be_used_from_the_ai_phone`, `eval/tasks-v1.yaml`, `eval/results/20261031-140852-tasks-gate.json`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Replay: 20 recorded one-sentence tasks (coding, research, analysis, watching) each get a specification with a checkable definition of done; no task gets more than two questions.
- Trace: `ops/tasks.py`, `prompts/intake.md`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/keys.sh`
- Findings: [FND-117](#fnd-117)

#### TSK-04

**One board for every task** · should · interface · **implemented**

The app shall list the tasks under Waiting on you, Running, Scheduled and Done, each with its plan, the live tree of its agents, its use of the subscriptions and the reason each agent was chosen; a scheduled task shall show when its last run ended, what it produced, and when the next run comes.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_after_another_task_waits_until_that_one_is_done`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_weekly_briefing_task_gets_its_content_not_the_plan_email_when_asked_and_shows_its_runs`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Test: a running two-agent task appears under Running with both agents in its tree; checked on screen at 375 px.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`
- Findings: [FND-77](#fnd-77)

### Plan graph and durable steps

#### TSK-05

**A plan graph for every task** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Conductor shall turn a specification into a graph of steps with dependencies and parallel branches, stored with the task in the runner's task database, and a step shall start only when every step it depends on has finished; a plan with a cycle, or with a step no agent can do, shall be refused. Cancelling a task shall stop the work its running steps started (their Claude tasks and test suites), and such a step shall end stopped, not failed.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, `Anthropic dynamic workflows (beta, 2026-10-09): the lead model writes the orchestration`, [DEC-35](#dec-35)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_changes.py::test_a_cancelled_task_stops_its_running_suite`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_branches_run_together_the_join_after_both_and_a_cycle_is_refused`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_one_off_task_with_several_agents_goes_to_the_conductor_as_a_plan_graph`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_owner_can_cancel_and_every_planned_task_goes_to_the_conductor`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: a plan with two parallel branches and a joining step runs the branches together and the join after both; a plan with a cycle is refused.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

#### TSK-06

**Steps survive restarts** · must · quality · **implemented**

Each step shall be recorded with its inputs, outputs and resume point before and after it runs, so that a restart resumes the task at its first unfinished step and no finished step runs twice.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: [SWM-19](#swm-19), `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_restart_runs_the_interrupted_step_again_and_no_finished_step_twice`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_step_waits_for_its_claude_task_also_across_a_restart`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_that_fails_twice_fails_its_task_and_a_limit_is_waited_out`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_cut_off_by_the_runner_stopping_is_interrupted_not_failed`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_task_cut_off_by_the_runner_stopping_is_queued_again`, `eval/results/20261031-152501-conductor-gate.json`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: the runner is killed during step 3 of a five-step task; after the restart step 3 runs again, steps 1 and 2 ran once, and the task finishes.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`

#### TSK-07

**Any step on any harness** · must · functional · **partial**

The Conductor shall run each step on the harness and model its plan names, through the one adapter contract every harness implements.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [SWM-02](#swm-02), [SWM-28](#swm-28)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_on_opencode_reads_only_and_records_its_tool_and_model_calls`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_each_step_s_prompt_carries_the_results_it_waits_for`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_long_requirements_list_reaches_the_builder_apart_from_its_brief`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_step_waits_for_its_claude_task_also_across_a_restart`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: one task whose steps run on Hermes, Claude Code and OpenCode finishes, each step through its adapter.
- Trace: `config/opencode-step.json`, `ops/conductor.py`, `prompts/shared.md`
- Gap: Hermes, Claude Code, Python and OpenCode have adapters (OpenCode since 2026-11-01, one `opencode run` per step, read only, run for real on a repository question); the phase 2 gate ran one task across Hermes, Claude Code and Python, and one task across Hermes, Claude Code and OpenCode has not run yet.
- Findings: [FND-99](#fnd-99)

#### TSK-08

**Steps start only with headroom** · must · quality · **implemented**

The Conductor shall start a step only when the PC has the memory its harness needs available (0.25 GB for a model call, 0.5 GB for a Hermes turn, 0.75 GB for Claude Code, by Windows' available memory) and the subscription the step uses is below its usage limit, and shall otherwise queue the step and show why it waits.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [CST-14](#cst-14)
- Sources: [AST-12](#ast-12), `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_starts_only_with_headroom_and_says_what_it_waits_for`, `tests/test_changes.py::test_changes_in_review_take_no_slot_from_the_work`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: with available memory stubbed below a step's need, the ready step stays queued with the reason shown, and starts when memory returns.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/windows/host-probe.ps1`
- Rationale: The first rule asked 1.5 GB free for every step and held the phase 2 gate at 0.7 GB while the owner played a game; a step asks for what its harness starts (owner, 31 Oct 2026: "You can make due with less RAM").

#### TSK-09

**One trace per task** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every agent run, model call and tool call of a task shall be part of one trace for that task, in the shape of the OpenTelemetry GenAI conventions (invoke_agent with chat and execute_tool spans under it), shown in Phoenix and in the task's run view.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions (development status, 2026)`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_one_trace_the_task_s_run_holds_each_step_s_run`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_hermes_run_without_a_ref_joins_the_live_step_of_its_agent`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_step_waits_for_its_claude_task_also_across_a_restart`, `tests/test_otlp.py::test_a_task_run_is_one_trace_from_the_run_to_every_model_and_tool_call`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: a task with steps on two harnesses yields one trace whose spans name every agent run, model call and tool call.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/runtime.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `images/mission-control/otlp.py`
- Rationale: The task is one run in the runtime record, each step a run inside it and the agents' own runs and model calls inside the steps (#/live/task:<id>, Live trace and the task's page); a Hermes run, which can't carry its parent in, joins the live step of its agent; a Claude step's Claude task runs inside its step (since 2026-11-01); a minute after the run ends the tree goes to Phoenix as one trace (otlp.tree_spans, TSK-26).
- Findings: [FND-101](#fnd-101)

### Event fabric

#### TSK-10

**One event bus** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall collect events from news searches and feeds, Forgejo and GitHub activity (read on a schedule, since nothing may reach the PC from outside), incoming mail, Home Assistant state changes, the calendar, watched files, prices, filings and finished tasks into one event log, each with its source, time and content, and shall show for each source when it last looked, what it found, or why it is off.

- Parents: [SN-20](#sn-20), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: trigger on different kinds of events, like a particular news event breaking`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_reach.py::test_a_calendar_rule_fires_once_its_time_before_the_event_and_the_address_stays_private`, `tests/test_events.py::test_the_web_sources_baseline_first_then_fire_their_rules`, `tests/test_events.py::test_the_local_sources_each_fire`, `tests/test_events.py::test_mail_and_forgejo_are_read_only_while_a_rule_asks_and_a_baseline_is_not_new`
- Phase: 3 Event fabric
- Acceptance: Test: a sample event from each source lands in the event log with its source, time and content.
- Trace: `ops/ics.py`, `ops/events.py`, `images/desk/desk.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/tasks.py`

#### TSK-11

**Rules that start tasks** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a trigger rule's condition matches an event, the stack shall start a task from the rule's template at most once per cooldown, after a model confirms the event is the one the rule describes where the rule asks for it, and shall record each firing with the event that caused it.

- Parents: [SN-20](#sn-20), [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_events.py::test_a_rule_waits_out_its_cooldown_runs_its_check_and_records_every_firing`, `tests/test_events.py::test_rules_are_checked_when_saved_and_a_time_is_no_source`
- Phase: 3 Event fabric
- Acceptance: Test: two matching events within the cooldown start one task; a rule with the model check skips an event the check rejects; every firing names its event.
- Trace: `ops/events.py`, `prompts/intake.md`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### TSK-12

**Breaking news** · should · functional · **implemented**

When at least three independent sources report the same new story on a watched subject within 30 minutes, the event bus shall raise one breaking-news event for that story, and later reports of the story shall attach to that event.

- Parents: [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: like a particular news event breaking`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_an_alert_s_answer_is_in_its_message`, `tests/test_events.py::test_breaking_news_is_three_publishers_on_one_new_story_within_30_minutes`
- Phase: 3 Event fabric
- Acceptance: Test: three sources on one story within 30 minutes raise one event, a fourth attaches to it, and two sources raise none.
- Trace: `ops/events.py`
- Findings: [FND-118](#fnd-118)

### Self-staffing

#### TSK-13

**What each agent can do, measured** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent card, harness and model shall carry its skills, tools and limits and its measured success, time and usage per task type, updated after each task.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_judged_tasks_credit_each_agent_and_measure_it`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: a finished task updates the measured success, time and usage of each agent, harness and model it used, for its task type.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `config/agents.yaml`

#### TSK-14

**Routing by measured results** · should · functional · **partial**

The Conductor shall choose each step's agent, model and harness from the measured results for the step's task type, and shall fall back to the next choice when a subscription reaches its usage limit.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: `Vals Router Index (2026-10-20): no router beat the best single model on long agent tasks`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_step_goes_to_the_agent_that_did_that_kind_best_or_away_from_a_limit`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_the_conductor_routes_a_step_before_its_first_attempt_and_says_why`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Replay: routing by measured results scores at least as well as the fixed assignment on the replay sets; with one subscription stubbed at its limit, steps go to the next choice.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `ops/conductor.py`
- Gap: The Conductor routes a step before its first attempt to an agent sharing the planned one's role that met that kind of task at least 15 points more often (from 3 judged tasks on), or away from a subscription at its limit, and writes why on the step; whether routing beats the fixed assignment can only be measured once enough tasks are judged.

#### TSK-15

**Agents made for the task** · must · functional · **implemented**

When no agent card fits a step, the Conductor shall write a temporary agent (its prompt, tools, model, harness and budget) within the grants the task's autonomy level allows, run it under the existing keys, grants and sandbox, and end it with its task.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: even invent new agents for this task specifically`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, [DEC-34](#dec-34)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_temporary_agent_stays_within_its_grants_and_ends_with_its_task`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_step_no_agent_fits_gets_a_temporary_agent_that_ends_with_the_task`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_an_agent_the_intake_names_that_does_not_exist_gets_a_temporary_agent`, `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: a step no card fits gets a temporary agent whose grants are inside the task's autonomy level; a grant outside it is refused; the agent is gone when its task ends. Security review before the phase ships.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `ops/conductor.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

#### TSK-16

**Good temporary agents become permanent** · should · functional · **implemented**

A temporary agent that served at least three tasks that met their definition of done shall become a Coach proposal, replay-tested like any agent change, and every agent design shall stay in an archive with its scores.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `Automated Design of Agentic Systems (Hu, Lu, Clune; ICLR 2025)`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_design_is_archived_reused_by_name_and_proposed_after_three_met_tasks`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: the third successful task of a temporary agent creates one Coach proposal; the archive keeps the design with its scores.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`

#### TSK-17

**Each task judged against its definition of done** · must · quality · **implemented**

The Checker shall judge each finished task against its definition of done, reading beside the answer what the stack measured itself (each build's checks, the judge's check steps, the pages its agents opened), and the stack shall keep a daily history of success, time, tokens, usage headroom, queue length and the owner's corrections per agent, model and task type.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [MIS-16](#mis-16), `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_the_judge_reads_what_the_stack_measured_not_only_the_summary`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_each_run_of_a_recurring_task_is_judged_on_its_own_and_a_quiet_run_is_not`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_finished_task_is_judged_point_by_point_and_a_build_by_what_happened`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_judged_tasks_credit_each_agent_and_measure_it`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: a finished task gets a verdict against its definition of done, and the day's history row of each agent, model and task type it used changes.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `prompts/shared.md`
- Findings: [FND-97](#fnd-97), [FND-92](#fnd-92), [FND-102](#fnd-102)

### Reach

#### TSK-18

**More harnesses at no cost** · could · functional · **partial**

The stack shall offer Codex CLI on the ChatGPT subscription as a harness, and shall add a further harness only if running it costs nothing.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_on_opencode_reads_only_and_records_its_tool_and_model_calls`
- Phase: 5 Reach
- Acceptance: Test: the adapter conformance suite (SWM-28) passes for Codex CLI.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `config/opencode-step.json`
- Gap: OpenCode runs steps on the ChatGPT subscription through the gateway, at no cost (TSK-07). Codex CLI is not a harness yet.

#### TSK-19

**Agents outside the stack through A2A** · could · interface · **implemented**

The swarm shall accept A2A tasks from agents outside the stack on the tailnet, each outside agent under its own key and grants.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`, `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_reach.py::test_the_owner_s_calendar_and_a2a_switches_are_refused_from_the_ai_phone`, `tests/test_reach.py::test_outside_agents_are_off_until_switched_on_and_then_need_the_tailnet_and_their_token`, `tests/test_reach.py::test_an_outside_agent_s_message_is_a_task_that_waits_for_the_owner_and_it_sees_only_its_own`, `tests/test_reach.py::test_the_owner_adds_and_revokes_an_outside_agent_and_only_a_hash_is_kept`
- Phase: 5 Reach
- Acceptance: Test: an outside agent's A2A request with its token becomes a task that waits for the owner, and one without a token or from outside the tailnet is refused.
- Trace: `ops/a2a.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Rationale: The swarm already speaks A2A on the Docker network. The owner asked for it on 31 Oct 2026; it stays off until the owner switches it on, and an outside request waits for the owner's OK by default.

### Task kernel

#### TSK-20

**What the Conductor may do without asking** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Conductor shall plan, staff and run tasks and create temporary agents within read-only and sandboxed grants without the owner's approval, and shall ask before it messages other people, spends money, deploys code, deletes data or acts on the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, [DEC-34](#dec-34)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_sentence_gets_a_specification_and_reaches_its_door`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_owner_s_presets_are_kept_and_show_me_the_plan_first_waits`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel

### Plan graph and durable steps

#### TSK-21

**Every task through the Conductor** · must · functional · **partial**

Every occurrence of every task, one-off, scheduled, event-triggered or after another task, shall run as a plan graph under the Conductor, a single-agent task as a one-step graph, with builds, Claude tasks, mission agents, the Topic Desk and delivery as step kinds that keep their own approvals.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_briefing_s_alerts_move_with_it_and_an_alert_runs_the_desk_s_short_alert`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_briefing_that_runs_as_its_mission_moves_under_the_conductor_and_its_mission_keeps_the_topic`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_every_briefing_and_price_watch_still_on_its_mission_moves_under_the_conductor`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_worker_starts_an_event_s_and_a_schedule_s_run_while_it_holds_its_lock_and_heals_a_half_start`, `tests/test_triggers.py::test_a_task_s_trigger_starts_the_task_s_next_run_not_a_mission_run`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_task_on_an_event_waits_for_its_trigger_and_is_watched_with_the_missions_triggers`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_an_event_triggered_briefing_writes_the_desk_s_alert_and_delivers_it`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_price_watch_runs_its_shopping_agent_s_check_and_a_reached_target_starts_its_next_run`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_an_event_starts_a_run_whose_steps_know_the_event_and_more_events_wait_their_turn`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_weekly_briefing_task_gets_its_content_not_the_plan_email_when_asked_and_shows_its_runs`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_owner_can_cancel_and_every_planned_task_goes_to_the_conductor`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_build_step_waits_for_its_build_and_the_owner_s_tap_and_a_task_runs_again_on_request`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_sentence_gets_a_specification_and_reaches_its_door`
- Phase: 6 Every task a plan graph
- Acceptance: Test: a scheduled briefing task, an event-triggered task and a one-step chat task each run as a plan graph with their steps in the task's trace.
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/shopping.py`, `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/shop_steps.py`, `ops/desk_steps.py`, `ops/tasks.py`
- Gap: Every task the intake plans runs under the Conductor, once, on a schedule, on an event (each firing an occurrence, its event in every step's prompt) or after another task, with coding as a build step, code questions as Claude steps (DEC-36), a briefing as the desk's plan or, on an event, the desk's alert (TSK-22), and a price watch as checks of its shopping agent, whose purchases stay its own runs (DEC-37). Since 10 Nov 2026 each later occurrence of a scheduled task is started by its n8n workflow (WFL-09, DEC-51), never by the Conductor's own clock. Since 8 Nov 2026 the runner moves every briefing and price watch still running as its mission under the Conductor by itself, so no repeating task runs on two engines; a briefing's alerts are its task's too (the rhythm holds the schedule and the triggers; an alert's run is the desk's short alert). Still apart are a cleanup analysis (ops/cleanup.py's, on demand) and work started at a door directly (a chat, a build, a Claude question), which is followed there, not run as a graph.
- Findings: [FND-84](#fnd-84)

#### TSK-22

**A recurring task runs its plan each time, fanned out** · must · functional · **partial**

A recurring task shall keep its plan as a template that each occurrence copies with its own window, and a step that lists items shall run once per item in parallel as headroom allows, the next step getting every result; a briefing's plan shall be collect, research per topic group, write, check, deliver.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_scouts_findings_reach_the_desk_through_the_runners_own_folder`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_delivered_briefing_grows_its_topic_wiki_and_a_deleted_one_stays_deleted`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_each_run_of_a_recurring_task_is_judged_on_its_own_and_a_quiet_run_is_not`, `tests/test_desk.py::test_the_collected_events_survive_the_hand_over_and_each_finding_cites_its_links`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_briefing_runs_collect_scouts_write_and_deliver_and_each_desk_step_hands_on_its_work`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_scheduled_task_runs_at_once_then_each_time_from_its_template_with_its_own_trace`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_with_items_fans_out_in_parallel_and_the_next_waits_for_every_copy`
- Phase: 6 Every task a plan graph
- Acceptance: Test: one occurrence of a weekly briefing runs a collect step, three research steps in parallel and a write, check and deliver step, and the next occurrence copies the template again.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `images/desk/desk.py`, `ops/desk.sh`, `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/desk_steps.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`
- Gap: A recurring task's occurrences copy its plan, each with its own steps, trace, result and verdict against the definition of done (a run that found nothing new is kept and not judged); a step with items fans out; a briefing runs collect, a scout per key question in parallel, write and deliver. The check step is the writer's citation check inside the write step, not a step of its own.
- Findings: [FND-84](#fnd-84), [FND-85](#fnd-85)

### Observability of the swarm

#### TSK-23

**What every step used and made is recorded** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every page, search result list, job ad, filing, price series, vault note and memory a step reads, and every finding, draft, report and delivery it makes, shall be recorded as an artifact with W3C PROV's used, generated and derived links, and the report's citations shall mark which sources it cited.

- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`

- Phase: 7 Artifacts, map and telemetry
- Acceptance: Test: a finished occurrence's record links each source to the step that read it, the findings to the report, and marks the cited sources.
- Trace: `ops/provenance.py`, `ops/conductor.py`

#### TSK-24

**The swarm map** · must · interface · **implemented**

Each task occurrence shall show on its task page its swarm as a flow of sources, agents, findings, writer and outputs (Sources) and a timeline with one lane per agent run (Timeline), live while it runs; a tap on any part of the flow shall show what it came from and what it fed, and sources read but not cited shall be told apart.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_provenance.py::test_the_run_s_answer_is_derived_from_the_last_findings_and_marks_what_it_cites`, `tests/test_server.py::test_a_task_run_s_map_is_served_as_the_runner_wrote_it_and_the_newest_run_without_a_number`, `tests/test_app_after_n8n.py::test_a_run_is_followed_on_its_task_page_and_the_old_run_screens_land_there`
- Phase: 7 Artifacts, map and telemetry
- Acceptance: Test: the map of a recorded occurrence has every artifact and run of its record, and its lineage of the report reaches every cited source; checked on screen at 375 px.
- Trace: `ops/provenance.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`

#### TSK-25

**Telemetry history and dashboard** · should · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall keep a daily history per agent, model, harness and kind of task of runs, successes, the judge's verdicts, tokens, median and 95th-percentile time and cost for a year, and show it on the Stack page's Costs with filters, each agent opening its runs.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_history.py::test_a_day_of_recorded_runs_gives_one_row_per_agent_model_harness_and_kind`, `tests/test_server.py::test_the_telemetry_history_is_served_and_says_when_there_is_none_yet`, `tests/test_app_after_n8n.py::test_the_stack_page_holds_the_stack_and_the_rest_is_where_it_belongs_two_taps_from_the_menu`
- Phase: 7 Artifacts, map and telemetry
- Acceptance: Test: a day of recorded runs gives the expected rows, and the dashboard's numbers match them.
- Trace: `ops/history.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app-base.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### TSK-26

**One trace tree per occurrence in Phoenix** · should · quality · **implemented**

Each task occurrence shall be exported to Phoenix as one trace whose root is the occurrence, its steps the children and their model and tool calls under them, in the OpenTelemetry GenAI shape.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_task_s_planning_run_sits_under_the_task_s_run`, `tests/test_otlp.py::test_a_task_run_is_one_trace_from_the_run_to_every_model_and_tool_call`
- Phase: 7 Artifacts, map and telemetry
- Acceptance: Test: the spans of a recorded occurrence share one trace id and form a tree from the occurrence to every model and tool call.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/otlp.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`

#### TSK-27

**Cost of every run at list price** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every run, step and occurrence shall show its tokens and their cost at the provider's list price from the gateway's price table, marked as what it would cost if paid.

- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`

### Customisation

#### TSK-28

**Agent settings in the app** · should · interface · **partial**

The owner shall be able to change an agent's model, harness, tools, budgets and prompts in the app; each change shall be replayed on the agent's replay set and apply only when it passes, show its diff and result, and be reversible.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_customise.py::test_an_agent_s_model_change_is_an_agent_change_put_up_for_its_replay`, `tests/test_customise.py::test_the_owner_s_change_passes_its_replay_only_when_no_score_drops`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_deployed_build_is_reverted_in_one_step_through_the_merge_queue`
- Phase: 8 Customisation and UX
- Acceptance: Test: a model change is replayed and applied on a pass and refused on a fail; a rollback restores the card.
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`
- Gap: Model, harness, tools and standing instructions change in the app (Swarm → Team → Settings) as a replayed agent change that applies through Build it, the deploy tap and Revert. An agent's budget (ops/keys.sh) and its prompt files (prompts/<owner>.md, replay-gated by ops/prompts.py) are still changed through a build, not in the app.

#### TSK-29

**A tool added to an agent is a grant** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Adding a tool to an agent in the app shall change its key's grants only after the owner's approval, and shall be recorded as a security change; removing one shall need only the replay.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_customise.py::test_a_tool_added_is_a_grant_the_owner_confirms_and_a_tool_removed_needs_only_the_replay`

#### TSK-35

**A step that cannot do its work is not done** · must · functional · **implemented**

The intake shall see, for each agent it may plan with, what the agent can reach (the AI phone, the house, e-mail, web research, the vault), and a step whose agent answers that it cannot do its work (its answer begins "BLOCKED:") shall fail with that reason, without a second attempt, so that its task fails and the owner's message says why, instead of the task being reported done.

The Conductor shall build its adapters, step owners, memory needs and waiting harnesses from the step kinds declared by itself and by each module in its list of domain modules, and shall reach a task kind's own briefing, build and checkpoint logic only through that kind's hooks; when two modules claim one harness or task kind, or a step kind has no function, the import of the Conductor shall fail with a message that names it.

### Everything is a task

#### TSK-40

**A task is yours or the stack's** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every task shall have an owner, the stack for the work it gives itself (trials, probes, gates, the crew's duties, onboarding, and a question to Claude one of its crew asked) and otherwise the owner; the owner's lists shall show the owner's tasks, and the stack's shall be one filter away, never hidden.

How long it runs should not define the term. ... I feel we should keep 6 tabs and integrate tasks in agents. But tasks are not an afterthought. ... I want to see how tasks and agents connect ... Can Agents run without tasks? ... I feel like these should just be a special kind of tasks.`

Every mission shall be a task however old it is, an archived one as done, so that a repeating task never leaves the Tasks list after seven days; a task's own shopping agent shall stay that task.

Each crew duty shall be the stack's repeating task, its plan the duty and its When the duty's schedule (its n8n workflow's, WFL-09), once per duty and following the schedule when it changes.

The run record shall say which agents worked on which task in a period of 1, 7, 30 or 90 days, each run walked to the run its work started from and that named as a task (the Conductor's and its planning, a mission's run, a build, a question to Claude, a chat, a crew duty); the tools an agent used and the Conductor shall never count as agents, and the runs no task holds shall be counted apart.

## TRL Trials

### TRL-01

**A trial goes through the front door** · must · functional · **implemented**

A trial shall be a task in the owner's words with what is forbidden and a budget (eval/trials), given through the runner's inbox like the app gives it, into a fresh repository holding only its specification, watched to its end or its budget, an intake question answered for the unattended run and counted.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_replay.py::test_the_watcher_closes_its_read_of_the_runner_s_database`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_trial_is_checked_before_it_runs`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_task_given_straight_to_the_builder_is_followed_through_its_build`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_runner_s_answer_is_read_in_both_of_its_shapes`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `eval/trials/semver.yaml`, `Makefile`

### TRL-02

**An answer key no agent reaches, proved before it scores** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A trial's answer key shall live where no container is given it (/srv/ai/private/trials) and shall score its own reference solution fully before a run starts; a key that cannot is refused.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_answer_key_must_score_its_own_reference_fully_and_scores_by_area`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_trial_s_build_ends_at_review_and_is_never_merged_and_the_judge_scores_it_too`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`

### TRL-03

**Scored by a program, in a sandbox** · must · functional · **implemented**

The delivered code shall be scored by the answer key's own program in a container with no network, read-only, as nobody, with limited memory, per area of the specification; the model judge's verdict is kept beside it.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_answer_key_must_score_its_own_reference_fully_and_scores_by_area`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `ops/staffing.py`
- Findings: [FND-92](#fnd-92), [FND-94](#fnd-94)

### TRL-04

**The clean room is audited** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall be audited for forbidden reads (the pages its agents read) and actions (the commands and files of its builds and steps, not the prompts that name what is forbidden) and the delivered code, and its code's similarity to the upstream implementation measured with normalised token fingerprints and flagged above the trial's threshold.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_clean_room_audit_catches_forbidden_reads_and_actions_but_not_the_trial_s_own_names`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_similarity_tells_copied_code_from_independent_code`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`

### TRL-05

**A trial's build is scored, never merged** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A trial's build step shall end when its build reaches review, giving the branch and head the key scores; the run then archives the code, closes the build and removes the clone, so nothing of a trial is merged, deployed or left waiting for the owner.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_build_or_claude_task_a_step_started_is_not_a_task_of_its_own`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_trial_s_build_step_ends_at_review_and_is_scored_not_merged`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_trial_s_build_ends_at_review_and_is_never_merged_and_the_judge_scores_it_too`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/trials.py`
- Findings: [FND-91](#fnd-91), [FND-99](#fnd-99)

### TRL-06

**Every run reported and kept** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall be reported in the vault (score by area, every failed case, the clean room, the plan, builds, time, tokens, questions) and kept as a result for the scoreboard, and the owner told.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_report_says_the_score_the_clean_room_and_how_it_went`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`
- Findings: [FND-90](#fnd-90)

### TRL-07

**The roles software work needs** · should · functional · **implemented**

The swarm shall staff an Analyst, a Tester, a Tracer, a Breaker and a Presenter beside the coder, the reviewers and the judge, each with what it may and may not read enforced by its key, network and workspace.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_role_s_build_stays_in_its_lane`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_repo_cards_pass_the_registry_check`
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/conductor.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

### TRL-08

**The Orchestrator loops and re-plans** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Conductor shall run bounded loops (code, test, fix until it passes or the budget ends) and, after each wave, let the Orchestrator change the plan with the change shown to the owner.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_between_waves_the_orchestrator_keeps_or_changes_the_rest_of_the_plan`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_recurring_or_fixed_plan_gets_no_checkpoint`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_checkpoint_reads_a_long_result_s_start_and_knows_it_is_cut`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_check_sends_failures_back_to_the_builder_until_they_pass`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_check_out_of_fix_rounds_reports_what_still_fails_and_the_plan_goes_on`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_only_a_check_loops_and_only_back_and_a_fold_keeps_the_loop_on_its_build`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_intake_keeps_a_check_s_fix_loop_and_a_budget_preset`
- Acceptance: Test: a re-plan after a wave is recorded and shown on the task.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `prompts/shared.md`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`
- Rationale: The checkpoint is the intake agent on its own key (one model call); it never touches a finished step, adds at most three steps a look (each after finished or planned steps, and before steps not yet started that must wait for it) and looks at most three times a run, so a run's cost stays bounded. It reads each result's first 6,000 characters, marked as cut where it is longer.
- Findings: [FND-94](#fnd-94), [FND-100](#fnd-100)

### TRL-09

**A scoreboard across versions** · should · functional · **implemented**

The app shall show every trial's runs side by side, with score, cost, time, interventions and the coordination numbers, per version of the agents and the code.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_run_s_tokens_count_each_model_call_once`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_scoreboard_holds_every_run_side_by_side_with_its_version`
- Acceptance: Test: two runs of one trial on different versions show side by side with the change between them named.
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/server.a2a.py`
- Findings: [FND-101](#fnd-101)

### TRL-10

**A builder's work is one build** · must · functional · **implemented**

Consecutive builder steps of a plan (a builder step whose only predecessor is a builder step nothing else waits for) shall become one build whose brief lists them as stages, so a stage that plans or checks without committing does not fail the task.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_consecutive_builder_steps_become_one_build_with_stages`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`
- Rationale: The first trial run (semver, 1 Nov 2026) failed in 3 minutes because its plan's first builder step read and planned, committed nothing, and a build without a commit fails; its retry did the same.
- Findings: [FND-90](#fnd-90)

### TRL-11

**A task stops at its budget** · should · functional · **implemented**

A task may carry a budget of hours and tokens (its run's model calls and its builds' tokens); once it is spent, no further step shall start, and the task shall fail saying which budget ran out.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_build_inside_the_trace_counts_once_against_the_budget`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_spent_budget_stops_the_next_step`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_intake_keeps_a_check_s_fix_loop_and_a_budget_preset`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/trials.py`
- Findings: [FND-101](#fnd-101)

### TRL-12

**Requirements traced and gaps found, scored** · should · functional · **implemented**

A trace trial (T2) shall give the swarm a specification's requirements and an implementation with every requirement ID taken out of its code, ask for each requirement's file and line and for every requirement the code does not meet, and score the answer against where the IDs sat (the function), the deviations planted in the code and the code's own defects the key has confirmed, a gap the key has reviewed and rejected counting as a false alarm (TRL-13 for one it does not know); on an open-source broker (mqtt-trace) and on the stack's own code against its own requirements (stack-trace).

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-01: take the existing implementation and the requirements ... map where in the code requirements are fullfilled ... detect which requirements are unfullfilled`, `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_trace_trial_gets_the_implementation_beside_its_specification`
- Trace: `eval/trials/mqtt-trace.yaml`, `eval/trials/stack-trace.yaml`, `ops/trials.py`
- Rationale: The MQTT spec pilot (spec/README.md) gives the key and the numbers to beat; the key and its make_key.py live under /srv/ai/private/trials/mqtt-trace, out of every agent's reach.

### TRL-13

**A gap beyond the key is for review, and a run can be scored again** · should · functional · **implemented**

A trial's scorer shall list a gap its key does not know for review, unscored, with the agents' reason; the report and the scoreboard shall name those gaps; and a recorded run shall be scored again with the key as it is now, its tokens counted again from the run record, the record keeping what it said before.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `mqtt-trace run 2026-11-02 02:16: 25 gaps beyond the key, all counted as false alarms; of four read in the code, three were real defects of mochi-mqtt (topics.go 615, packets.go 427, clients.go 537) and one a MAY`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_gap_beyond_the_key_is_for_review_and_a_run_can_be_scored_again`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`
- Rationale: The key of a trace trial is one reading of the code, not all of it; a trace that finds a real defect the key does not know must not lose points for it, and the owner reviews such gaps into the key (real, or not).

### TRL-14

**The trials run as a weekly rotation with a trend** · must · functional · **partial**

One trial shall run each night at 01:00 in a weekly rotation (research accuracy, stack-trace, memory, coding, review catch, offer picking, phone routine and the voice test), never two at once and skipped while a task runs, and

- Trace: `ops/rotation.py`, `n8n/workflows/crew-trial-rotation.json`, `ops/systemd/rotation.service`

### TRL-15

**A drop in score freezes what it examines** · must · functional · **proposed**

A trial run that scores below its last good run by more than its noise band (the spread of its first three runs) shall open a finding naming the commits since that run and hold the licences of the classes it examines at ask until it scores again.

Offer picking, review catch and the phone routine are not written yet.

**Probes look for what the trials do not** · should · process · **proposed**

## VOI Voice

### VOI-01

**Speech services on the NAS, for the NAS only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Speech to text and text to speech for Home Assistant's voice pipeline shall run on the NAS, listen on the NAS itself only (127.0.0.1), store no audio, keep their logs (which carry transcripts) to 1 MB each, and be deployed host-side with images pinned by digest.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)

### VOI-02

**The voice is measured, not assumed** · should · functional · **implemented**

A repeatable measurement shall report, for the speech services on the NAS, the word error rate and the time per sentence of speech to text on spoken home sentences with noise, and the time to the first audio of each candidate voice; it shall store no audio.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)

The app shall offer Home Assistant's own Ollama integration a gateway on a port of its own (127.0.0.1:11435 on the PC) with only the model list and the chat, refuse every call without its key (and all calls while no key is set), offer one model per room (hermes:kitchen), and stream Hermes' answer back as Ollama does.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`

**One running Voice chat** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every spoken turn shall join one chat called Voice, with its room, in the same Hermes session from every room and the app, framed by the voice prompt (main.md VOICE); Home Assistant's own history and prompt shall not be used, and "new topic" shall start a fresh Hermes session while the chat keeps what was said.

When Hermes reaches for a tool before saying anything, the gateway shall say "Let me check" at once, and it shall record on each spoken answer its room, the time to its first word and whether it bridged.

## WFL Workflows

### WFL-01

**n8n is part of the core** · must · constraint · **implemented**

n8n shall run as a core service on every deployment, its image pinned by digest in versions.env, its port published on 127.0.0.1 only and opened to the owner on the tailnet (or on the PC itself in a deployment without one, WFL-15), never on the internet.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_n8n_is_part_of_the_core_and_starts_after_its_database`, `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_the_n8n_image_is_pinned_by_digest`, `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_n8n_is_reached_only_from_this_machine`
- Acceptance: Test: the compose core has n8n without a profile, after its database job; the image carries a digest; every published port is on 127.0.0.1.
- Trace: `compose/core.yaml`, `versions.env`

### WFL-02

**n8n keeps its data in the stack's Postgres** · must · constraint · **implemented**

n8n shall use its own role and database in the stack's Postgres, made by a one-shot job that is safe to repeat, so the nightly backup holds its workflows, credentials and executions; its database password and the key that encrypts its stored credentials shall come from the sops secrets.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_n8n_uses_its_own_role_database_and_secrets`, `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_the_database_setup_is_safe_to_repeat`, `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_the_boot_check_counts_the_database_job_as_set_up`
- Acceptance: Test: n8n-db runs the setup script with the admin and n8n passwords; n8n reads its database and encryption key from variables that make up refuses to start without; the boot check counts n8n-db as a set-up job.
- Trace: `config/n8n/db.sh`, `compose/core.yaml`, `ops/stack_ready.py`

### WFL-03

**n8n sends nothing home and runs no shell command** · must · constraint · **implemented**

n8n shall send no telemetry, version pings or survey, and its Execute Command node shall stay off; a command runs only at a place, approved by its fingerprint (GEN-22).

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_n8n_sends_nothing_home_and_keeps_its_command_node_off`
- Acceptance: Test: the service sets diagnostics, version notifications and personalisation off and lists the Execute Command and local file nodes as excluded.
- Trace: `compose/core.yaml`

### WFL-04

**n8n's history stays small** · should · quality · **implemented**

n8n shall prune execution data older than 30 days.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_n8n_service.py::test_n8n_prunes_executions_after_30_days`
- Acceptance: Test: pruning is on with a 720-hour age, tagged as a limit.
- Trace: `compose/core.yaml`

### WFL-05

**Steps run through one step service** · must · interface · **implemented**

n8n shall start every step of the stack's own work through Mission Control's step service, which hands it to the runner and returns at once; the runner calls n8n's resume URL with the result, also after a restart of either side. The step kinds are agent, crew, task, ask, notify, build (a coding change in the builder sandbox), recall (a memory search), remember (a note through the memory gate), command (a job kind at a place), plan (an improvised plan's steps in waves), wait_item (a wait on a tracker item, WFL-20) and tracker (a write to it, WFL-21), each run through the stack's own code for that work. A crew job whose unit is still running shall answer at once that the run in progress covers it, never start a second or wait behind it. Every state file the step service and the run register write (answers, jobs, questions, builds, the register's ledger, outbox, runs and items, the trackers' cursors) shall be written whole or not at all (a temp file in the same folder, then os.replace), and an empty or unreadable answer file shall count as no answer yet (said once, removed, the step handled again), so one bad file never holds a request.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_request_is_checked_before_anything_runs`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_the_answer_only_ever_goes_to_n8n_on_this_machine`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_an_agent_step_runs_through_the_conductors_own_runner_and_n8n_gets_the_answer`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_an_answer_n8n_did_not_take_is_sent_again_after_a_restart_without_running_the_step_again`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_step_that_fails_twice_answers_failed_with_its_reason`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_refused_request_runs_nothing_and_says_why`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_crew_job_runs_as_its_own_unit_and_waits_for_it`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_crew_job_still_running_covers_the_next_run_at_once`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_the_runner_starts_the_step_worker_and_the_app_serves_the_step_port_on_its_own_network`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_the_step_port_drops_a_step_for_the_runner_and_answers_what_is_known`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_an_agent_reads_its_brief_as_its_step`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_the_new_kinds_are_checked_before_anything_runs`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_coding_change_runs_as_the_conductors_build_step_and_answers_with_its_build`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_memory_search_answers_with_its_hits_each_cut`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_note_enters_memory_only_through_the_memory_tools_gate`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_command_runs_at_its_place_only_once_approved_there_and_is_submitted_once`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_plan_runs_in_waves_through_the_agent_path_and_answers_every_steps_output`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_a_state_file_write_cut_short_leaves_the_old_file_or_none`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_an_empty_answer_file_is_handled_again_and_answered`
- Acceptance: Test: a request is checked before anything runs; an agent step runs through the Conductor's own step runner on a row of its own and n8n gets the answer at this machine's n8n only; the agent reads its brief as its step; an answer n8n did not take is sent again after a restart without running the step again; a failed step answers with its reason; a crew job runs as its unit; the app's step port is on a network only n8n shares; a coding change runs as the Conductor's build step and answers with its build, branch, pull request and checks; a memory search answers with its hits, each cut visibly; a note enters memory only through the memory tool's gate; a command runs at its place only once approved there and is submitted once, also across a restart; a plan runs its steps in waves through the agent path and a step after a failed one does not run; a write killed half way leaves the old file or none, and an empty answer file left by one (10 Nov 2026, a deploy restarted the runner) is handled again and answered.
- Trace: `ops/flows.py`, `ops/statefile.py`, `ops/runreg.py`, `images/mission-control/server.flows.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `ops/domain/packs.py`, `ops/domain/places.py`, `ops/memstack.py`

### WFL-06

**The stack's own nodes on the canvas** · should · functional · **implemented**

The canvas shall have the stack's own node, AI Stack step, whose kind makes it one block of the palette (agent, build, crew, task, ask, notify, recall, remember, command, plan), which sends the step to the step service and waits for its answer; its Agent, Crew job, Place and Repository pickers shall list the stack's own choices, read live from the step service; an answer whose status is not done shall end the n8n run as an error; every workflow of the repository uses it.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_n8n_sync.py::test_the_ai_stack_step_sends_the_step_waits_and_gives_the_answer`, `tests/test_n8n_sync.py::test_every_workflow_in_the_repository_is_a_whole_n8n_workflow`, `tests/test_n8n_ask.py::test_a_workflow_tells_the_owner_through_the_stacks_own_notifications`, `tests/test_flows.py::test_the_nodes_pickers_read_the_agents_crew_jobs_places_and_repositories_the_runner_writes`, `tests/test_n8n_sync.py::test_the_ai_stack_step_offers_every_block_with_the_stacks_live_lists`, `tests/test_n8n_sync.py::test_an_answer_that_is_not_done_ends_the_n8n_run_as_an_error`
- Acceptance: Test: the node posts with its execution's resume URL, waits and gives the answer; it offers every kind of the step service with its fields; its pickers load the agents, crew jobs, places and repositories from the step service's catalog, which the runner writes; an answer that is not done fails the node on resume with the answer's error and the whole answer kept; n8n loads it from the repository; every workflow's work goes through it. Shown on a throwaway n8n 2.44.7 with a stand-in step service on 10 Nov 2026: a workflow of plan, recall, command, remember and coding change blocks waited, was resumed five times and ended in success; a failed answer ended its run as an error; the pickers' methods listed the stand-in's agents, crew jobs, places and repositories.
- Trace: `n8n/custom/AistackStep.node.js`, `compose/core.yaml`, `ops/n8n_gen.py`, `ops/flows.py`, `images/mission-control/server.flows.py`

### WFL-07

**The owner decides in Needs you** · must · functional · **implemented**

A workflow that needs the owner shall put one question into Needs you with the answers it offers and wait; the owner is told once, and the owner's tap there shall resume it; nothing but one of the offered answers, once, counts.

### WFL-08

**Workflows live in git** · must · process · **partial**

Every workflow of a deployment shall be a JSON file in its repository; a change made on the canvas shall be exported and committed through the landing path, and a deploy shall import the repository's workflows.

- Sources: [DEC-51](#dec-51), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`

### WFL-09

**One scheduler** · must · constraint · **partial**

n8n shall be the one thing that decides when work starts on a schedule. Every scheduled job (a crew job, a task on a schedule, the task of a mission on a schedule) shall run from its own n8n workflow, and its schedule, next run and on/off switch shall be read from that workflow. The rule's only exceptions are the systemd timers of the units that must run when n8n is down: the boot check (it brings the stack, n8n included, up), the backup and the restore drill (they hold n8n's own database). A task that gets a schedule shall get its workflow once, made by the runner; a task that is over shall have its workflow switched off; after that its schedule is changed on n8n's canvas. One switch shall publish or unpublish a workflow.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)

### WFL-15

**n8n without the tailnet stays on the PC** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Where a deployment has no tailnet, n8n's editor address shall be the app's on the PC (http://127.0.0.1:8090/n8n/) and its webhook and resume URLs shall be n8n's own on 127.0.0.1:5678, set by the deployment (N8N_EDITOR_URL, N8N_WEBHOOK_URL); the deployment check shall refuse a deployment without the tailnet whose app gate is not local or whose n8n addresses are not on 127.0.0.1, and one with the tailnet whose gate is local.

### WFL-16

**The app's gate follows the deployment** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The app's front door shall admit by the deployment's gate: with the tailnet (APP_GATE tailnet, the default), only a request with Tailscale's identity header; without it (APP_GATE local), only a request that names the PC itself (127.0.0.1, localhost, ::1, or the app's service name), which shall be the owner's under one local login whatever Tailscale header it brings; under both, a write from another site shall be refused, and n8n inside the app shall be told the scheme the browser used.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-45](#dec-45), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_customer_deployment.py::test_without_the_tailnet_the_apps_gate_takes_this_pc_as_the_owner_and_still_refuses_a_write_from_another_site`, `tests/test_n8n_inapp.py::test_n8n_is_served_under_the_apps_address_behind_its_gate`
- Acceptance: Test: with the local gate the PC's request is the owner's and a forged Tailscale header names nobody, n8n gets http as the scheme, another host name and a cross-site write are refused before n8n is asked; with the tailnet gate the same request without a login is refused and the owner's login passes as before.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/server.frames.py`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `deployments/example-work/deployment.env`
- Rationale: Without a tailnet no Tailscale header exists, and the app (with n8n and Phoenix inside it) would refuse everything. Its port is published on 127.0.0.1 only, so a request that reaches it comes from the PC; the host check keeps out a page of another site whose name was made to point here.

### WFL-17

**Customer data loads only n8n's logic nodes** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Where a deployment holds customer data, its n8n shall load only the logic nodes (the schedule, webhook and manual triggers, wait, if, switch, merge, loop, set, no-op, sticky note, execute workflow and its trigger, respond to webhook) and the AI Stack step, through n8n's NODES_INCLUDE from the deployment's N8N_NODES_INCLUDE; the data guard shall refuse such a deployment whose list is empty or names a node off ops/dataguard.py N8N_NODES, and dataguard.n8n_nodes() shall give the code the deployment's list (None when n8n loads every node).

- Acceptance: Test: the example work deployment's list is exactly N8N_NODES, home's is none; an HTTP Request node in the list and an empty list are refused by the data guard. Shown on a throwaway n8n 2.44.7 on 10 Nov 2026 with the example's list: it loaded those 15 node types and no other, the AI Stack step and no langchain node among them.
- Trace: `ops/dataguard.py`, `compose/core.yaml`, `deployments/example-work/deployment.env`, `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Rationale: An HTTP Request, Code, Jira, Bitbucket or mail node would reach a system past the stack's steps, their approvals and shadow mode. Home keeps every node.

### WFL-18

**n8n's executions stay on the PC** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Where a deployment holds customer data, the data guard shall refuse it when a published port of any service is not on 127.0.0.1, when n8n's database volume (pgdata) or its home (n8n) is not a local volume, when n8n sends diagnostics or version pings, or when a feature by which they leave the PC (home's backup, offsite) is on.

- Trace: `ops/dataguard.py`, `ops/backup.sh`, `compose/core.yaml`, `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`
- Rationale: n8n keeps every node's output in its database, and an agent step's whole text is its output.

### WFL-19

**Tracker items start and wake runs** · should · functional · **implemented**

A crew job on n8n's schedule (tracker-poll, every minute, asking the trackers every poll_minutes of the deployment, 5 by default) shall poll every tracker of the deployment and the watched pull requests, and route each event once by its tracker, key and the change's identity, also across a restart of the runner or the poller, through the run register. An event for an item with no run shall start the item's workflow through n8n's webhook item/<pack>/<name> at n8n's root with its tracker, key, run, reference and comments; an event with new comments from someone other than the stack shall answer the run's waiting wait_item; an event without new comments shall never wake a wait; any other event is recorded, its comments going to the run's next wait. A run that has ended (its n8n execution finished) shall not start again when its item changes, except that a new comment starts a new run when the tracker says restart on_comment (default never). An event n8n does not take stays for the next pass.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`, [DEC-54](#dec-54)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_n8n_items.py::test_a_new_item_starts_its_workflow_once_through_its_webhook_even_when_its_event_comes_twice`, `tests/test_n8n_items.py::test_a_comment_from_someone_else_wakes_the_waiting_run_and_the_stacks_own_change_does_not`, `tests/test_n8n_items.py::test_a_comment_while_the_run_is_busy_elsewhere_answers_its_next_wait_at_once`, `tests/test_n8n_items.py::test_an_ended_run_is_not_started_again_unless_its_tracker_says_restart_on_comment`, `tests/test_n8n_items.py::test_an_event_n8n_could_not_take_stays_in_the_outbox_and_starts_its_run_on_the_next_pass`, `tests/test_n8n_items.py::test_the_poller_is_a_crew_job_on_n8ns_minute_and_asks_the_trackers_every_poll_minutes`
- Acceptance: Test: a stand-in item starts its workflow once through its webhook, also when its event comes twice; a comment by someone else wakes the waiting run and the stack's own change does not; a comment while the run is busy answers its next wait at once; an ended run is not started again unless the tracker says on_comment; an event n8n could not take starts its run on the next pass; the poller is a crew job on n8n's minute that asks every poll_minutes. Shown on a throwaway n8n 2.44.7 on 10 Nov 2026: POST webhook/item/toy/bug started the run and answered its execution.
- Trace: `ops/runreg.py`, `ops/domain/trackers.py`, `ops/domain/forgework.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/systemd/tracker-poll.service`, `n8n/workflows/crew-tracker-poll.json`

### WFL-20

**A run waits on its tracker item** · should · functional · **implemented**

The step kind wait_item shall register the run's step on its tracker item with the step service and answer done with outcome reply and the new comments from others, or with outcome timeout once its timeout_hours have passed (0 waits without end); the AI Stack step node shall treat an answer with an outcome as a result, so the workflow branches on it, while a failed, refused or cancelled answer still fails the node.

- Acceptance: Test: a wait that times out answers done with outcome timeout and the run reminds in shadow and waits again; the node sends the wait's fields and fails only a failed, refused or cancelled answer. ### WFL-21

**A run writes to its tracker item** · should · functional · **implemented**

The step kind tracker shall make one write (comment, summary, attach, link, transition or label) to its item through trackers.write with the step's own write id, and answer with the shadow reference while that kind of write is in shadow and with the live reference once the owner let it go live. A write that names an earlier step of its run (from_step) shall add that step's whole answer from the run register to its text, so n8n never needs to carry the text the stack writes, and shall be refused when the run has no answered step of that name.

### WFL-22

**n8n keeps summaries in a customer deployment** · should · constraint · **implemented**

In a deployment whose data class is customer (ops/dataguard.py), the step service shall answer n8n with the status, the outcome, the ids and references (task, step, build, pull request, artifacts, tracker writes) and a summary of at most 300 characters, while the whole answer stays in the run register and the task's record; the deployment may relax it with n8n_answers full in its data.yaml.

### WFL-23

**The run register is the stack's record of a run** · should · functional · **implemented**

The step service shall keep a run register, persisted on disk and surviving restarts of the runner and of n8n, holding the map from each tracker item to its runs and the wait registered on it, the ledger of routed events, and every step's request, whole answer, files (artifact ids) and times by its n8n run; a run shall export as one zip from the register (each step in order, its task's record, artifact ids and the files' hashes, the tracker writes with their shadow or live references, the pull request), with only the workflow's shape taken from the workflow file in git or the pack's cache, never from n8n's internal API. n8n holds time and order only.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-54](#dec-54), `docs/N8N-MIGRATION.md`, `docs/AI-STACK.md`

- Acceptance: Test: every step is kept with its request, answer, files and times, once across a restart; a stand-in run (agent, tracker writes, a wait that timed out, a command's file, a draft pull request, the summary) exports as one zip with its steps in order, its task's record, its files by hash, its tracker writes in shadow, its pull request and its workflow's shape from the toy pack, and nothing from n8n's internal API.
